# RoarMe · instructions for Claude Code

RoarMe is a public chat app (working name), separate from the THEMADLIONS hub (repository
alexisdigipro-hub/themadlions-projects). Owner: Alex Konstantinidis, film director, not a developer.
Talk to him in Greek, friendly and concrete, no em dashes; the app's own text is English.

- React + Vite, plain CSS in src/styles.css, no UI framework. Dark glass look like the hub's chat:
  round glass buttons, pill fields, soft rounded tiles, the chosen item as lighter glass, orange accent.
- **Never a word with an underline as a button** (Alex, 5 Nov, for every site we make): actions are real buttons, a flat matte glass pill or a round glass icon.
- Data in Supabase: only the `roar_*` tables and the `roar-files` / `roar-avatars` buckets; never touch the
  hub's tables. Every database change goes in supabase/schema.sql, written so it can be run again.
- **How we work** (Alex, 14 Nov, after changes he had not asked for: "how will we understand each other, you do your own freestyle"):
  1. Before writing code, say in one or two plain sentences exactly what will change and where, and start only after he says yes.
  2. Only what he asked: no extra buttons, bars or ideas. An idea of your own goes in the reply as a question, never into the app.
  3. Change what already exists: "this button" means that button, never a new one beside it.
  4. When unsure what he means, ask first; never guess.
- **A sample first, then the app** (Alex, 14 Nov: "can you make me the sample here without changing the app", and he chose the live test page): for any change to how something looks, first publish a live test page (an Artifact) built from the app's real stylesheets (src/styles.css, src/themes-halo.css, src/themes-glass.css) with the real classes, light and dark, at phone width, where he can tap and see the motion, with 2 or 3 versions side by side when there is a choice. Touch the app only after he says to put it in ("βάλ' το"). Say plainly that the page is a sample and not the app.
- Desktop first (Alex, 15 Oct). Email + password sign-in, @username to be found.
- Pushing to main deploys to GitHub Pages; open a PR, wait for the green build, merge it yourself.
- Since 1 Nov 2026 RoarMe has no tie to the THEMADLIONS hub (the hub has its own chat); supabase/hub-unlink.sql removed
  the pieces (links, project-group guards, hub sign-in, roar_hub_* functions). Accounts and chats stay.
- Slack features (20 Oct): @all / @here / @role mentions (src/roles.jsx, supabase/mentions.sql: roar_roles, roar_mentioned,
  roar_my_mentions, roar_push_targets now with `mention`) reach people even in a muted chat; Remind Me on a message
  (src/reminders.jsx, supabase/reminders.sql + pg_cron 'roar-reminders', roar-push {reminder}); Tasks > Reminders.
- Quote (31 Oct, Alex from Telegram): words picked from a message ride at the top of the reply as "> " lines (src/format.jsx withQuote / splitQuote, read only on a reply); a computer selects them and gets the sel-bar (Copy, Quote), a phone uses the menu's Quote (QuotePick in src/Chat.jsx). No database change.
- One theme (Alex, 12 Nov: Simba, Velvet and Mango removed, then "delete Air, Stone and Halo"): Glass, in dark and light. It is built in three layers that all stay: styles.css (Stone's build, data-theme 'stone'), src/themes-halo.css (data-skin 'halo': the palette, bubbles, menus, windows, motion) and src/themes-glass.css (data-glass: the liquid glass on top). All three come with the app (main.jsx imports them in that order; index.html and prefs.js dressTheme put the three attributes on <html>); there is no theme picker. prefs.js getPrefs moves everyone to theme 'glass' (Halo's own look carries over; a colour or bubbles picked for Stone or Air are let go); haloish() is always true and wearTheme does nothing, both kept for the code that calls them. Where these notes say "Halo" or "Stone" they now mean those layers of Glass. styles.css still holds old rules for data-theme 'ember' and the mango / velvet / air skins; they match nothing and can be pruned. The Tasks panel (tasks.jsx, with calendar.jsx) is lazy; what the start needs of Tasks is src/taskCore.js.
- Tasks is off by default (Alex, 7 Nov): Settings > Features > Tasks (prefs.tasksTab, as callsTab) puts it in the bar at the foot and the phone's chat head; whoever already had tasks gets it on once (Chat.jsx).
- Settings menu (Alex, 12 Nov, tidied again: "not so many menus"): six doors in three boxes: 1 General · Appearance · Notifications and Sounds; 2 Chats · AllMedia; 4 Privacy and Security (SettingsPages.jsx PAGES, the last field is the group; TUCKED are pages without a door, SUBS the line under a door). General's page also shows Features and Language; Chats' page has doors to Chat Folders and Auto-Reply and shows Data and Storage; Privacy and Security also shows Active Sessions. The pages features / language / folders / autoreply / data / sessions still exist for links from elsewhere. RoarTransfer's own list is in AllMedia > Transfers > Sent by Me (media.jsx). Features holds what can be added or taken away (Calls tab with the call devices, Tasks); a new optional part goes there. Power Saving is in Appearance > Effects.
- Own chat wallpaper (Alex, 8 Nov): besides the built-ins, any colour ('color:#rrggbb') or one's own photo ('img:<url>|<opacity>', uploaded to roar-avatars/<id>/wall-*), in Settings > Appearance > Chat wallpaper (prefs.background) and per chat from ⋯ > Change Wallpaper (localStorage roarme-walls). src/walls.js ownColor / ownPhoto / photoValue / wallLook; extras.jsx WallTiles has the Colour and Your Photo tiles and the Photo Strength slider; CSS at the end of styles.css.
- Nothing of Roardino in RoarMe (Alex, 8 Nov): no Ordino (compose button, attach tile, Roardino window, ordino card; old ordino messages show as their text) and no ?embed=roardino mode (src/embed.js, the html.in-roardino look and the roardino:* messages are gone). Roardino's own table roardino_ordinos stays (it is Roardino's).
- Admin moderation (Alex, 8 Nov; supabase/admin-moderation.sql): an admin's own edits carry no "edited" (Chat.jsx amAdmin); roar_admin_delete_message deletes anyone's message for everyone (message menu: Delete for Everyone (Admin); RoarMe Inbox); roar_official_edit corrects only the official RoarMe account's text messages, quietly (RoarMe Inbox). Nobody can edit another person's words. Careful: roar_admin_delete (u uuid) is a different, older function that deletes a whole account.
- Blur (Alex, 9 Nov: "the heaviest thing, make it much lighter everywhere"): one knob, --blur (10px, phone 6px, no saturate) for every glass surface, and --blur-veil (4px, phone 3px) for full-screen veils (scrim, modal back, viewer, call, chat menu). No hard-coded blur(...) in backdrop-filter; no filter: blur in animations.
- Motion (Alex, 9 Nov: "they go and come back, choppy"): --spring is a smooth ease with no overshoot; the overshoot is only --pop (reactions, big emoji) and the long-press pop. Lists, pages and menus come in as one piece (no row-after-row stagger); one opening animation per thing; entrances short (.22 to .32 s) and small (scale .95 to .98, 6 to 14 px).
- Chat theme (Alex, 9 Nov; Settings > Appearance > Chat theme, SettingsPages.jsx ChatTheme): ready-made CHAT_THEMES (prefs.js: wallpaper + myBubble + theirBubble + accentHex) and one's own colours through Edit Your Theme (src/themeEditor.jsx: a sheet with the chat preview on top, Background / Accent / Messages (yours, theirs), its own colour field, #hex, Cancel / Set) plus Message Corners (bubbleR). applyLook sets --my-bubble / --their-bubble (+ -ink, chosen by inkOn) and --bubble-r with data-my-bubble / data-their-bubble / data-bubble-r; CSS at the end of styles.css, over every theme. Reset Appearance clears them.
- Folder tabs at the foot (Alex, 9 Nov; Halo since 12 Nov has a plain bar of words instead, themes-halo.css, wheelOn off for it; there the folder bar and the rail float over the list, which runs on under them by negative margins, --hfmb / --hpb; the same top bar on Contacts holds the search (.contacts-top, sticky) and on Calls the words CREATE NEW CALL (.new-call-bar)): no bar; a horizontal wheel that goes round (Chat.jsx wheeling / lightMid / toMiddleCopy / onWheelScroll; WHEEL_COPIES copies of the folders, the middle one real, a settled wheel jumps back to it unseen). The chosen pill never moves: .wheel-pill, its own lens in .wheel-box, stands in the middle and only takes the width of the word over it; a slide picks the folder that snaps to the middle (with a tick), both edges fade.
- Halo chat head and profile (Alex, 12 Nov): back, the name pill filling the row (weight 600: Safari blurs 650), the round picture; the pill and the picture open the profile, which in Halo is Telegram's (Profile.jsx pf-head / pf-hero / pf-back / pf-edit, call · video · mute · search · more; its Search reaches the chat through the window event roarme-find, and its More menu (wallpaper, Encrypted Chat, Create Group, Schedule Call, Export, Auto-Delete, Clear; nothing that Edit or the buttons beside it already do) through roarme-roomact, which Chat.jsx answers from roomActs).
- Voice and video messages as Telegram's (Alex, 12 Nov): a tap on the microphone swaps it with the camera (prefs local roarme-rec-mode); holding records (Chat.jsx holdStart: left 110px throws away, up 80px locks), voice as before, video a round message drawn square through a canvas (startVideo / flipCam / stopVideo, VideoRec overlay, at most a minute), sent as kind 'file' with file.round and dur and shown by extras.jsx NoteTile. Under 0.7 s nothing is sent.
- Halo's colours (Alex, 12 Nov, from a palette): Settings > Appearance > Accent Colour in Halo offers its orange and five of its own (prefs.js HALO_ACCENTS: navy, blue, lilac, rose, pink, each with a light form for the dark, a gradient and an ink); applyLook sets --halo-l / --halo-d / --halo-2 / --halo-mine(-top) / --halo-ink and data-halo-accent, themes-halo.css turns them into --halo. Halo's orange things use var(--halo) / --halo-mine, never a hard-coded orange.
- Halo's two foot bars alive (Alex, 12 Nov): the lens glides on a soft spring, the chosen sign pops, a pressed tab dips; a finger held on a bar swells the lens into glass with a rainbow rim that follows it and picks the tab under it on release (ui.jsx useLiquid, Chat.jsx on footLens and tabsLens; a bar that scrolls sideways keeps its scroll). Off with Power Saving and reduced motion.
- Halo in jelly (Alex, 12 Nov: "all the animations more jelly, many, in many places"): Halo alone leaves the calm Motion rule of 9 Nov. Last block of themes-halo.css: --spring / --pop overshoot there, jl-* keyframes for what comes in (messages, menus, windows, sheets, pages, the chat's head), a wobble for what is chosen, a squash for what is pressed. Off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion; the other themes stay calm.
- Halo's motion tuned (Alex, 12 Nov: "more immediate and smoother, a little choppy"): in the jelly block only transform and opacity move, a press answers in 70 ms, entrances take .22 to .34 s with one small overshoot, nothing that mounts all the time is animated, and what pops or slides has no backdrop blur (sheet, message menu, toast). A deleted message dissolves and the gap closes (Chat.jsx vanish, styles.css .msg-out / msg-dust), in every theme.
- Lighter Halo, second pass (Alex, 12 Nov): full-screen veils do not fade in (a blurred layer whose opacity moves is blurred every frame), the foot bars have no backdrop blur, and a message's bubble carries what it is as classes (Chat.jsx kindCls: rt-b, aud-b, file-b, solo; with pic, loc-b, art-b, event-b, note-b) so themes style messages without :has(). Keep new message rules on those classes.
- Halo's pop-ups are two families, the same everywhere (Alex, 12 Nov; last block of themes-halo.css): menus (.menu, .menu-list, .rx-pick, .emoji-pick, .mention-pick, .toast, .vw-menu) are white frosted glass at 80% with dark words, 24px corners (dark grey glass in dark mode; Alex first had them dark with grain, then chose white); windows and sheets (.modal, .as-sheet, .te-sheet) are light frosted glass at 88% with a bright rim, 30px corners, solid when they fill a phone's screen. A new menu or window takes one of the two; do not give it a look of its own.
- Photo editor before sending (Alex, 12 Nov, after Telegram's): in the attach sheet a tap on a picked photo (or its pencil) opens src/photoEdit.jsx: Turn, Crop to a shape (Square, 4:5, 16:9, centred), Draw (six inks, Undo), Adjust (Light, Contrast, Colour). All on a canvas on the device; Done replaces the picked file with a JPEG. The quality switch reads SD / HD.
- Sliding to get about on a phone (Alex, 12 Nov: "every window slides right and left, wherever it can"): pages in .main already go back with a slide to the right (Chat.jsx, presses the page's [data-back]); every window (ui.jsx Modal, useSwipeClose) follows a slide to the right and closes; the attach sheet is pulled down by its top ([data-grab]) and slides between Gallery, File and Audio; the profile's tabs and AllMedia's tabs change with a slide (useTabSwipe; data-tabs-swipe 'mid' keeps the close / back slide off while not on the first tab). Things that move sideways themselves (fields, sliders, maps, canvases, rows of tabs, sideways scrollers) are left alone.
- Glass (Alex, 12 Nov: "take Halo and make a theme all in liquid glass, the menu thick with words but our icons, the folders at the top"): a fourth theme, 'glass', Halo's build with data-glass on <html> (prefs.js dressTheme; wearTheme loads themes-halo.css and then src/themes-glass.css, whose rules start html:root:root[data-glass] so they win over Halo's). prefs.js haloish(t) is true for Halo and Glass: ask it wherever the code asked for Halo (accents, wallpapers, app background, the preview's bars). Its own: the foot menu a tall glass capsule with icon over word, the folders always at the top (Chat.jsx tabsDown is false for it, whatever Chat Folders > Tabs View says), on Calls CREATE NEW CALL up in the folders' place (order in .side), on Contacts the search sticky at the top, nothing on Settings. First pass: bars, round buttons, cards' glass; build the rest out in themes-glass.css. It replaced the Glass mode and the Menu Shape setting of the same day.
- Halo's page wears the chats' wallpaper (Alex, 12 Nov: "the overall background follows the one chosen for the chats"): App.jsx gives the body class app-wall and what a chat wears (data-bg, data-img, data-own, data-wop and the --wall vars, from walls.js wallLook of prefs.appBackground, or of prefs.background while that is '': Settings > Appearance > App background has Same as Chats and, with it off, its own tiles and, since 13 Nov, a strength of its own, prefs.appWallStrength, null = the chats' wallStrength; a chat is same-wall only when the two strengths agree); themes-halo.css draws it (the wallpaper washes also name body.app-wall; last block for colour, photo and strength). A chat on that same wallpaper is .room-view.same-wall and lays nothing of its own; a chat with its own wallpaper keeps it. Plain stays Halo's lilac clouds. Other themes are untouched.
- Before every push (12 Nov, after Settings took the whole app down: a deleted name, THEMES, was still used, and neither the build nor a syntax check sees that): run eslint's no-undef over src (the global eslint in /opt/node22 with a flat config that only sets browser globals and 'no-undef': 'error') and push only when it names nothing new. When something is deleted, grep the whole of src for its name, not just for the pattern being removed.
- GLASS TRAVEL (Alex, 12 Nov: "keep this animation in memory, we will need it"; it began as "the animation the bar at the foot of the Claude app has", with its RGB split): the app's signature motion, by this name. A lens moving between choices goes in two beats: .lens-go, 150 ms on cubic-bezier(.3, .7, .4, 1): it lifts into clearer glass (scale about 1.03 by 1.16), stretches from the old choice across to the new one, and the colours split at its rim (thin shadows: red left, blue right, a little green above, yellow below); then .lens-land, 300 ms on cubic-bezier(.25, 1.45, .45, 1): it draws in onto the new choice and settles, the split fading; classes off at 470 ms. Only transform, width and box-shadow move. ui.jsx useLens does it for any bar with [data-travel] (the menu at the foot, the folders' bar, every Lens); the look is in themes-glass.css (.foot.lens.lens-go and the GLASS TRAVEL block). Off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion (calm()). GLASS PRESS is its small sister with the same response (in at once, out on the same spring): the split colours at the rim of what a finger is on (round buttons, Send, the name's pill, sheet and profile tabs, a switch's knob that stretches). Since the same day it is also on everything a long press works on: a message, photo or round video and a chat's row (.pressing from Chat.jsx), a folder's tab, the record button, a contact's row. Three more of the family, same response (themes-glass.css, last block): GLASS MORPH (gl-morph: what opens grows out of the pressed place as a round drop with the split rim: .menu, .msg-menu, .emoji-pick, .mention-pick, the attach sheet), GLASS MERGE (gl-bud: Send buds out of the writing box and the microphone comes back the same way) and GLASS DROP (gl-drop / gl-rise / gl-bead / gl-toast: an arriving message, the unread counts, a notice). Names kept for later, not built: Glass Shine, Tilt, Glow, Lift, Ripple, Scroll, Pull. When Alex asks for "Glass Travel" or "the same response" somewhere, use these timings and curves, not new ones.
- Contacts in glass (Alex, 12 Nov, picked from nine looks shown on a test page: "do the third"): each contact a pill of glass with @username and last seen under the name and a green dot on who is here now; the favourites (localStorage roarme-fav) in a row on top that slides sideways (Chat.jsx .contacts-favs, hidden while searching); no lines between rows and no star in the row. Slimmer since 13 Nov ("a little thinner in height"): a 40px picture and 5px around it, about 50px a pill, 3px between pills. Settings > Appearance > Chat List > Contacts > Shape (prefs.contactShape, 13 Nov): Capsule, or Card (data-contacts='card' on <html>: 24px corners and more room, like a row of Settings), with a preview of two people drawn by the same rules. A third shape for both the contacts and the chat list, Inset (13 Nov: "a version where the glass goes inwards"): no rim or drop shadow, a shade inside the top edge and light inside the bottom, the piece sunk into the page. The contacts also have Plain ("with nothing, as the calls are"): no frame, the calls' fine line between people. Plain is how RoarMe starts and what Reset Appearance goes back to (DEFAULTS.contactShape, Alex, 13 Nov). A picture made of initials has a pair of colours of its own, the same for the same name, with the grain, wherever an Avatar stands (ui.jsx Avatar: .av-init, --ia / --ib from INIT_PAIRS; themes-glass.css). The other looks he saw and may ask for by letter: B the same with call / message buttons in the row, D plain list with letters of the alphabet, E a grid of three, Z one glass panel, H two columns, Θ who is online first, I large cards with Call / Video / Message.
- Glass on a screen of ordinary sharpness (Alex's desktop PC with a 2K monitor, 12 Nov: "a little pixelated, the phone is finer and more shiny"): last block of themes-glass.css, @media (max-resolution: 1.5dppx): a finer, fainter --grain, thinner and softer split-colour rims on Glass Travel and Glass Press, and --blur at 6px as on the phone. Details one pixel wide are made on an iPhone (3 dots a pixel); anything new of that kind (grain, hairlines, rims) gets its softer form there too.
- A chat slid sideways in the list (the buttons were there since 21 Oct, from the screen's edges only; Alex, 12 Nov, from iOS's swipe and "both, with a limit"): on a phone a sideways slide that starts anywhere on a chat opens its buttons (Chat.jsx pager: lock 'row', rowW(side): Unread · Pin on the left, Mute · Delete · Archive on the right); past ROW_FAR (0.52 of the list's width) or on a quick flick the chat goes back and the folder turns as before (or goStop at the last folder); the chat dims past that point. A slide that starts on no chat still drags the folders. Look in themes-glass.css: the chat a grey capsule (.room.slid), each button a capsule of colour with the grain and its word under it, growing with the finger, landing on Glass Travel's spring, Glass Press under a finger.
- The chat list as one panel of glass (Alex, 12 Nov, picked from six looks on a test page: "the third"): the chats stand in one sheet of glass with fine lines, each with its last message, the time (Chat.jsx room-top small, listTime) and the unread count. themes-glass.css draws the panel on the rows (.rooms-pager .rooms > .room: first rounds the top, last the bottom), so it is only as tall as the chats. Show Last Message is on for everyone once (prefs.js prevOn; DEFAULTS.listPreview true); it can still be turned off in Settings. Since 13 Nov it is off when RoarMe starts and after Reset Appearance (DEFAULTS.listPanel false: the chats straight on the background, Alex's choice from his own screen); it can be put on and its opacity set: Settings > Appearance > Chat List (prefs.listPanel -> data-nopanel on <html>, which every panel rule waits for with :not([data-nopanel]); prefs.listPanelOp -> --lp). The same box has Panel Colour (prefs.listPanelFill: plain glass, the orange or one of HALO_ACCENTS; applyLook sets --lpa / --lpb at the panel's opacity, data-panel-fill, and from 60% up the pair's ink as --lp-ink with data-panel-ink) and Panel Texture (listPanelGrain -> data-panel-grain); the colour runs left to right so each row's piece matches. The box opens with a preview (Alex, 13 Nov; SettingsPages.jsx .set-preview.lp-prev): three rows with the real rows' classes inside .rooms-pager > .rooms on the app's background, so the panel's own rules draw it; a new panel setting needs nothing more to show there. Shape (prefs.listShape, 13 Nov: "with different cards, not only the panel"): One Panel, Capsules or Cards (data-rooms='pill' / 'card' on <html>; the panel's rules stay on and each row only gets its own rim and corners, so opacity, colour, texture and ink keep working). The other looks, by letter: B each chat a pill, D the pinned in a row of round pictures on top, E large cards with two lines, Z unread first in bright glass.
- App Text Size and more fonts (Alex, 13 Nov): Settings > Appearance > Text has App Text Size (prefs.uiText, 85 to 130 %, applied when the slider is let go: ui.jsx Slide onDone) above Message Text Size (textSize, the messages only) and Font. applyLook: on a phone (pointer: coarse) it sets text-size-adjust on <html>, so only the letters grow; on a computer, where browsers ignore that, it sets zoom on <html>, which scales the whole page. Fonts added, all with Greek: Roboto, Open Sans, Fira Sans, IBM Plex Sans, Ubuntu, Commissioner, Comfortaa, Noto Serif (prefs.js FONTS + the Google Fonts link in index.html, both lines). DM Sans and Space Grotesk have no Greek; a new font should have it, since Alex writes Greek.
- The menu's signs move as what they are (Alex, 13 Nov): on being chosen the handset rings and its two waves go out (ui.jsx FOOT_I.calls is now a handset with waves: paths .fi-hand / .fi-wave), the speech bubble hops, the gear turns, the person nods (Chat.jsx gives the buttons foot-contacts / foot-calls / foot-settings; keyframes gi-* in themes-glass.css, once, about half a second, transform and opacity only, off with Power Saving / Motion off / reduced motion). A new tab in the menu gets a movement of its own kind.
- Appearance in groups, each with its own preview where the hand is (Alex, 13 Nov: "group everything as it should be, and whatever I change I see a preview at the place I change it"; the one preview stuck to the top, 12 Nov, is gone). SettingsPages.jsx Appearance, headings .set-group: Colours (colourPv: one message, Send and the menu; Day and Night, Accent Colour, Accent Opacity, Glass Look), Messages (chatPv; Chat theme, Text and spacing, Chat wallpaper), Chat List (listPv; Panel, Spacing), The Whole App (listPv with the menu; App background, App Text Size, Font), More (Effects, Your name in groups with the name in its colour, Reset). A new look setting goes into the group whose preview shows it; if none does, the preview grows.
- Wallpapers as drops of glass (Alex, 13 Nov: "this way of showing them is Telegram's, make one of our own"): in .walls each wallpaper is a round lens with a bright rim and a shine, its name under it, the chosen one inside a ring of the accent; no little chat in the tile (the .wall-b bubbles are still in the markup, hidden). Last block of themes-glass.css; wherever WallTiles stands. Do not bring back tiles with sample bubbles.
- Recent calls set straight (Alex, 13 Nov: the bin sat on the date): in Glass a call's row is the row and, beside it, a round glass bin of its own (.call-row is a flex row; .call-del is never laid over the row), and Clear all is a glass pill. Last block of themes-glass.css.
- How RoarMe starts and what Reset Appearance goes back to (Alex, 13 Nov, each from his own screen): chat wallpaper Plain (DEFAULTS.background 'soft'; Orange was the start for one day), no panel behind the chats, contacts Plain. A saved choice is kept; only DEFAULTS change.
- Report (Alex, 14 Nov, the first step toward the App Store, which asks it of any app where people write to each other): a message's menu > Report, a contact's card and Edit Contact > Report. src/report.jsx: report({ message, person }) asks for the window from anywhere (window event roarme-report), ReportHost in Chat.jsx shows it (six reasons, a note, Also block). supabase/reports.sql: roar_reports is reached only through roar_report (a member of the chat, 30 a day, the message's words kept as they were, none for an Encrypted Chat message), roar_admin_reports and roar_admin_report_done; Settings > Admin > Reports lists them with Delete Message, Block Account and Done. The Terms say reports are looked at within 24 hours: keep that promise true. Inside the iPhone app (push.js inApp(): window.Capacitor on a native platform) nothing speaks of the Home Screen or of downloads for a computer: isStandalone() is true there and Settings > General has no RoarMe app box; anything new about installing asks inApp() first. The iPhone app (step 3, 14 Nov; Alex enrolled as an Individual): mobile/ holds only Capacitor's settings (bundle id com.themadlions.roarme, which can never change once on the App Store; package.json version = the App Store version); .github/workflows/ios.yml builds the website, makes the Xcode project fresh (cap add ios), writes the permission texts and the icon, and builds it unsigned for the simulator on any pull request that touches mobile/ (the TestFlight upload comes with the App Store Connect key in the repository's secrets). npm cannot install here, so the shell is proved only by that workflow's log. lib.js appUrl() gives the website's address inside the app (SITE_URL), since capacitor://localhost cannot go into an email. App Store order agreed with Alex: 1 Report, 2 iOS clean-up, 3 the iOS shell (Capacitor, built on GitHub), 4 Apple's notifications and call permissions, 5 TestFlight, 6 the listing.
- RoarMe for iPhone (Alex, 14 Nov; App Store step 3): a Capacitor shell around the built site. mobile/ holds only Capacitor's settings (bundle id com.themadlions.roarme, name RoarMe, version in mobile/package.json); the Xcode project is never kept, .github/workflows/ios.yml makes it fresh each run (cap add ios, permission texts, icon as a 1024px picture with no alpha, CFBundleVersion = the run's number). On a pull request touching mobile/ or the workflow, job `check` builds it unsigned for the simulator (first green 14 Nov, Xcode 26.6, Capacitor 7). Run workflow > testflight archives for a real iPhone with automatic signing through an App Store Connect API key (Admin role, -allowProvisioningUpdates) and exports with destination upload straight to App Store Connect. It reads the repository secrets ASC_KEY_ID, ASC_ISSUER_ID, ASC_KEY_P8 (the .p8 file's text) and APPLE_TEAM_ID, which Alex puts in himself; never ask for them in the chat. Inside the app lib.js appUrl() gives the website's address for links in emails.
- Apple's notifications in the iPhone app (Alex, 14 Nov; App Store step 4): @capacitor/push-notifications in mobile/ (ios.yml writes App/App.entitlements with aps-environment, sets CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS and adds the two AppDelegate methods that hand the token to Capacitor). push.js native(): the plugin from the shell, or reached through the native bridge (bridgePlugin: nativePromise / nativeCallback), since the site does not carry @capacitor/core. The token is kept in roar_push_subs as https://api.push.apple.com/3/device/<token> (p256dh and auth 'apns'), so who is told is decided exactly as for Web Push; roar-push sends those to APNs (ES256 token reused 45 minutes, topic com.themadlions.roarme, thread-id = the chat, collapse id = the tag; BadDeviceToken counts as gone). Its key: the Edge Function secrets APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID, APNS_KEY_P8 (an APNs key from developer.apple.com > Keys), set by Alex; without them those devices get 503 and Web Push is untouched. A tapped notification opens its chat (push.js onNotificationTap). No badge on the icon yet (iOS keeps it until the app clears it natively). Camera and microphone for calls are getUserMedia inside WKWebView with the Info.plist texts; seen working nowhere yet.
- The first time (Alex, 14 Nov, from Wire's screen): Auth.jsx Onboarding is two steps with no card, a big light title in the middle, one field each with the round arrow inside it (.ob-step, .ob-field, .ob-go, .ob-hint at the end of styles.css): Your username (checked as you type against roar_people: Free / taken), then Your name (may stay empty). Sign out and Back are glass pills.
- Signing in and finding friends (Alex, 14 Nov, before the App Store; Wire's way): Auth.jsx EmailCode is the first way in: the email, then the 6-digit code from the email (signInWithOtp with shouldCreateUser / verifyOtp type email; the same steps sign in and sign up; the code is {{ .Token }} in Supabase's Magic Link and Confirm signup templates, which Alex set; the link in the email works too). Sign in with Password opens the old form (sign up with a password, forgotten password), which has Sign in with a Code Instead. PhoneSignIn (number, then the SMS code) comes first only while Supabase's phone sign-in is on (Twilio, for later; usePhoneAuth reads /auth/v1/settings external.phone). src/phone.jsx: COUNTRIES (Greece first), toE164, contactDigits (an address-book number as auth.users keeps it: digits, country code first, the national 0 dropped except Italy), sha256hex, PhoneField, friendsFromContacts + PhoneFriends (Contacts > Find Friends From Your Contacts, only inside the iPhone app: @capacitor-community/contacts in mobile/, NSContactsUsageDescription in ios.yml; matched by the contacts' emails and numbers, invite by SMS for the rest who have a number). Settings > Privacy and Security: Phone Number (add or change with updateUser + verifyOtp type phone_change, shown while phone sign-in is on or to whoever has a number) and Let People Find Me (always). supabase/contacts-find.sql: roar_contact_index (only SHA-256s of the confirmed email in small letters and of the number's digits, kept by the trigger roar_contact_sync on auth.users; nobody reads it), roar_contact_findable, roar_find_contacts (5,000 hashes a call, 15,000 a day, findable only, not blocked either way, not suspended), roar_findable, roar_set_findable. The contacts' emails and numbers never leave the phone, only their hashes.
- Frost words (Alex, 14 Nov: "in light the letters do not show, too white"): by Day every word is a warm dark brown (--fr-ink #3a2f26, the small ones --fr-ink-soft), menus light stone glass, the accent dark brown with light ink; by Night white words as before. In themes-frost.css words take var(--fr-ink), never a literal white.
- Frost (Alex, 14 Nov, from a picture of frosted glass buttons on a warm stone wall, first as test pages, then "make me such a theme; when it is on, none of the colours and settings we made, only day night, slab / capsule, one panel / cards, and the background, all in the same palette"): a second theme on top of Glass, chosen in Settings > Appearance > Theme (Roar · Frost; prefs.frost; the first is Glass, shown as Roar since 14 Nov, Alex: "name Glass Roar"). prefs.js applyLook builds the look from DEFAULTS plus mode, uiText and motion (so the chat list and the contacts stand straight on the wall, no panel, no card, contacts Plain: Alex, 14 Nov, "take the frames off the list and the contacts, and One Panel / Cards") and puts data-frost on <html>; one's own colours, wallpapers, chat theme and the rest stay saved and come back with Glass. App.jsx and Chat.jsx give the body and every chat data-bg 'frost' (every chat is same-wall). src/themes-frost.css (imported after themes-glass.css; every rule starts html:root:root:root:root[data-frost][data-glass][data-skin][data-theme] and its tokens carry !important to beat inline styles): the taupe wall (lighter by Day, near-black brown by Night), white ink, frosted glass with a bright rim, a light leaking along the foot menu's bottom edge, deep soft shadows down and right; counts, Send and the main buttons bright white glass with the warm dark ink #3f3329; messages flat since 14 Nov ("no outline on the bubbles, flatter": one even colour, no rim, no shadow), yours --halo-mine with that ink, theirs a darker white wash; menus, windows and sheets one frosted stone glass. Slab gives 14px corners to bars, fields and buttons, 10px to tabs and lenses, 13px to round buttons, 16px to the list, 22px to windows; Capsule keeps Glass's round shapes. While Frost is on, Appearance shows only Theme and Day and Night. Never put a pseudo-element inside :is() there: it voids the whole rule.
- Contacts' sort and add (Alex, 14 Nov, from Telegram's Contacts; "on the buttons themselves, not new ones"): on the Contacts tab the head's own ⋯ (head-edit) opens Sort (by Last Seen, by Name; a tick on the chosen; localStorage roarme-contacts-sort; favourites stay first) and the head's own + opens New Contact (the find-people window, modal 'newchat') and Invite Friends (modal 'invite'); on the other tabs both do what they did. The search bar holds only the search. The @username and last seen under each contact are fainter (.contacts-list .contact .room-prev opacity .62).
- Calls in the head (Alex, 14 Nov, from the iPhone's Phone app; each point confirmed with him first): on the Calls tab the head's middle holds All | Missed (a soft Lens, white glass with a grey chip) in place of ROARME; the head's own ⋯ turns editing on (Chat.jsx callsEdit; it shows ✓ while on, and leaving Calls ends it): a round glass bin beside every call deletes that call (calls.jsx .call-del, shown only while editing; Alex chose bins over red minuses) and the head's + becomes Delete All (callsClear -> RecentCalls clearTick -> clearAll with its confirm); off editing + opens New Call (modal 'newcall'). The bins beside the rows, Clear all and Create New Call at the foot are gone. Missed = incoming missed or cancelled, with its own empty line.
- Frost's pieces in Roar (Alex, 14 Nov: "all of Frost's colours in Roar, for anyone to make it"): the wallpaper 'stone' named Frost (prefs.js WALLS, supabase/frost-wall.sql adds its row to roar_wallpapers, themes-glass.css draws it by Day and Night), two accents in HALO_ACCENTS (h-stone Stone: light stone with the dark ink; h-umber Umber: dark brown with light ink) and a ready-made chat theme Frost in CHAT_THEMES (the stone wall, your messages #efe8e0, theirs #a99c8f, accent #5a4a3c). In Frost the attach sheet's bar has no colour (no fill, rim or shadow; only the chosen tab in a soft flat box; Alex, 14 Nov), and the Settings boxes and the profile card wear the bars' glass (--lg, --lg-rim, --lg-shadow), as Create New Call does.
- Menus of + and ⋯ as the message menu (Alex, 14 Nov, from two screenshots; last block of themes-glass.css): every .menu has the message menu's rows (34px, 13.5px plain weight, 10px to a 20px icon), its corners (24px) and its glass (white .93 by Day, grey .93 by Night). Frost keeps its own stone glass for menus.
- The lock in the writing box (Alex, 14 Nov: "a small discreet lock in the writing box, and take the emoji out"): Chat.jsx puts a small lock in the emoji button's place (.emoji-btn.seal-btn): open and faint while Encrypted Chat is off (LOCK_OPEN), closed and green while on (I.lock); a tap turns it on through askSeal's confirm, as the profile's More does, and off at once (turnOff). The emoji button and its picker are gone from the writing box: a phone has emoji on its keyboard, a computer its own (Mac Ctrl+Cmd+Space, Windows Win+.).
- Reactions as iMessage's (Alex, 14 Nov: "no outline, over the message, that small"): last block of themes-glass.css: each reaction under a message a small chip (22px, 13px emoji) with no rim or shadow, laid 9px over the bubble's bottom edge at its start (end for one's own), plain grey by Day and Night, one's own a little tinted with the accent.
- Onyx is the accent RoarMe starts with and Reset Appearance goes back to (Alex, 14 Nov; DEFAULTS.accent 'h-onyx'; '' is still the orange). Frost ignores it (its applyLook branch sets accent ''). A saved choice is kept.
- Messages flat (Alex, 14 Nov: "take the glass off, make it flat, and the least noise", after three glassier samples he turned down): last block of themes-glass.css: every bubble one even colour (theirs white .86 by Day, white .1 by Night; one's own the accent's two colours mixed to one, or one's own bubble colours), no gradient, no rim, no shadow, no blur behind; one's own carry only --grain-min (the grain at .06). Frost keeps its own. Do not bring back the gradient or the rim unless he asks.
- The heart reaction is our own drawing in the accent (Alex, 14 Nov): Chat.jsx RX_HEART stands in for ❤️ in the reactions under a message (fill var(--halo)); the reaction bar above the menu keeps the emoji. Stored as ❤️ as before.
- The notification's sound in the iPhone app (Alex, 14 Nov: "a strange sound, neither ours nor the iPhone's", then "each one's own"): Apple plays only files inside the app, so scripts/push-sounds.mjs draws each of prefs.js SOUNDS with chime()'s own notes and envelopes into mobile/sounds/<kind>.wav (run it again after a change to SOUNDS), ios.yml's step Notification sounds copies them into the app and adds them to its resources (the xcodeproj gem), push.js keeps the device's pick in roar_push_subs.sound (supabase/push-sound.sql; 'off' while Sound When a Message Arrives is off; syncPushSound on every change in Settings) and roar-push sends <kind>.wav (Glass when unknown, none for off; it reads the rows with select * so it works before the SQL is run).
- The chat's head shows only the name in a chat with one person (Alex, 14 Nov: "only the name, nothing else"): Chat.jsx sub is '' there (the @username and last seen are in the profile); groups keep "N members".
- The newest message is never left half under the writing box (Alex, 14 Nov, a voice message): Chat.jsx watches the chat's rows with a ResizeObserver and, while one is at the bottom (stick), follows a row that grows once its voice message, photo or video has loaded.
- The keyboard in the iPhone app (Alex, 14 Nov, TestFlight): main.jsx's noKeys test (the visible area not shrunk, so no keyboard) is skipped inside the app, where the page itself shrinks above the keyboard and the test took the html.kb class away, leaving a gap over the keyboard; and the iPhone's bar of arrows and Done over the keyboard is turned off (@capacitor/keyboard in mobile/, setAccessoryBarVisible false at start, through the native bridge).
- A folder tab's long-press menu stays open when the finger lifts (Alex, 14 Nov): Chat.jsx tabPress onTouchEnd restarts folderMenu.at, and the scrim's click and context menu wait 500 ms from it, so the phone's tap from that same press does not close the menu.
- A slid chat's buttons fit inside the row (Alex, 14 Nov: "they spill out, right and left"): last block of themes-glass.css: capsule 30px with a 16px sign, word 10px under it, both centred in the row, 6px / 12px of room so the glow is not cut.
- Sign-in is email and password again (Alex, 14 Nov evening: "make it as we had it"): Auth.jsx shows only the password form (Sign in, Create an account, Forgot password); EmailCode and PhoneSignIn are gone (the note above on Wire's way is history for sign-in; phone.jsx stays for Settings and Find Friends). The emails Supabase sends (Confirm signup, Reset password) go out from noreply@themadlions.com through Resend's SMTP, in RoarMe's own templates.
- Contacts' search results sit right under the search at the top (Alex, 14 Nov): the last block of themes-glass.css puts .contacts-list.searching back to flex-start; Halo's flex-end was for a search at the foot.
- Calls while editing, as the Phone app (Alex, 14 Nov evening, changing his earlier pick of bins: "the red minus left of the name, not the bin on the right"): calls.jsx puts a red round minus (.call-minus) before each call; a tap turns it a quarter and shows a red Delete pill (.call-del-go) at the row's right, which deletes; a tap on the row or leaving editing hides it. The round bins (.call-del) are gone. CSS at the end of themes-glass.css.
- A long press on a chat shows its last messages above the menu on a phone too (Alex, 14 Nov: "the menu we have, plus a preview of the messages in it"): Chat.jsx shows the ctx-card (head with the name, PeekList) on every screen; on a phone the card takes up to 44vh and the menu 42vh (styles.css). The menu is unchanged.
- The long press on a chat picks nothing when the finger lifts (Alex, 14 Nov): Chat.jsx pressEnd restarts roomMenu.at, the .ctx-stack swallows clicks for 450 ms from it (onClickCapture) and the backdrop waits 450 ms, so the phone's tap from that same press neither picks a menu row nor opens the preview's chat.
- Finding people (New group, new chat) also by the name I saved them under (Alex, 14 Nov): Chat.jsx usePeopleSearch asks roar_people (their own name, @username) and roar_contacts (my nickname, imatch with foldRx) together; my contacts come first, shown under my name for them.
- The sign-in screen is only the logo, the fields and the buttons (Alex, 14 Nov): no "Welcome back" and no line under it; the email field has no example in it. Create an account keeps its title; Forgot password keeps its title and one line.
- Contacts (Alex, 14 Nov): the search stays fixed at the top (.side-scroll.contacts has no rubber band, overscroll-behavior-y none); pulled down at the top only the people move (Chat.jsx contactsTouch / cMove on every child but .contacts-top) and the arrow under the search fills, let go when full and RoarMe reloads, as the chat list's pull does. Find Friends From Contacts is the third item of the Contacts head's + menu, only inside the iPhone app; the bar in the list (.btn.phone-find) is gone.
- The long-press preview card has the menu's glass (white .93 by Day, grey .93 by Night; last block of themes-glass.css), not see-through (Alex, 14 Nov).
- Every window sits whole above the keyboard on a phone (Alex, 14 Nov: New group was half under it): styles.css, last block: with html.kb the .modal-back takes the visible area (main.jsx --vvt / --vvh), 10px clear of the keyboard, and the .modal is at most that tall (it scrolls inside).
- New Call (Alex, 14 Nov, from Telegram's; step 1 of 3, group calls and call links not built yet): Chat.jsx NewCall, opened by the Calls head's +: a search on top (foldHas on name and @username), the people in letter order under small letter headings, the letters down the right side in the accent to jump (.nc-index), a call and a video call on each row. CSS at the end of themes-glass.css.
- The long-press preview's messages stand on that chat's own wallpaper (Alex, 14 Nov: "not white, the background the messages normally have"): Chat.jsx lookOf(room) (roarme-walls or prefs.background, wallLook) on a .room-view.ctx-wall around PeekList; the head with the name keeps the menu's glass.
- The long-press view on a phone is one size every time (Alex, 14 Nov): the preview card 38vh, the menu under it as tall as its rows (up to 42vh); a tap on the empty space beside the menu or the card closes it (the .ctx-stack closes on a click on itself, after the 450 ms guard).
- The long press on a chat opens in GLASS MORPH (Alex, 14 Nov): the preview card grows out of the pressed chat (gl-morph-card, .34s on cubic-bezier(.25, 1.35, .45, 1), split rim), the menu follows .05s later with gl-morph from its top; last block of themes-glass.css, off with Power Saving / Motion off / reduced motion.
- The profile's Mute shows a muted chat (Alex, 14 Nov: "a little red, not the same sign"): Profile.jsx .pf-a-mute.on with the crossed bell (I.bellOff) and the word Muted; a red-tinted circle and a small shake as it turns on (end of themes-glass.css). The "Muted" notice stays.
- The long-press menu on a chat has no Preview Messages any more (Alex, 14 Nov): the preview stands above it.
- The long-press view as Telegram's (Alex, 14 Nov, from its screenshot): on a phone the preview card is 48vh, the menu sits under it on the right (min(290px, 66%)), and the backdrop blurs everything behind (22px, his explicit ask, only on this view).
- Location is a tab of the attach sheet (Alex, 14 Nov: it popped a window of its own, the sheet was gone and he could not go back): share.jsx LocationPicker takes `inline` (no Modal, no close of its own) and extras.jsx AttachSheet shows it in its body when the Location tab is chosen, between File and Audio in the slide between tabs; Send sends from the sheet and closes it (Chat.jsx onLocation = sendCard). The old Location window still exists for other callers.
- The Location tab as Telegram's (Alex, 14 Nov, from its screenshot): AttachSheet adds .as-loc while it shows; the map fills the sheet edge to edge, the sheet's X and title lie over it, the search is a round button at the right (LocationPicker inline: findOpen) that opens the field under them, and the send row under the map reads Send My Current Location / Accurate to N metres (the fix's accuracy) or Send This Location with the address. Live location is not built.
- Haptics in the iPhone app (Alex, 14 Nov: "a small click wherever I press, and on a long press"): @capacitor/haptics in mobile/; lib.js nativeTap(style) reaches it (Plugins or the native bridge) and haptic() uses it first inside the app (LIGHT for a touch, MEDIUM over 10 ms, i.e. long presses); main.jsx gives a LIGHT tap on pointerdown of controls only (round buttons, Send, tabs, segments, switches, menu rows, the chat head's pill), never on list rows, which are touched to scroll. On the web nothing changes.
- Bold, italic, underline and strike in a folder's or a group's name (Alex, 15 Nov, angry that it was forgotten: "we said bold and underline when we write the title"): src/fancy.jsx useNameMarks(value, setValue) gives the field inputProps and a bar of four small round glass buttons (B, I, U, S) shown while the field is in use; a mark goes on the chosen letters, or the whole name when none are chosen, and a second press takes it off. The name stays plain text that goes everywhere (folder bar, list, notifications): bold and italic are Unicode's mathematical sans-serif letters (Greek too; accents kept), underline and strike are the combining lines U+0332 / U+0336. lib.js fold() now normalises with NFKD, so search reads them as plain letters. Used on Edit Folder's name (SettingsPages.jsx FolderEdit), New group's name (Chat.jsx NewGroup) and the group's name in its profile (Profile.jsx). CSS .name-marks at the end of styles.css.
- The chat's search as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov, from its screenshot): Chat.jsx find !== null puts .finding on the room view: the head, the pin bar and the writing box step aside (kept mounted, the draft stays); .findtop holds the search pill ("Search this chat", a chip of the chosen person) and a round X at the very top; .findfoot over the keyboard holds .find-tools (FIND_DAY: a hidden date input whose picker jumps to the first message of that day, goDay; FIND_WHO: a .menu of the members, findWho keeps only that person's messages, with or without words) and, once looking, .find-count "1 of N" with older / newer. CSS at the end of styles.css.
- Every pop-up menu made right (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's: "beautiful and tidy, not cramped, with room"; version A of the menus sample, https://claude.ai/artifact/VU1McFeS6GsnrXQk5zzzUX): last block of themes-glass.css over every .menu and .menu-list (the + and ⋯ menus, the folder and chat long-press menus, the message menu, the profile's More, which stays in the middle): 34px corners, 8px around, rows 46px (48 in the profile's More) with 16px words and 22px signs, a pressed row a soft capsule, separators inset 16px; clear glass (white .8 to .68 by Day, grey .76 to .7 by Night) with blur 18px, a bright rim, a sheen on top and a deep soft shadow. This replaces the 14 Nov "menus as the message menu" sizes (34px rows). Frost keeps its own stone glass.
- The profile's head folds as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov: "scrolling the files, the window grows so you see more"): Profile.jsx watches the profile's scroller; once the tabs reach the top, .pf-docked shows .pf-dock, a small sticky bar (back, the name, how many of the chosen tab, e.g. "1,262 files" from DOCK_WORDS, and search, which opens the chat's search), and .pf-tabs stay sticky under it, so the list fills the screen. CSS at the end of styles.css.
- Any member of a group adds people (Alex, 15 Nov, "9 B"): supabase/members-add.sql (roar_add_members asks roar_is_member, not roar_is_admin; schema.sql changed the same way); Profile.jsx shows Add people to every member. Removing people and Delete group stay with admins.
- Mute as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov, from its screenshot): the profile's Mute opens a menu (Profile.jsx pf-mute-menu, under the buttons in the middle): Unmute with "Muted until …" while muted, Mute for… (1 h, 8 h, 1 day, 7 days), Disable / Enable Sound (roar_members.silent: notifications come with no sound), Customize (roar_members.sound: one of prefs.js SOUNDS for this chat, played as it is picked), Mute Forever in red. supabase/mute-more.sql adds muted_until, silent, sound and redefines roar_push_targets so a mute whose muted_until has passed lets notifications through. Chat.jsx answers the window event roarme-mute (mute for hours, for ever, or off; also writes muted_until, as the chat list's Mute now does) and keeps chatPrefs for its own chime; roar-push reads each target's roar_members row (select *) and sends 'off' for silent or the chat's sound over the device's (a device's own 'off' wins); sw.js shows Web Push quietly when silent.
- Video chats in groups, as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov, "1 yes"): src/groupcall.jsx. supabase/group-calls.sql: roar_group_calls (room, started_by, video, started_at, ended_at; members read it, Realtime), roar_group_call_start(r, v) -> {id, fresh} (the one already on in that group, or a new one), roar_group_call_end(c). roar-livekit {groupCallId} gives any member a pass to room roar-gcall-<id>. Who is in it is Realtime presence on gcall-<id> (one shared channel per call: holdPresence / dropPresence, since two joins of one topic clash). Starting: the group profile's Call (camera off) or Video Chat > Start Video Chat, a scheduled call's Join in a group (EventCard joins the group's chat), each through the window event roarme-gcall {room, video, id?}, answered by GroupCallHost in Chat.jsx; a fresh one leaves a message with file.groupcall (GroupCallCard: Join while on, Ended after), which also notifies everyone (roar-push says "📹 started a video chat"). GroupCallBar over the chat ("Video Chat · N joined", faces, Join / Return). GroupCallScreen: a grid of tiles (camera or picture, a green ring while speaking, a crossed mic), Mute, Camera, Flip (phone), Screen (computer), Leave in red, End for All (who started it or an admin), the arrows make it a pill over the chats. Joiners come in with the camera off. The last one out ends it. Schedule Video Chat = the old ScheduleCall card.
- Call links and New Group Call (Alex, 15 Nov, "3 yes", from Telegram's New Call): Chat.jsx NewCall shows, above the people, New Group Call (tick people, a round toggle for video or voice, Call N people: roar_call_link_create makes a call of no group, roar_open_direct + a message with file.groupcall {id, video, link} into each one's chat with me, which notifies them, then I join) and Create Call Link (the same call, shown as <site>#call=<token> with Copy, Share, Join Now). Opening #call=<token> asks roar_call_by_link and joins (Chat.jsx hash handler). The card in a chat for such a call is LinkCallCard (roar_call_state every 30 s). roar-livekit gives a pass to anyone for a call with no group (only its uuid, from the link or the card, reaches it). All in supabase/group-calls.sql.
- Writing in full screen (Alex, 15 Nov, "5 yes", from Telegram's): the writing box's corner button (.compose-grow, shown once the words pass about three lines; not in Encrypted Chat) now opens Chat.jsx BigWriter instead of growing the box: the same text, the whole screen, X (back with the words kept), Undo / Redo (its own history), and at the foot a glass bar with B, I, U, S (toggleMark), a list (chosen lines start with "• ", Enter goes on with "• ", an empty one ends it), the clip and Send. Enter is a new line there. CSS .bw at the end of styles.css.
- The attach sheet as Telegram's, the rest (Alex, 15 Nov, "6 all"): File and Audio already had their recent lists; now Contact (a search and everyone in my chats; a tap sends file.contact {id, username, name, avatar}, shown by Chat.jsx SharedContact, whose View opens their card through the window event roarme-card), Article (share.jsx ArticleEditor inline, in the sheet's body) and Transfer (transfer.jsx TransferWindow slot: it portals into the sheet's .as-transfer, given by the ref callback transferSlot; when the sheet goes, an upload carries on hidden and an unstarted one closes) are tabs inside the sheet, none opens a window of its own. Tabs scroll sideways; Article and Transfer make the sheet tall (.as-tall). roar-push says "👤 <name>" for a contact.
- The phone step of the first time (Alex, 15 Nov, "4 A", as WhatsApp's): Auth.jsx Onboarding, after Your name, while Supabase's phone sign-in is on (usePhoneAuth; Twilio Verify, set by Alex in Authentication > Sign In / Providers > Phone): Your phone number ("you will find more of your friends, and they will find you"), the 6-digit SMS code (updateUser({ phone }) then verifyOtp type phone_change), Change Number, Not now. Sign-in stays email and password; the number feeds Find Friends From Your Contacts (supabase/contacts-find.sql).
- SMS code errors in plain words (Alex, 15 Nov): phone.jsx smsErr maps Twilio's and Supabase's messages (number taken, too many tries, bad number, wrong code, anything else: "Couldn't send the SMS right now. Try again later."), used by Settings > Phone Number and the first-time phone step.
- The keyboard in the iPhone app, second pass (Alex, 15 Nov: the writing box came up after the keyboard, and black showed at the keyboard's round corners): mobile/capacitor.config.json Keyboard resize none (the shell no longer shrinks the web view) and ios.scrollEnabled false; main.jsx listens to the Keyboard plugin's keyboardWillShow / keyboardWillHide (K.addListener or the bridge's C.addListener) and sets --kbh (and --vvh / --vvt for the windows that fit above the keyboard) with html.kb-native; styles.css html.in-shell #root takes --kbh off its height on the keyboard's curve (.28s), scrolls inside if it must, and the page stays whole under the keyboard. The visualViewport fit leaves --vvh alone while kb-native. Proved only on a phone (TestFlight).
- Friends From Your Contacts, lighter (Alex, 15 Nov, TestFlight: "a small card first, then the big one, it stutters, no animation"): phone.jsx friendsFromContacts hashes 200 at a time side by side with a breath between (was one by one); PhoneFriends opens at full size (.pfr) with pulsing ghost rows while it looks, fades the list in, keeps the answer ten minutes (lastFriends) so it reopens at once, and draws the invite list 40 rows at a time as it is scrolled; rows have no grain or glass and use content-visibility.
- Enter on a phone is a new line (Alex, 15 Nov: "the message goes only with the arrow"): prefs.enterSendsPhone (false by default) is used on a phone (push.js onPhone) instead of enterSends, which a computer keeps; Settings > Chats > Typing shows the phone's pair there; the writing box's enterKeyHint follows it.
- The writing box as it grows (Alex, 15 Nov): its corners stay 26px (themes-glass.css, last block; it was a 999px pill that went oval), and the corner button for full-screen writing shows from the third line (Chat.jsx pastTwo: two lines' height plus the padding).
- No more articles (Alex, 15 Nov: "take the articles out of the app altogether"): no Article tab in the attach sheet, no article editor or reader, Edit on an old article message edits it as text; old article messages (file.article) show as their plain text. share.jsx still holds ArticleEditor / ArticleReader / ArticleCard, unused. The attach sheet's tabs wear filled icons in the foot menu's hand (extras.jsx AS_I: gallery, file, location, audio, contact, transfer).
- The attach sheet's tab bar is the chat's foot menu (Alex, 15 Nov): extras.jsx gives .as-tabs useLens + data-travel (GLASS TRAVEL), themes-glass.css last block gives it the foot's glass (--lg, --lg-rim, --lg-shadow), 54px buttons with icon over a 10.5px word, the chosen one in --halo on the --lg-chip lens.
- Name marks on the iPhone (Alex, 15 Nov: "it sticks, bold does nothing"): fancy.jsx no longer marks on touchstart and again on the click that follows (it toggled twice); the press only keeps the focus, the click marks, and the bar hides 250 ms after the field lets go unless a button is being pressed. Name fields have autocorrect, autocomplete and spellcheck off (iOS's correction bubble sat under them).
- Underlined words as buttons, gone again (Alex, 15 Nov, angry: the group members' Make Admin / Remove): every button.link / a.link anywhere is drawn as a flat glass pill (styles.css, last block; red for .danger-link); the members' row has round glass icons: a shield with a star for Make Admin, lit (Remove Admin / Step Down) while they are one (.pf-row-adm), and a bin for Remove (.pf-row-del). Never add a text-only button again.
- While a profile menu is open (More, Mute, Video Chat), .pf-tabs and .pf-dock drop to z-index 1 and .pf-head rises to 50, so the held tabs no longer show through the menu (styles.css, end).
- The profile's Mute and Video Chat menus live inside .pf-acts and caught the round buttons' look: themes-glass.css (last block) gives their rows back the menu look and the veil a plain full-screen one.
- No GIFs tab in a profile (Alex, 15 Nov); its rendering is still there, unreachable.
- A profile shows only the tabs that have something in them (Alex, 15 Nov): Members always, all while loading (items null); if the chosen one is gone, the first that has something. The profile has no sideways guard at all (on .pf it cut the tabs, on .modal-body it clipped the dark head and hid the words under its round buttons); its rows keep inside on their own (min-width 0, ellipsis). The picture in the head has no white ring, only its shadow.
- A chat's sounds grouped (Alex, 15 Nov: "put Customize elsewhere, group them right"): the profile's Mute (bell) holds only muting (Unmute, Mute for…, Mute Forever); More > Notifications and Sounds opens a window (Profile.jsx soundsOpen, .ns) with Sound (With / Without Sound = roar_members.silent) and Tone (Default or one of SOUNDS, heard as it is picked = roar_members.sound; dimmed while without sound).
- The folded profile bar's backing (.pf-dock::before) reached 40px past each side even while hidden, so the profile scrolled sideways and the swipe back to the chat was taken as a scroll (Alex, 15 Nov); it now stops at the body's 16px padding.
- The profile's More menu is a little smaller than the other menus (Alex, 15 Nov): 300px wide, 40px rows, 15px words, 20px signs, 6px around (themes-glass.css, end).
- The profile's More has no Encrypted Chat (the lock in the writing box turns it on and off) and reads Export Chat History without the dots (Alex, 15 Nov).
- The profile's More in four groups (Alex, 15 Nov: "group them right"): this chat's settings (Notifications and Sounds, Change Wallpaper, Auto-Delete Messages, Remove Group Photo) · people and calls (Share Contact, Create Group, Schedule Call, or Add People, Roles for @mentions) · Export Chat History, Archive · in red Clear Chat History and Leave / Delete Group or Delete Chat.
- Back to the profile (Alex, 15 Nov: "after search X or a More item it throws me into the chat"): Profile.jsx sends roarme-find and roarme-roomact with fromProfile; the chat's room (Chat.jsx backToPf, backTick) waits until the search, the wallpaper, schedule, roles and seal windows are all closed, then sends roarme-profile, and the main Chat opens the profile again (modal 'info'). Delete and Create Group do not come back.
- Photos in a chat no taller than 4:5 (Alex, 15 Nov, a 9:16 photo filled the screen): Chat.jsx Pic reads the photo's shape on load and gives one taller than 4:5 the class .tall (styles.css, end: aspect-ratio 4/5, object-fit cover); the width stays as it was, shorter photos keep their own shape, a tap still opens the whole photo.
- Gallery light, File raw (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's): the attach sheet has no SD / HD any more. What is picked in the Gallery tab or taken with the camera is sent lighter, one middle size (photos 2048 px at .85, videos to 720p where the device can). What is picked in the File tab (Files, or its Gallery tile) goes exactly as it is, any kind (zip and the rest), as a file message with file.asFile (extras.jsx AttachSheet asFile, Chat.jsx sendFiles hooks.asFile; a photo gets a 320 px thumb only to show). In the chat such a file is Chat.jsx FileTile: a square with its picture or its kind, a round download sign, the name in the accent and the size; a tap downloads it with a ring that fills (fetch, kept for the visit in fileDone), then opens it: photos and videos in the app's viewer, anything else saved on a computer or opened in the browser inside the iPhone app. CSS .file.ft at the end of styles.css. prefs.compress and prefs.largePhotos are no longer read by the sheet.
- Live location (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's): the Location tab's foot is a small line with the place and two buttons side by side, the same size: Send Location (the pin's place, as before) and Live Location, which opens a menu (For 15 minutes, 1 hour, 8 hours, Until I turn it off) and then shares the phone's own place (share.jsx LocationPicker onLive, .loc-foot / .loc-acts / .loc-live-menu at the end of styles.css). Chat.jsx sendLive sends a message with file.live { until } and file.location, and a row in roar_live (supabase/live-location.sql: one row per message, members read it, its sender moves it, Realtime). src/live.js keeps this device's running shares in localStorage roarme-live and, while RoarMe is open, follows the phone with watchPosition, writing at most every 8 s (resumeLive at start). share.jsx LiveCard shows it in the chat: the map follows, a green pulse while it lasts, "updated N min ago · until hh:mm", Ended after; its sender has Stop Sharing. It does not move while the app is closed (that needs a native background location plugin).
- The + and ⋯ menus at the top of Contacts, Calls and Messages are small again (Alex, 15 Nov: "you had these smaller and they were nicer"): .side-head .menu at the end of themes-glass.css, rows 36px, 14px words, 19px signs, 24px corners, 6px around. The other menus keep the 15 Nov sizes.
- Every pop-up menu small again (Alex, 15 Nov: "I mean the menus"): last block of themes-glass.css, over .menu and .menu-list everywhere (the + and ⋯ menus, the long-press menus of a chat, a folder and a message, the profile's More, Mute and Video Chat): rows 36px, 14px words, 19px signs, 24px corners, 6px around; the profile's More 280px wide. This replaces the 15 Nov "with room" sizes (46px rows, 34px corners); the glass stays.
- No chat types in a folder's Add Chats (Alex, 15 Nov: "this is not needed"): SettingsPages.jsx FolderEdit's picker lists only the chats; an old folder that already has Private chats or Groups still shows them as rows with their X, to take them off.
- No Excluded chats box in Edit Folder either (Alex, 15 Nov: "this is not needed either"); a folder's saved exclude list is kept and still applies.
- Location foot fixed (Alex, 15 Nov, TestFlight): Send Location wears the theme's colour with its ink (styles.css end, !important over the sheet's glass); the Live Location menu is a portal over everything (.loc-live-back, near the foot, the screen dimmed), it had hidden under the tab bar. The attach sheet also counts anything picked while on the File tab as raw (extras.jsx asFile = pickedRaw || tab 'file'), after a photo from the File tab went as a photo.
- While selecting messages (Select), a tap anywhere on a message only picks it or lets it go, nothing opens (Alex, 15 Nov): Chat.jsx catches the click on the row first (onClickCapture) while picked is on.
- No effect on a quick tap inside a chat (Alex, 15 Nov): a message, photo, file or round video shows nothing when tapped; only a finger that stays 160 ms makes it sink (.pressing, Chat.jsx sink timer), then it pops (.held) as the menu opens. The squash on .msg .bubble:active and .note:active (themes-halo.css) and on .file.ft:active is gone.
- Clean-up and a lighter start (Alex, 15 Nov: "tidy the code, make the whole app light, quicker to answer"): the Article code is gone from share.jsx; Chat.jsx lost the unused emoji state and addEmoji and the recording timer that redrew the whole chat four times a second for a number nobody showed (RecClock keeps its own); unused imports went from tasks.jsx and SettingsPages.jsx. CSS rules whose every selector named a class no code uses any more (article, old attach tabs and quality switch, Create New Call bar, theme lab, layout demos and the like) were taken out of styles.css, themes-halo.css and themes-glass.css (about 30 KB), checked by a script that leaves any rule with a used selector, or a dead class only inside :is() / :not(). The map (share.jsx) and the photo editor (photoEdit.jsx) load only when shown (lazy with Suspense; a location in a message has a small empty card while the map comes).
- Privacy Policy for the App Store (Alex, 15 Nov; legal.jsx, dated 15 November 2026, English and Greek): adds the phone number and SMS (Twilio), Find Friends with only SHA-256 codes leaving the phone, Send / Live Location, camera, microphone and photos, Apple push, group calls and call links, the call log, reports, blocking, contact names, deletion inside the app, the legal bases, no tracking across apps; articles taken out. The Terms' Greek text now has the zero-tolerance paragraph (report, block, 24 hours) as the English. Its public address for App Store Connect: https://alexisdigipro-hub.github.io/roarme/?legal=privacy
- Pull to refresh on Contacts comes back to Contacts (Alex, 15 Nov: it landed on Messages): Chat.jsx keeps sessionStorage roarme-tab for the reload and starts on it.
- App Store check (Alex, 15 Nov): ios.yml now writes NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription (Send / Live Location) and NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription; the sign-up line links the Terms and Privacy Policy by the website's address (appUrl, a relative link opened nothing inside the iPhone app) and says there is no tolerance for objectionable content or abusive users; legal.jsx legalUrl uses appUrl too. Already in place: Report (message, person) and Block, reports looked at within 24 hours, Delete My Account inside the app, no payments, no other login providers (so no Sign in with Apple needed), nothing about downloads or the Home Screen inside the app.
- Menus solid (Alex, 15 Nov: "all the menus when you long press, with no transparency"): last block of themes-glass.css, .menu, .menu-list and .rx-pick are plain white by Day (#fff) and #2c2c31 by Night, no blur behind; corners, rim and shadow as before. Frost keeps its own.
- TestFlight only when everything asked is done (Alex, 15 Nov: "before you send to TestFlight, have done them all"): finish every change he has asked for in the session first, then send one build; never a build after each small change.
- Send Location's sign sits at the left of its words, as on Live Location (Alex, 15 Nov): the button's class was .main, which is also the app's page column (flex-direction column) and stacked them; it is .loc-btn.go now.
- Nothing heavy inside the chats (Alex, 15 Nov: "no weight in the messages, show thumbnails, compressed"): a photo's thumb is now 640 px at .72 (was 900 at .78); a video gets a still frame when it is sent (lib.js videoPoster: one frame at 0.5 s, 640 px JPEG, 320 for a File-tab video, kept as file.thumb), and extras.jsx VideoTile shows only that frame with a play sign and the size (.vid-poster, end of styles.css), fetching the video when it is tapped and playing it at once; videos sent before keep the old way. With Videos set to play by themselves (Settings > Data and Storage) they load as before. A raw video from the File tab now shows its frame in its file square too.
- The photo library inside the attach sheet (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's screenshot): inside the iPhone app the Gallery tab is a grid of the phone's own photos and videos, edge to edge, three across, the camera first (extras.jsx LibraryGrid; mediaCall reaches the native plugin @capacitor-community/media 8.x, added to mobile/package.json, over Capacitor's bridge: getMedias for 300 px thumbnails, 60 more as the end comes into sight; getMediaByIdentifier for the whole file, fetched through convertFileSrc, a HEIC turned into a JPEG). Each tile has a ring that takes a number when picked; the foot becomes the caption and Send with the count, and the picked ones go lighter, as the Gallery always does. The sheet is tall while it shows (.as-lib). If the plugin is missing or the Photos permission is refused it falls back to the old tiles; the web keeps them (a page cannot read the photo library). CSS .lib-* at the end of styles.css.
- The File tab as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov): a card of three ways in, line signs and words in the accent (Select from Gallery, Select from Files, Scan Document: the camera, its picture sent as a file; Scan Document only on a phone), a round search in the sheet's head that filters the list, and RECENTLY SENT FILES with each file's little picture (its thumb) or kind, name, size and when (extras.jsx RecentFile, FT_I, .as-ways). The recents showed nothing because they left out every photo and video: they now list what was sent as a file (file.asFile, or any file that is not a photo or video), not the Gallery's photos and videos.
- TestFlight tidies Apple's development certificates first (Alex, 15 Nov, "way 2", after build 41 stopped on "maximum number of certificates"): in ios.yml's Sign and send to TestFlight, a short Ruby step signs a token with the App Store Connect key and revokes every DEVELOPMENT / IOS_DEVELOPMENT certificate before the archive (each fresh Mac makes a new one); Distribution certificates are never touched. If it fails it only warns.
- Polls (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's New Poll): a Poll tab in the attach sheet (after Location; extras.jsx, the head reads New Poll with a Send pill that lights once the poll is complete). src/poll.jsx PollEditor: Question and Add Description, up to 12 options (a new empty line appears as the last is filled, "You can add N more options."), Settings with coloured signs: Show Who Voted, Allow Multiple Answers, Allow Adding Options, Allow Revoting (the first, the last on at start), Shuffle Options, Set Correct Answer (a circle before each option to mark the right one, a quiz), Limit Duration (1 hour to 1 week). Chat.jsx sendPoll sends a message (body "📊 <question>", file.poll) and the poll's row. PollCard in the chat: tap to vote (several, then Vote, when allowed), results as bars with the share and a tick on mine, green / red for a quiz, voters' faces when shown, Retract Vote, Add an Option, Stop Poll (its maker or a group admin), the time left. supabase/polls.sql: roar_polls (members read; Realtime on updated_at), roar_poll_votes (own votes always, everyone's only when the poll shows voters), roar_poll_state, roar_poll_vote, roar_poll_add_option, roar_poll_stop. CSS .pl-* and .poll-* at the end of styles.css.
- The attach sheet's Contact tab as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov: "thin, small letters, not chunky, no big send button"): extras.jsx ContactPick: a small letter over each group, each group a card of slim rows (40px picture, the name in plain weight 16px, last seen or @username small and grey, a fine inset line), the letters down the right side in the accent (sticky) to jump; a tap on a row sends the contact (the send arrow on each row is gone); the search opens from a round button in the sheet's head. CSS .cp-* at the end of styles.css.
- Stop Sharing on a live location is a plain pill in the message's own ink (currentColor at 14%), not red (Alex, 15 Nov: the red words did not show in dark mode; styles.css end).
- Accent buttons made after 15 Nov use the messages' pair, not --halo (Alex, 15 Nov, dark mode: Send Location was light with light words): Send Location, the poll's Send, Vote, chosen duration and picked boxes, the live menu, the library's picked ring and the like take linear-gradient(170deg, var(--halo-mine-top), var(--halo-mine)) with var(--halo-ink), as Send and one's own messages do; --halo alone is a light colour by Night. Keep new accent fills on that pair.
- The last message rises with the keyboard (Alex, 15 Nov: "when I tap to write, the last message stays under"): main.jsx sends the window event roarme-kb-move as the keyboard comes or goes (native keyboardWillShow / Hide, and the web's visualViewport fit); the open chat (Chat.jsx, scrollRef) keeps the messages' distance from the bottom for about half a second while the page changes height, so what was at the bottom stays just over the writing box.
- Reactions inside the bubble (Alex, 15 Nov: "never towards the middle, put it inside the frame, no odd gaps"): Chat.jsx renders .rx.rx-in inside .bubble just before .meta; chips sit on the bubble's outer side (right on one's own, left on theirs), 24px pills; on one's own a white chip. The heart takes --rx-accent: the colour of one's own messages at that moment (the chat theme's --my-bubble, or the accent pair), so it follows the theme (end of styles.css). The 14 Nov "over the bubble's edge" placement is replaced.
- A shared contact as a card (Alex, 15 Nov: "ugly, cramped"): Chat.jsx SharedContact .sc2: the 52px picture and the name with @username, a fine line, then View Contact across the card; the message's own text (the name again) is no longer shown under it.
- The reaction chip is a circle (24px) when it holds only the emoji, a pill only with a count, and lighter: currentColor at 7% on theirs, white at .45 on one's own (Alex, 15 Nov; styles.css end).
- The head's picture turns into Call and Video (Alex, 15 Nov, from Telegram's head: "the picture does the same as the name, so make it bring out these two"): Chat.jsx callsOpen; a tap on .head-ava flips the picture away and a glass capsule .head-calls (handset, camera) grows in its place; .plus-wrap widens from 44 to 112px on a spring, so the name's pill gives up width on its right; a call, a tap anywhere else or six seconds turn it back. The name's pill still opens the profile. CSS at the end of styles.css.
- The handset and the camera are fine-line drawings everywhere (Alex, 15 Nov: "wherever we have a phone, this phone; wherever video, this video"): ui.jsx I.phone and I.video drawn after Alex's screenshot of Telegram's head (15 Nov, second pass: "exactly that design"): the classic handset with rounded ear and mouth pads, and a rounded camera body with a trapezoid lens joined to it, stroke 1.3; 26px in the head's capsule.
- A shared contact half as tall and as wide as other messages (Alex, 15 Nov): Chat.jsx SharedContact .sc3, one row: 48px picture, the name and @username, a small round arrow; width var(--media-w). A file in a message laid out our own way, not Telegram's: a 76px clear picture (its kind in a corner) or the kind's tile, the name and size, and the download a 38px round button at the end with the ring while it comes (a → arrow once here); width var(--media-w). End of styles.css.
- Reactions ride on the time's row (Alex, 15 Nov: "a huge gap under the live location"): .rx.rx-in floats left on theirs, right on one's own (outside the time), so no extra band (the bubble already holds the time's float). One's own messages have a soft fall of their colour again (linear-gradient 170deg from --halo-mine-top to --halo-mine, or from a lighter --my-bubble to it), not flat (Alex, 15 Nov, changing 14 Nov's "flat"). The map in a place's message has the bubble's radius less 4px and a mask so Safari clips its tiles cleanly. End of styles.css.
- No frame round a map (Alex, 15 Nov: "wherever you can, no border, the background only under the words, as photos"): .bubble.loc-b has no padding and clips its map with its own curve (overflow hidden + mask for Safari); the map is 170px and reaches the edges, the colour shows only under the name, address and time. End of styles.css. A rule for anything new with a picture: no border round the picture, the bubble's colour only under its words.
- Opening a downloaded file (Alex, 15 Nov: a photo file opened a page showing alexisdigipro-hub.github.io): FileTile treats a file as a photo or video also by its extension (jpg, png, heic, mov, mp4…), so it opens in the app's viewer; anything else on a phone goes to the phone's share sheet (navigator.share with the file: Save to Files, AirDrop, another app) instead of a download page. The files themselves live in Supabase Storage; github.io is only the website's own address (a custom domain would hide it).
- The photo viewer's ⋯ as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov): extras.jsx MediaViewer menu: Show in Chat (onShow: closes and goes to the message), Save Image / Save Video (saveIt: the phone's share sheet with the file, or a named download on a computer), Reply (onReply), Delete in red; "Open in a New Tab" is gone (it showed the file's address). A photo or video sent as a file opens there with who sent it, when, and the message, so the menu and the bin work for it too.
- A PDF opens inside RoarMe, as Telegram's (Alex, 15 Nov): FileTile onPdf -> Chat.jsx pdfView -> src/pdfview.jsx PdfView (lazy): full screen, the file's name in a pill, "1 of N" as you scroll, every page drawn by pdf.js (pdfjs-dist 4.4.168 fetched from jsDelivr only when a PDF is opened; its worker wrapped in a blob from our own origin; pages drawn as they near the screen), and a glass bar at the foot with Save (the phone's share sheet into Files, or a named download on a computer) and Share. If pdf.js cannot load, a line says so and Save / Share still work. CSS .pv-* at the end of styles.css.
