- The phone and video icons are the very drawings from Alex's screenshot (15 Nov, third pass: "exactly as they are, both"): cut from it into src/icons/phone.png and video.png (alpha masks, 96px), worn by ui.jsx I.phone / I.video as <i class="ic-mask"> with CSS mask and currentColor, so they take the words' colour; 22px by default, 27px in the head's capsule.
- One measure for words in every kind of message (Alex, 15 Nov, angry: "one letter huge, the other small; if I enlarge them in Settings they all grow"): titles in maps, files, contacts, polls, songs, call cards take the message text's size var(--fs) (Message Text Size) in weight 500, their second lines --fs × .86, a poll's small lines × .8 (styles.css end). Anything new inside a message uses --fs for its words, never a fixed px size.
- In Frost the message menu's holder (.msg-menu) has no glass, rim or shadow of its own (Alex, 15 Nov: square boxes showed round the reaction bar and the menu); themes-frost.css end.
- The round video's front flash (white screen) keeps its controls readable (Alex, 15 Nov): .vrec.flash gives the round tools a soft grey with dark signs (the lit flash in amber), the bar stays dark, Send keeps its colour pair with a shadow (styles.css end).
- On one's own message the heart takes the message's ink (currentColor) on a faint chip of that ink, since the message's own colour made it vanish (Alex, 15 Nov, Frost); on theirs it keeps --rx-accent.
- Contacts' second line (@username · last seen) reads as a light grey at full opacity (55% of the words' colour), not a ghost (Alex, 15 Nov). Under RoarMe's contacts, inside the iPhone app, the phone's own contacts (phone.jsx PhoneContactsTail): the first time a pill "Show Contacts From Your Phone" (then the iPhone's permission; remembered in localStorage roarme-phone-contacts), after that by themselves; a fine line "From Your Phone", fainter rows (initials, name, number) with a small Invite pill (Messages with the invitation), only those not on RoarMe, 40 at a time with Show More, filtered by the search. CSS .pct-* at the end of styles.css.
- Over the photo library the caption and Send are solid (Alex, 15 Nov, TestFlight 44: "too transparent"): .as-lib .as-send has a fade of the sheet's colour behind it, the caption a solid white (#2c2c31 by Night) field with rim and shadow, Send its colour made solid (styles.css end).
- The attach sheet's Send arrow is centred in its circle (grid, no padding or margin; Alex, 15 Nov; styles.css end).
- Selecting messages: the bar (Cancel, N selected, Copy, Forward, Delete) stands at the foot in the writing box's place (Chat.jsx footer .pick-foot / .pick-bar), not in the head, which stays as it is (Alex, 15 Nov: the head was too tight for the buttons). CSS at the end of styles.css.
- The brand block (Wordmark with `by`: sign-in, lock, Settings' foot, legal pages): the mark takes the words' colour (white by Night), not the accent, and "by THEMADLIONS" is all one small size, 10px, THEMADLIONS as small as the word by (Alex, 15 Nov; styles.css end).
- .ic-mask draws its icon on ::before (the <i> itself can then carry a circle); in the profile's actions Call and Video wear the same 44px round glass as Mute, Search and More (Alex, 15 Nov, desktop: they had no button outline, since the circle was drawn on the svg). A rule that styles "button svg" as a circle needs its .ic-mask twin.
- Over the photo library the attach sheet's tab bar is solid (Alex, 15 Nov, Frost: "the whole menu has holes", the pictures showed through the bar that Frost draws with no fill): in Frost a stone capsule (#e9e3dc by Day, #3a322c by Night) on .as-lib and .as-loc (themes-frost.css end); in Roar white / #2c2c31 on .as-lib (styles.css end). Elsewhere Frost's bar keeps no fill.
- RoarTransfer for iPhone (Alex, 15 Nov: "make an app, do it all yourself"): a native SwiftUI app in transfer-ios/ (not Capacitor; see its README), bundle id com.themadlions.roartransfer, Team ID U56B8UZ9SQ. It lives here only because the App Store Connect key is in this repository's secrets. .github/workflows/transfer-ios.yml: check on pull requests (simulator, unsigned), Run workflow > testflight registers the bundle id and Associated Domains, needs the app made once in App Store Connect (New App), then archives and uploads. roartransfer.com/.well-known/apple-app-site-association (roartransfer repo) opens links in the app. It downloads with roar-transfer-get ?dl=1&o=1 (deployed v30: R2 answers as plain bytes). roartransfer-r2 is deployed with the pay_lock code (v34, 15 Nov); roarstudio still carries pay_lock code that is not deployed yet (merged with #208). Security check of 15 Nov: roar-transfer-get makes a password cover every file and resend (password ticket in &c=); roar-transfer-sweep and roartransfer-notify answer only with the Vault secret in x-roar-cron (supabase/transfer-cron-secret.sql); roar_transfer_forget() keeps data only as long as the Privacy Policy says (supabase/transfer-hardening.sql); accounts younger than 7 days email at most 10 people a day; the app has Settings > Delete account (roar_delete_me).
- RoarTransfer for iPhone looks exactly as roartransfer.com on a phone (Alex, 16 Nov: "make the application exactly as the web app"; the foot menu of 15 Nov is gone): the wordmark, PRO (amber) with the Settings gear in one glass capsule (no EN | GR: the app is English only since 16 Nov, L10n.swift Lang is fixed to en); under them the site's menu Send · My transfers · Receive (Receive where the site has Admin, which the app never carries); the card in the middle with the site's big dashed drop box; the site's foot under every page (Theme.swift SiteFooter, its pages in a window through router.page); titles and the wordmark in Jost (Jost.ttf, the variable file, UIAppFonts; Theme.swift jost(size, weight)). A change to the site's phone layout is mirrored here.
- RoarTransfer for iPhone, App Store (16 Nov): iPhone only (TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY 1 on the target in project.yml; XcodeGen's iOS preset would put 1,2 there and App Store Connect then asks for every iPad orientation, error 90474). transfer-ios.yml mode `screenshots` takes the App Store's 6.9" pictures on a Pro Max simulator (`-shot send|receive|mine|pricing|settings`) and pushes them to the branch appstore-screens. Build 65 is the one for version 1.0; the listing texts and review notes were given to Alex in the chat. Waiting on Alex: (1) a review account he makes himself in the app (an address of his own), which Claude then makes Pro in roar_transfer_pro; (2) yes / no to version 1.0 without selling Pro, the Pricing line "Pro is set up on roartransfer.com." removed first (Apple 3.1.1), and Pro sold by In-App Purchase in 1.1 (StoreKit subscription, monthly and yearly, server check setting roar_transfer_pro.until, Restore Purchases; Alex signs the Paid Apps Agreement and adds bank and tax); (3) Pro's prices; (4) his own wallpapers for the app, sent by a RoarTransfer link, after 1.0.
- Roardino for iPhone (Alex, 15 Nov: "make this an app for the phone too, as the others, as the site is on mobile"): roardino-ios/ (see its README), a Capacitor shell that opens https://roardino.com itself (server.url), so the app is always the site and needs no build for a change to it; with no connection it shows www/offline.html. Bundle id com.themadlions.roardino. It lives here only for the App Store Connect key in this repository's secrets, as transfer-ios/, and touches nothing of RoarMe (the 8 Nov rule "nothing of Roardino in RoarMe" is about the app). .github/workflows/roardino-ios.yml: check on pull requests (simulator, unsigned); Run workflow > testflight registers the bundle id, needs the app made once in App Store Connect (New App, name Roardino, SKU roardino), tidies development certificates, archives and uploads. No Apple notifications yet. Roardino's own source is public/site/social in themadlions-projects.
- Roardino for iPhone, the clock's colour (15 Nov): @capacitor/status-bar in roardino-ios/; the site (app.js wearPrefs) calls Capacitor.Plugins.StatusBar.setStyle with the theme, so the clock and battery read on dark and light. The fit under the clock and over the home line is the site's own CSS (env(safe-area-inset-*) in social.css), not the shell.
- Roardino for iPhone, the keyboard (Alex, 15 Nov, TestFlight: a gap between the chat's writing box and the keyboard, the top bar gone, and the bar of arrows over the keyboard): @capacitor/keyboard in roardino-ios/ with resize native (the web view ends at the keyboard, so the site's sticky bars and its foot stay where they are); the site (app.js, start) hides the accessory bar and sets html.kb while the keyboard is up, which social.css uses for the foot's distance. Roardino's pages scroll with the window, so RoarMe's way (resize none, scroll off) does not fit it.
- Roardino for iPhone, elastic scrolling (Alex, 15 Nov: "more bouncy, no click at the ends, everywhere"): Capacitor's iOS source sets the web view's scrollView.bounces to false; roardino-ios.yml turns that line to true in node_modules/@capacitor/ios before cap add ios (it warns if the line is not found). Roardino's pages scroll with the window, so this one switch gives the rubber band everywhere. RoarMe's own shell is not touched (it keeps scrolling off).
- Roardino for iPhone, the colour past the page's ends (Alex, 15 Nov, TestFlight 9: light bands above and below the dark theme when the page is pulled): Capacitor sets aWebView.scrollView.backgroundColor to the shell's one colour, and a colour set on the scroll view by the app stops WebKit from painting it in the page's own; roardino-ios.yml deletes those lines (CAPBridgeViewController.swift, seen in the log) so the page's html background, which follows the theme, shows there. Not yet confirmed on a phone.
- Signing in with the keyboard up (Alex, 15 Nov, iPhone; second pass after he refused the compact card at the top: "the whole frame just above the keyboard"): html.kb .auth is a flex column and the card has margin-top auto, and the page (fixed to the screen in Glass) ends at the keyboard (bottom: var(--kbh)), so the whole card sits with its foot 12px over the keyboard; the mark is 48px, and on screens under 900px tall the card has a little less air inside (40px mark, smaller paddings and gaps) so all of it fits between the clock and the keyboard; Auth.jsx scrolls the card's last row into view 380 ms after a field takes the focus. Do not move it to the top or hide parts of it (styles.css end).
- The PDF viewer's classes are .pdfv / .pdfv-* (Alex, 15 Nov: in Appearance the previews' messages flew to the screen's corners): it had taken .pv, .pv-head and .pv-name, which are the Settings previews' own classes, and its position: fixed caught them. A new block of CSS takes a prefix that grep finds nowhere else in src first.
- Security and App Store check (Alex, 15 Nov: "a very good check of the security gaps, Apple's conditions, and as light as it gets without losing anything"). Found sound: every roar_* table has RLS, column-level UPDATE grants (a member cannot make themselves admin or move a message), the 2FA policies are RESTRICTIVE, buckets for chats private, no secret in the repo, no HTML injection, links only http(s), Edge Functions check the caller (JWT or the hook token). Fixed: supabase/security-hardening.sql (roar_add_members asks roar_may_add, so blocks and "who may add me to groups" hold when a member adds people; roar_who is no longer callable from outside; fixed search_path on four helpers). pdf.js comes with the app (public/pdfjs, .js names so every server gives them a script type), nothing is imported from a CDN any more: an App Store app may not bring in code from outside (guideline 2.5.2). ios.yml writes App/PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy (no tracking; UserDefaults CA92.1, file timestamps C617.1) and adds it to the app's resources. Lighter: index.html asks Google only for Inter and the code face; a face picked in Settings > Font is fetched then (prefs.js FONT_FILES / loadFont). The stylesheets hold no rules of the old themes any more (checked, 300 bytes). Left for Alex: Leaked Password Protection in Supabase > Authentication, and roar-cleanup / roar-email answer anyone (they can only do what cron would do anyway). Never import code from another site at run time; a new library goes in public/ or the bundle.

- Roardino for iPhone, the photo library (Alex, 16 Nov: "the camera on top, my photo library under it" in New post): @capacitor-community/media and @capacitor/filesystem in roardino-ios/ (version 1.1.0); the site (app.js postFlow libCall / libFile) reaches them over the native bridge: getMedias for the grid's thumbnails, getMediaByIdentifier for the whole file, read through convertFileSrc + fetch or, failing that, Filesystem readFile. The camera preview is the site's own getUserMedia with the Info.plist texts already written. Not yet confirmed on a phone.
- Roardino for iPhone, the keyboard's corners (Alex, 16 Nov: "something happens round the keyboard"): with Keyboard resize native the web view ends at the keyboard and the window itself, black, showed behind the keyboard's round top corners; roardino-ios.yml adds one line to AppDelegate (window?.backgroundColor, the keyboard's own grey, light or dark with the phone). Not yet confirmed on a phone.
- Roardino for iPhone, the colour behind the keyboard (Alex, 16 Nov: "round the keyboard there are lines, the background is missing, things are cut", everywhere): roardino-ios.yml adds to AppDelegate a timer that paints the app's window in the web view's themeColor (the fixed keyboard grey of the same day is gone); the site (app.js kbTint) sets theme-color, while the keyboard is up, to the colour really on screen over it (the page's, black in Reels and stories, a window's) and puts the theme's own back when it leaves. Not yet confirmed on a phone.
- Around the keyboard in the iPhone app (Alex, 16 Nov: "lines round it, background missing, cut off sharply; check the whole app"). In the app the keyboard lies over the page (resize none), so anything that stopped at its top edge showed as a hard line with a flat colour under its round corners. The rule now: nothing ends at the keyboard. Every full-screen layer keeps the whole screen and only its inside steps up by --kbh (styles.css, last block, html.kb-native): .modal-back (its veil runs under the keyboard, the window is at most the room above it), .bw, .lock, the sign-in page (.auth: padding-bottom, not bottom), and the sheets that stand on the foot (.as-sheet, .te-sheet: their own glass runs under the keyboard, the caption row .as-send is lifted, the attach sheet is tall while one types). The page keeps its wallpaper while a field has the focus (html.kb body is flattened only outside the app), and a chat with a wallpaper of its own lays the same wallpaper behind the whole screen (.room-view:not(.same-wall)::before, fixed) so it is what shows under the keyboard. A new full-screen layer with a field follows this: pad its inside by --kbh, never shorten it with --vvh in the app. Checked on rendered pages with a stand-in keyboard, not on an iPhone.
- Qubi for iPhone (Alex, 17 Nov: "put this on TestFlight too"): qubi-ios/ (see its README), a Capacitor shell that opens https://qubi.gr itself, as roardino-ios/; bundle id com.themadlions.qubi; .github/workflows/qubi-ios.yml (check on pull requests; Run workflow > testflight registers the bundle id, needs the app made once in App Store Connect: New App, SKU qubi). Qubi's own source is the repository qubi-site. It lives here only for the App Store Connect key and touches nothing of RoarMe.
- 18 Nov (Alex, four screenshots): the heart under a message is red (#ff3b30) on anyone's message, in every theme (styles.css end; the 14 and 15 Nov "accent" and "ink" hearts are replaced). New group in the newer hand of the attach sheet and the poll (Chat.jsx NewGroup, .ng): the group's picture as a round button beside the name (file, CropModal, kept until the group is made, then uploaded to roar-avatars and set with roar_set_group_photo; a failed picture does not hold the group back), the name in a soft card, a small Members label with the count, then chips, search and list as before. A poll's options are put in order by a grip at the right of each written option (poll.jsx grip / dragMove: pointer events, the held row changes places with the one it passes and lifts, .pl-opt.drag; the empty last line never moves). In a poll's results my own vote shows as a tick in a small box under the share (.poll-me) with the option in a stronger weight, and each option shows its number of votes at the right (.poll-n); the small round tick beside the label stays only for a quiz's right and wrong. The reorder was checked only as a drawing, not by dragging in a built app.
- Wallpapers and any colour (Alex, 18 Nov). A colour of one's own as wallpaper did nothing since the page took the wallpaper (the base-grey rule body.app-wall[data-bg]:not([data-bg='soft']) was more specific than the colour's rule and won); fixed with a stronger selector (styles.css end). The list lost the look-alikes ("take the similar colours out"): Blue, Lilac, Rose, Twilight and Candy are in extras.jsx HALO_HIDE (whoever wears one still sees its tile; their CSS stays). New: White (key 'white': #fff by Day, near-black by Night, right after Plain) and Sky (key 'halo-sky': a light blue falling into a paler one, deep blue by Night); both are added in extras.jsx (WHITE, HALO_MORE), not in roar_wallpapers. Any colour is picked in a window of its own, themeEditor.jsx ColorPick (the Edit Your Theme field: hue across, white at the foot, a darkness bar, #hex; Cancel / Set): the Colour tile of the wallpapers opens it (the native colour input is gone), and so does the rainbow dot at the end of Accent Colour (Any colour: prefs.accent 'h-custom' with accentHex; prefs.js customAccent works out its lighter top, its light form for the dark and its ink). Seen on rendered pages, not on a phone.
- 18 Nov, second round (Alex, four screenshots). Poll editor: each option row is a <label> for its field (poll.jsx, htmlFor / id plo-<id>), so a tap anywhere on the row, the circle included, puts the cursor in it ("Add an Option does not take the tap easily"); the grip stops the label's click. The poll's Send in the sheet's head had white words on white glass (the glass rule for pills beat .pl-send.on and only the ink came through): the accent's fill is now forced, with the same block for the other accent buttons that had weak rules (.pl-dur .pill-btn.on, .poll-add .pill-btn.on, .name-marks .round.on, .gc-join, .gc-bar-ico, .nc-link-ico). A new accent button gets its fill with !important under html:root:root:root:root, or the glass wins. Poll card: Retract Vote reads Revote; Revote and Stop Poll are quiet (a 10% wash of the message's own ink, no rim, no shadow, no red). Now playing (player.jsx NowPlaying, .nowbar) is one slim solid capsule, 38px: a small round play, the song and its chat on one line, the X, the progress as a hair along the foot; on a phone inside a chat it is fixed just under the chat's head (safe area + 52px) and takes no room, so the head no longer drops; in the list it stays in the flow. It still plays on across chats (one player for the whole app). Seen on rendered pages; the float's place under the head is reckoned, not seen on a phone.
- Now playing, third pass (Alex, 18 Nov, with Telegram's screens: "it rides over things; on every page it must stand in its own place and never over anything; find a prettier design"). It is in the flow everywhere, never fixed (the floating capsule of the same day is gone): inside a chat Chat.jsx Room renders NowPlaying.in-room right under the chat's head, over the pinned message (hidden while searching the chat); in the list it stands under the folders (.in-side.under-tabs, before .rooms-pager) and on the other tabs or while searching at the top of the list; the page's own bar at the top of .main shows only where no chat is open (.main:has(.room-view) > .nowbar is hidden). A tap on the song's name sends the window event roarme-goroom with the chat's id, which the main Chat answers. Look (styles.css end): one glass bar in the head's own glass (--lg), 46px, 23px corners: pause / play at the left with no coloured disc, the song and where it is from in the middle on two centred lines, the X at the right, the progress as a 3px line along its foot in the accent's pair; no equaliser, no time. A new page that should show it puts it in its own flow under its head. One's own messages fall a little further (165deg: 20% lighter at the top, the colour through the middle, 12% deeper at the foot; the same for one's own bubble colour). Seen on a rendered chat, light and dark: head, bar, pinned message and day chip each in its own place.

- The big player (Alex, 18 Nov, after Telegram's: "make us a player with a bigger window, but more 2026"): a tap on the Now playing bar's song opens player.jsx PlayerSheet (mounted once in Chat.jsx beside the other hosts; player.open / close, state.big): a window on a computer, the whole screen on a phone (pulled down by its head, data-grab). In it: Playing from <chat>, a round Show in Chat (what the bar's tap did before: roarme-goroom), a living tile in the accent's pair with moving bars in place of a cover, the song's name large with "N of M", a thick line to drag through the song with the time gone and the time left, shuffle · previous · play · next · repeat (off, all, one), and under them Songs in this chat. The list is the queue: player.jsx loadQueue asks roar_messages once per chat for its sound files (newest first, no files sent as files, no round videos), a song that ends goes on to the next, links are made as a song is reached (fileLink). AUDIO_EXT and isAudioFile live in player.jsx now (extras.jsx passes them on). CSS .rpl-* at the end of styles.css; only transform and opacity move. Seen on rendered pages (light, dark, phone, computer); next, previous and the list's loading are not yet heard on a phone.
- The player's motion (Alex, 18 Nov: "add animations where we have none"): the big player leaves with a slide (player.close sets big 'out' for 230 ms; at once when calm()), a new song's tile and name come in (keyed by the song), the play sign and the shuffle / repeat signs pop as they change (.rpl-sign), previous and next nudge their way under a finger, the chosen song's square pops, the list comes in as one piece, and the Now playing bar's words rise when the song changes. Transform and opacity only, off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion. Last block of styles.css. Checked as computed styles on a rendered page, not watched on a phone.
- An accent of one's own is not wiped by Chat theme > Back to the Theme's Own (18 Nov, found after Alex's "it did not save the theme"): accent 'h-custom' keeps its colour in prefs.accentHex, the field the old chat themes used, so that row showed up as soon as a colour was picked and cleared it. SettingsPages.jsx ChatTheme now leaves accentHex alone, and does not show the row for it, while accent is 'h-custom'.
- How RoarMe starts and what Reset Appearance goes back to is Alex's own look (18 Nov, after he pressed Reset thinking it was already saved and lost it; read back from his screenshots): by Day, wallpaper Sky (DEFAULTS.background 'halo-sky'), one's own messages #1bb4fb (DEFAULTS.myBubble), accent Cyan (DEFAULTS.accent 'h-cyan'), font DM Sans (DEFAULTS.font 'dm'; Greek falls to the system's face), others' messages the theme's white, no panel behind the chats, contacts Plain. This replaces Plain / Onyx / the system's face of 13 and 14 Nov. Frost's applyLook branch sets its own myBubble '', font 'system' and background 'soft' so it does not pick these up. A saved choice is kept; only DEFAULTS changed, so whoever has saved settings sees it after Reset Appearance. The font was read from the letters' shapes, not from a setting: ask Alex if it is not the one.
- The starting accent is #1BB4FB (Alex, 18 Nov, one word: "#1BB4FB"): DEFAULTS.accent 'h-custom' with DEFAULTS.accentHex '#1bb4fb', the same blue as one's own messages, in place of Cyan from earlier that day. Frost's applyLook branch also sets accentHex ''.
- The starting look, final (Alex, 18 Nov, from seven screenshots of his Appearance page: "these settings are the initial ones and the reset"): Roar, Light, accent Your Colour #1bb4fb at 100%, Glass Look on, one's own messages #1bb4fb, theirs the theme's white, corners the theme's own, message text 14.5 px, Compact off, wallpaper Sky at Wallpaper Strength 60 (DEFAULTS.wallStrength; 100 before), no panel behind the chats, Spacing Standard, contacts Plain, App background Same as Chats, App Text Size 100%, font SF Pro / System (the DM Sans of an hour earlier was a wrong guess from the letters), Smooth Animations on, Bold Focus Rings and Power Saving off. Change DEFAULTS in prefs.js only when he asks; a saved choice is kept.
- Glass buttons B tried and taken back (Alex, 18 Nov): the thick glass rim with the accent inside (sample https://claude.ai/artifact/UPJaERzaj7mrmQydW2hDQ9, he picked B) stood for an hour on the big player's previous, play and next; seeing it on his phone he said "make them as they were before". The player's buttons are again the plain ones: play a 70px circle in the accent's pair, previous and next bare signs. Do not bring the glass rim back unless he asks.
- The day's chip in a chat (TODAY, MONDAY 16 NOVEMBER) is 30% smaller and minimal (Alex, 18 Nov: "these texts always 30% smaller and minimal"): 8px letters (11px before), 2px by 8px inside, no rim, no shadow, no blur, a 5% wash of the words' colour. Last block of styles.css, every theme. Small labels of this kind made later (a divider, a section mark inside a chat) take this size and plainness, not a glass chip.
- Share a song from the big player (Alex, 18 Nov, from Telegram's Share with and the iPhone's share sheet): the player's head has Share beside Show in Chat (.rpl-head-end; player state carries the song's file, MusicTile passes it). It opens Chat.jsx ShareSong (PlayerSheet onShare -> songShare), a sheet over the player (z-index 70; a window on a computer): X, Share with / N selected, a round button that hands the file itself to the phone's share sheet (navigator.share with the file: Mail, Messages, AirDrop, Save to Files; a computer downloads it), a search, the chats as round pictures to tick (several), Send, which puts a copy of the song in each ticked chat (copyMessageTo, as Forward does). Inside the iPhone app the sheet's inside steps up by --kbh. CSS .rps-* at the end of styles.css. Seen on rendered pages, light and dark; sending and the phone's share sheet are not yet tried on a phone (a large WAV is fetched whole before the share sheet opens, so it takes a moment).
- The player's head made even (Alex, 18 Nov: "put the buttons somewhere nicely, centred, symmetric, not just thrown somewhere"): one round button each side, close at the left and Share at the right, and between them the chat's name as a pill (Playing from / the name), as a chat's own head; a tap on the pill is Show in Chat (the round chat button is gone). Last block of styles.css. Keep the head one button a side.
- Messages wider on a phone (Alex, 18 Nov: "all the chat's elements wider: of the whole width of the screen, 10% stays empty at the left"): under 760px one's own messages and cards (poll, song, contact, video, file, map, photo) start a tenth of the screen from the left and run to the right edge of the list; the others' mirror it, a tenth empty at the right. Last block of styles.css: --media-w 90vw, the row keeps the gap (.msg.mine padding-left / the others' padding-right = 10vw less the list's 22px side room, --msg-side), .bubble-col max-width 100% (80% / 88% before). A short message stays as wide as its words. A computer is untouched. Measured on a rendered chat at 390px (mine from 39px, theirs to 351px), not on a phone.
- The player, more (Alex, 18 Nov, from seven ideas he was offered: "put 1, 3, 6"): speed (player.speed: 1x, 1.5x, 2x, 0.5x round again; state.rate, kept for the songs that follow; the pill in the middle of .rpl-more, lit while not 1x), 15 seconds back and on (player.skip, the two round signs either side of the speed) and a waveform in place of the line (.rpl-wave, WAVE = 56 bars, played ones in the accent's pair, dragged as the line was). player.jsx loadWave: the shape comes from the sound itself only for files up to 12 MB (fetched and decoded once, while the big player is open, kept for the visit); a bigger file (a long WAV) gets a calm shape of its own, the same every time for that file, since decoding it whole would not fit a phone's memory. The other ideas, not built: lock-screen and AirPods controls, a sleep timer, who sent it and when, notes at a time in the song.
- Share with, two more (Alex, 18 Nov: "New Group and Show in Chat too, not as Telegram's: more discreet, symmetric, perhaps small buttons"): under the search two small even pills (.rps-acts / .rps-act, 34px, a 6% wash). New Group closes the sheet and the player and opens the app's New group window; the new group then brings the sheet back with it ticked (Chat.jsx songHold; Cancel brings the sheet back as it was). Show in Chat closes both and goes to the song's chat. Seen on rendered pages; the New Group round trip is not yet tried in a built app.
- The big player went white when opened (Alex, 18 Nov, build 69): the waveform's useEffect stood after PlayerSheet's `if (!open) return null`, so React saw one more hook once the window opened and threw, taking the whole page down. It now stands above the return. Rule: in any component with an early return, every hook goes above it; neither the build nor no-undef sees this, so check it by eye before pushing.
- Reactions on a photo or video lie on the picture (Alex, 18 Nov: "the hearts on photos and videos should not make an extra frame; put them on the photo, inside"): on a .bubble.pic.solo (no caption) the reaction chips sit at the picture's bottom-left corner as small dark glass chips (rgba(0,0,0,.45), white count, red heart), level with the time's chip at the right, so no band is added under the picture; a round video keeps them at its box's corner. They needed `inset: … !important`, since an older rule sets `inset: auto !important` on every .rx-in. The time on such a photo is white on its chip for anyone's message (the others' grey ink vanished on it). A photo with a caption keeps its reactions by the caption. Last block of styles.css. Seen on a rendered chat, not on a phone.
- Submenus at the side (Alex, 18 Nov: "these should not come out underneath in the same menu, they should come out at the side"): in a chat's long-press menu, Mute and Add to folder… open their choices in Chat.jsx SubFly, a .sub-menu.menu-list.sub-fly portalled to the page (the menu scrolls and would cut it), fixed beside the row: at its right, else at its left, and on a phone, where neither side has room, floating just under the row with its right edge on the row's arrow so the row stays seen. The row's place is taken when it is tapped or hovered (roomMenu.subAt). z-index 61 over .ctx; hidden until placed, then a short pop. Hooks in SubFly stand before any return. Measured on rendered pages (computer: right of the row; phone 390px: under it), not on a phone.
- The profile as a page (Alex, 18 Nov: "a tap on the name's bar slides it in to the right, in the same window where the chat was, using the whole window, not a small pop-up"): ui.jsx Modal takes `page`; with it the window is portalled into the chat's own pane (.app > .main, the one in sight) as .modal-back.as-page: it fills the pane with the pane's corners, a solid ground (#f2f5fa by Day, #1c1d22 by Night), no veil, no rim, no shadow, and the window slides in from the right (page-in .34s); with no pane in sight it is the usual window. Profile.jsx's Modal passes page. The profile's content keeps at most 640px, centred. A slide to the right and Back still close it; windows opened from inside the profile (Notifications and Sounds, crop) stay windows. Seen on rendered pages (computer 1280, phone 390), not in the built app.
- The profile page's head as a banner on a computer (Alex, 18 Nov, from Telegram's desktop profile: "like this, in our colours, keep the banner, and whatever we need"): over 760px, in the page (.as-page), the head is a card with 26px corners in the accent's pair (--halo-mine-top to --halo-mine) with a soft glow under it; one's photo, when there is one, lies blurred under it as a texture (.pf-hero blur 34px at .55, clipped with clip-path, since the head keeps overflow visible for Mute's and More's menus); the picture 110px round in the middle with a light ring, the name and last seen in the accent's ink; every action we have (Call, Video, Mute, Search, More) as five even tiles of light glass, sign over word (Mute on: a red tint); Back and Edit as light glass at the card's top corners. The tabs are a pill as wide as they are, centred. Last block of styles.css. A phone keeps its own full-bleed dark head. Seen on rendered pages, light and dark.
- The banner rounded on a phone too (Alex, 18 Nov: "the banner rounded at the edges"): under 760px, in the page, the profile's head no longer bleeds to the screen's edges: a card with 26px corners, 8px under the clock (safe area), as wide as the cards below it, the photo clipped to the corners (clip-path), Back and Edit 12px from its top. It keeps the phone's own dark photo look; the computer's is the accent banner. Last block of styles.css.
- Calls as the iPhone's Phone app, second pass (Alex, 19 Nov, two screenshots). (1) A call slid to the left shows a red Delete at its right (calls.jsx RecentCalls tStart / tMove / tEnd on the .call-row: the row and its (i) follow the finger by --slide, the Delete grows with it (width = -slide), let go past 44px it rests at 84px, a slide back or a tap on the row puts it away; a tap on Delete hides the call for me, as before). The ⋯ editing mode keeps its red minus. (2) An (i) at the right of each call (.call-info, a fine round line sign in the accent) opens the person's page, the same page as a tap on the name in a chat: Chat.jsx infoFromCalls opens that chat (the call's room, or the direct chat with them) and 40 ms later the profile (modal 'info'), so it slides into the chat's pane; Back from it comes back to Calls (infoBack clears activeId); Message keeps the chat. With no chat with them, their card (roarme-card). CSS at the end of styles.css. Seen on a rendered list, light and dark; the finger's slide and the page from (i) not yet tried on a phone.
- Pictures kept on the device (Alex, 19 Nov: "I don't think it works; the pictures and everything are slow to load", after Storage Usage read 0.1 MB in the iPhone app): a file's signed link changes on every visit, so neither the browser's cache nor the iPhone app's ever found a picture again and every chat fetched its photos anew. lib.js picLink(file) keeps every small picture (a photo's thumb, a video's frame, an old photo with no thumb) in IndexedDB ('roarme-pics', key bucket|path, which never changes) and hands back a blob link; only the first sight fetches it (through fileLink); in-flight fetches are shared; at most 2000 pictures, the oldest dropped first. Not for videos, sounds or files over 8 MB (they keep fileLink). Used wherever a thumb is drawn: Chat.jsx Pic and FileTile, Profile.jsx Thumb, extras.jsx (media grid, file recents, video poster, AllMedia), media.jsx. Settings > Data and Storage > Clear Cache also empties it (dropPics), and Storage Usage now counts it. Proved in Chromium: first sight 1 fetch, after a reload 0, after Clear Cache 1 again. Full photos in the viewer still load by link.
- What comes from the cloud is kept on the device (Alex, 19 Nov: "whatever loads from the cloud should be on the phone too, so the app is fast"): src/cache.js. The chat list (localStorage roarme-c-rooms-<me>, written by loadRooms) and the people (roarme-c-people-<me>, written as people change) are read at once when Chat mounts, so the list shows before the cloud answers; each chat's last 60 messages (IndexedDB 'roarme-data', key m:<room>, with a copy in memory for the visit; deleted ones left out) show at once when the chat opens (Room's useState from keptMsgs, else readMsgs while the fresh load runs; the fresh load always replaces them) and are kept again whenever the chat's messages change (keepMsgs, 900 ms later). Pictures: lib.js picLink. Profile pictures already have stable public links, so the phone's own cache keeps them. Signing out (supabase onAuthStateChange SIGNED_OUT) empties all of it: localStorage roarme-c-*, roarme-data and roarme-pics (dropKept). The full-screen viewer still builds its links all at once (caching there would fetch every big photo together), so full photos load by link. Proved in Chromium: 81 messages kept as the last 60 without the deleted one, the list read back after a reload, nothing left after sign-out.
- The main bar's Calls sign is the profile's handset (Alex, 19 Nov, from a crop of the profile's Call: "this design on the icon in the main bar"): ui.jsx FOOT_I.calls is the phone mask (.ic-mask.fi-phone, src/icons/phone.png), 24px, in the words' colour and the accent when chosen; it rings once when Calls is chosen (gi-ring on .fi-phone, end of styles.css). The handset with two waves of 13 Nov (fi-hand / fi-wave) is gone; its CSS stays, matching nothing. A mask image needs the same origin, so a test page from the disk must embed it as data.
- Night without blue (Alex, 19 Nov: "the dark should not go blue; a very dark grey"): last block of themes-glass.css, in Roar by Night (not Frost): --bg #121214, panels, --float, --muted and the bars' glass (--lg) are neutral greys; the page is #151517 into #0e0e10 with faint white glows in place of the lilac clouds; Sky by Night is a neutral near-black (#1d1d20 to #0e0e10), not deep blue; the wallpaper-strength veil, the sign-in page and the profile page's ground are neutral too. Wallpapers that are a colour of their own and the accent keep their colours. Measured on rendered pages (page #1f1f21, bars #1e1e21, R≈G≈B), not on a phone.
- A kept chat never flashes what is gone (Alex, 19 Nov: "I open a chat and for a second it shows a map or a photo that is not there, perhaps deleted"): the kept copy of a chat (cache.js) showed messages I had deleted for myself, since the list of those (roar_msg_hides) came from the cloud a moment later. Room now keeps that list on the device too (localStorage roarme-c-hides-<room>, read at once) and leaves those messages out of the kept copy; and a message deleted or changed while its chat is closed is put right in the kept copy at once (Chat.jsx realtime UPDATE -> cache.js fixKept). The first open after this build may still flash once, until the list is kept.
- No Clear History in a chat's long-press menu (Alex, 19 Nov): the row is gone from Chat.jsx's room menu; Delete Chat stands alone under the line. Clearing stays in the profile's More (Clear Chat History).
- Going into a chat without a stutter (Alex, 19 Nov: "the animations stick as I go into the chats"): since the kept copy, a chat opened with its messages and then, mid-slide, the cloud's copy replaced every one with a new object, so every row was drawn again. Chat.jsx sameMsgs keeps each message that did not change as the object already on screen (the row cache then reuses it) and keeps a message that came live meanwhile; the fresh copy lands 360 ms after the chat mounts when a kept copy is showing; the Delete for Me list is replaced only when it changed. Pic starts with a picture already opened in this visit (lib.js picNow), so it is in the first frame, without its fade.
- The chat's long-press menu on a phone (Alex, 19 Nov): 220px (min 58%) at the left under the preview, opening from its top-left; Add to Folder without the dots; SubFly takes its sides from the whole menu and on a phone puts Mute's and Add to Folder's choices at the menu's right, as wide as the room there (long names end in dots). End of styles.css. Measured on a rendered page at 390px (menu 14 to 224, choices 230 to 382), not on a phone.
- Appearance minimal, in three tabs (Alex, 19 Nov, from the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/KejKiy3PnxiJj7CYTvL6Va: "one cancels the other in the colours, make it minimal, no titles twice, tabs"): SettingsPages.jsx Appearance has Colours · Chats · App on a Lens at the top (.apx-tabs; sessionStorage roarme-look-tab). Colours: preview, Theme (Roar | Frost), Day and Night, Colour (the accents and Any colour). Chats: chat preview, Message Text Size, Compact Messages; Wallpaper (one for the chats and the whole app, with its strength); Name Colour in one short row. App: App Text Size, Font, Spacing, Contacts (Plain | Capsule); Effects (Smooth Animations, Power Saving Mode); Reset Appearance. Gone: the group headings, Message Colours and the chat theme editor's door (ChatTheme), Message Corners, Accent Opacity, Glass Look, App background, the Chat List panel and its settings, Bold Focus Rings, the Card and Inset contacts. prefs.js getPrefs keeps them at their fixed values (appBackground '', listPanel false, focusRings false, accentOpacity 100, glassBubbles true, theirBubble '', bubbleR 0, chatTheme ''; card / inset contacts become Capsule), and once (oneColour) turns a message colour of one's own into the accent when no accent was picked; applyLook paints one's own messages in the accent's colour (myBubble from the accent, none for the orange). Rows with a short control are .apx-line (words and control on one line). Frost shows Theme and Day and Night in one box. CSS at the end of styles.css. Seen as a sample page with the real stylesheets, not in the built app.
- Appearance's spacing set right (Alex, 19 Nov, TestFlight 84: "buttons ride over each other, the words go into the other box; close together, but not overdone"): the tabs' wrapper (.apx-pane) had dropped the page's 22px gap, so boxes and titles touched; it is a flex column with an 18px gap of its own, and a preview at a box's head leaves 8px before the first row. The three-way choices (Day and Night, Spacing) stand under their title at full width (set-col), only the two-way ones (Theme, Contacts) and Font sit beside their words, their buttons unshrunk. End of styles.css. Checked on a rendered page with the app's stylesheets at 390px.
- Media's five tabs fit a phone (Alex, 19 Nov, TestFlight 85: "something is off up there"): media.jsx's .lib-seg scrolled sideways under a fade at its right edge, so Music and its lens stood cut. Under 760px (end of styles.css) it no longer scrolls or fades: each tab takes an even share, its word centred, 13px, the count a small 9.5px chip. Checked on a rendered page at 390px (five tabs inside 364px, Music's lens inside the bar).
- Unlock with Face ID (Alex, 19 Nov: "open the passcode with Face ID too"): lock.jsx bioAvailable / bioName / bioOn / bioOff / bioCheck (#542). On the web a passkey made on the device only asks for the face or finger (its id kept as the lock's bio). Inside the iPhone app a passkey cannot be made (the app's page has no web address of its own), so there the phone's own Face ID is asked through @capgo/capacitor-native-biometric 7.x in mobile/ (plugin NativeBiometric: isAvailable, verifyIdentity; the lock keeps bio 'native'), with NSFaceIDUsageDescription in ios.yml. Settings > Privacy and Security > Passcode lock > Unlock with Face ID while a passcode is on; LockScreen asks at once once the app is in sight, and its Face ID button asks again; the passcode always still opens it. The simulator build passed with the plugin; not yet tried on a phone.
- A slide between tabs (Alex, 19 Nov: "wherever there are pages, slide right and left"): Appearance (.apx-swipe, display contents, data-tabs-swipe so the first tab still slides back out) and Settings > Admin now use ui.jsx useTabSwipe, as the profile, Media, the attach sheet and Tasks already did. Calls' All | Missed does not: a call's row slides left for Delete there.
- The lock has no field of its own (Alex, 19 Nov: "no need for two keyboards, I press on the screen"): lock.jsx LockScreen lost its hidden autofocused password input, so a phone's own keyboard no longer comes up under the keypad; a computer's keys still type through a keydown listener (digits, Backspace, Enter).
- The menu's glass in four more places (Alex, 19 Nov, from the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/K4AqpAMr7CD73VFBiXqwHV: "do them, go ahead"; last block of themes-glass.css, GLASS TRAVEL's beats, curves and split colours): (1) a Settings page and Media come in from the right, stretch a little and land (gx-page on .set-body / .lib, in place of the slide-in); (2) a Settings page's boxes grow like drops with the split at their rim, all at once (gx-box); (3) a switch's knob stretches with the split and lands each time it is turned (gx-knob-on / -off, on the scale property so the knob's own translate is kept); (4) a row of the chats, contacts or calls under a finger lifts into clearer glass with the split and lets go on the spring, after 70 ms so a scrolling finger lights nothing (scale, not transform, so a slid row keeps its slide). Off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion. Seen as a sample with the real stylesheets, not in the built app.
- 19 Nov, one round (Alex, by voice, "do all these and send the update"):
  - The contact card (contactCard.jsx): the chain button copies the contact's link and says so (copy() falls back to a hidden field and the old copy command inside the iPhone app, else shows the link); Share Contact opens the phone's share sheet and copies the link when there is none or it fails; Notes are written straight in the card (a field kept when it lets go, roar_contacts.note), no Edit needed and no jump to the name.
  - A call's slide to Delete starts only within CALL_EDGE (72px, calls.jsx) of the screen's right edge, or on a call whose Delete is out; Chat.jsx's list swipe leaves those touches alone, so the page no longer turns to another tab.
  - The message's menu: no Quote (quoting stays on selected words); the deletes in one order under the line: Report (others' messages), Delete for Me, Delete for Everyone (one's own, or anyone's for an admin, no "(Admin)" in the word), Ask to Delete; who has seen one's own message stands above them.
  - The profile's More: Block User (Unblock User) in red above Clear Chat History, for a chat with one person (Profile.jsx BAN_I).
  - Back to the newest messages: scrolled up more than 600px in a chat, a round arrow (Chat.jsx ToLatest, .to-latest) rises over the microphone, measured to its very place and size, and a tap glides to the bottom.
  - LIQUID POP (themes-glass.css, end): a drop of glass that overshoots and wobbles (wider, taller, wider) and lands in about half a second with the split colours fading; on the contact's card (.modal:has(> .modal-body > .cc)), the chat's long-press preview and menu (a beat apart), and the message's reactions bar and menu, the reactions popping in one after another as beads. Off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion.
  Checked as computed styles on a page with the app's stylesheets; not yet seen on a phone.
- The lock fills a phone's screen (Alex, 19 Nov): under 760px the lock card runs to 10px from every edge (safe areas added), its contents centred, the keys min(84px, 24vw) with 30px digits (end of styles.css).
- The contact card keeps its pieces whole with the keyboard up (Alex, 19 Nov: writing Notes squeezed the boxes into each other): .modal-body > .cc and .cc's children no longer shrink; the window scrolls inside (end of styles.css).
- A person's link works from the iPhone app (Alex, 19 Nov: "I paste it in Safari and it goes nowhere"): invite.jsx inviteLink used the page's own address, capacitor://localhost inside the app; it now uses lib.js appUrl() (https://alexisdigipro-hub.github.io/roarme/?u=<username>). Opening it opens RoarMe (signing up if needed) and a chat with that person by itself (takeInvite / pendingInvite). Used by the contact card's chain and Share Contact and by Invite people.
- A call's Delete stays put (Alex, 19 Nov, build 90: "it neither deletes nor stays, it goes to the next page"): the bar-at-the-foot swipe (Chat.jsx onStopTouch, Contacts · Calls · Messages · Settings) also leaves alone a touch that starts within CALL_EDGE of the right edge on a call, or on a call whose Delete is out, as the list's own swipe already did.
- In a group, a tap on a sender's picture or name opens my chat with them, made if there is none (Alex, 19 Nov): Chat.jsx toDm sends the window event roarme-dm, which the main Chat answers with chatWith (the direct chat in the list, else roar_open_direct). The picture is a bare round button (.who-ava); end of styles.css.
- The arrow to the newest messages, set right (Alex, 19 Nov: "squashed, cut at the edge"): the glass rules for round buttons outranked its inline size, so ToLatest now hands its size and right edge as variables (--tl-d, --tl-r) that the stylesheet applies with !important; it measures the microphone's own size (offsetWidth) and centre, not its look mid-pop, and measures again 450 ms later. Measured on a page with the app's stylesheets: 44 by 44, exactly over the microphone.
- A chat opens at its newest message even while photos load (Alex, 19 Nov: "it put me up there, not at the bottom"): for 2.5 s after a chat mounts, until the finger or the wheel moves it (holdNew, holdingNew()), it stays at the bottom: a row that grows pulls it down and a scroll in that time is put back; going to one message (goTo: search, Show in Chat, a reply) ends the hold.
- The contact card's window clips what scrolls inside it (Alex, 19 Nov: "lines spill out at the bottom"): .modal:has(> .modal-body > .cc) has overflow hidden, so its boxes end at the window's round corners (end of styles.css).
- The lock on a phone is one surface (Alex, 19 Nov: "fill the screen, one surface, not several"): under 760px .lock-card has no background, rim, shadow, corners or blur; the mark, the dots and the keys stand straight on the wallpaper (end of styles.css).
- The App Store page filled by the API (Alex, 19 Nov: "do the rest for me"): .github/workflows/appstore.yml (Run workflow, give the build) runs scripts/appstore.mjs with the ASC_* secrets: content rights, categories (Social Networking, Productivity), subtitle and Privacy Policy URL, age rating (Messaging and Chat, User-Generated Content), Free, copyright, description, keywords, promotional text, support URL, the build and the notes for App Review, all from mobile/appstore/listing.json. App Privacy, the review contact with the demo login and the screenshots are done in App Store Connect itself. The review account is @review (App Review), with a demo chat from Alex (place, contact, poll, a reply, a heart).
- The App Store screenshots are made, not taken (Alex, 19 Nov: "make the screenshots yourself"): appstore.yml mode screenshots builds the site and runs scripts/shots.mjs, which opens it in Chromium at 440 x 956 at 3x (1320 x 2868, the 6.9" slot), signed in as a made-up Nina Rossi with made-up chats, every Supabase call answered by the script (no real account, name or photo; the photo is from picsum.photos, the map OpenStreetMap's); the pictures go to the branch appstore-screens-roarme. Mode upload-screenshots does the same and sends them to the version's APP_IPHONE_67 set through scripts/appstore-shots.mjs (the old ones deleted first). Change the chats in shots.mjs, never the app.
- App Store, second pass (Alex, 20 Nov, from App Store Connect's "Unable to Add for Review"): iPad 13" screenshots too (shots.mjs ipad-*.png at 1032 x 1376 at 2x = 2064 x 2752, the computer layout; appstore-shots.mjs sends ipad-* to APP_IPAD_PRO_3GEN_129, the rest to APP_IPHONE_67); mainland China is left out of the countries (it asks for a security assessment and an AI filing there; appstore.mjs turns its territoryAvailability off); the age rating also answers sharedLocation (yes: Send / Live Location) and datingAndRelationships (no). appstore.yml mode status reads what is still missing, never the password.
- RoarMe for the Mac App Store (Alex, 20 Nov: "a proper app, not the iPhone one on a Mac"; he added the macOS platform to the RoarMe record): the same desktop/ Electron shell, built with electron-builder's mas target by desktop/mas.config.cjs (bundle id com.themadlions.roarme as the iPhone app, universal, sandboxed, version mobile/package.json's, build = the run's number). main.js under process.mas carries the built site inside (Resources/www served as app://roarme/ by protocol.handle; Apple does not let an App Store app load its code from a website), runs no updater and shows no update rows in the tray; preload.js gives the page roarDesktop.store, so update.jsx's offer and Settings > General's Download and Check for Updates are left out ("Updates come from the App Store"); lib.js appUrl() gives the website's address when the page is not on http(s). .github/workflows/mac-appstore.yml (Run workflow) builds the site into desktop/www, runs scripts/mac-signing.mjs (a Mac App Distribution and a Mac Installer Distribution certificate made fresh with the ASC key in a keychain of its own, the oldest of the same kind revoked only when Apple's limit stops a new one; a MAC_APP_STORE profile; entitlements.mas.plist from its .in with the team), builds the .pkg and sends it with xcrun altool. The .dmg build (desktop.yml) is untouched.
- The first Mac App Store build went up on 20 Nov (mac-appstore.yml run 2, version 1.0.0). Apple allows one Mac Installer Distribution certificate, so every run revokes the previous run's (as planned). appstore.yml mode status also lists the newest iPhone and Mac builds with their processing state.
- roarme.app (Alex bought it at Cloudflare, 20 Nov). Waiting on Alex: in Cloudflare's DNS, CNAME @ and CNAME www -> alexisdigipro-hub.github.io, both DNS only (grey cloud). Then Claude runs .github/workflows/pages-domain.yml (it checks the domain points at GitHub first and stops if not, sets the Pages custom domain, waits for the certificate and enforces HTTPS), and after that moves lib.js SITE_URL, the invite and call links, legal.jsx, mobile/appstore/listing.json (privacy and support URLs, then appstore.yml listing) and Supabase's Site URL / redirect list (Alex, by hand) to https://roarme.app/. Never set the custom domain before the DNS points there: the old github.io address redirects at once and RoarMe would be down.
- The Mac App Store page (Alex, 20 Nov: "start"): appstore.yml takes platform IOS or MAC_OS. With MAC_OS, scripts/appstore.mjs fills the macOS version (texts from listing.json's mac block: no Face ID, screen sharing, desktop review notes; the build; the review notes, with the contact and demo login copied from the iPhone version's review details, never printed), and appstore-shots.mjs sends mac-*.png (shots.mjs at 1440 x 900 at 2x = 2880 x 1800, no touch) to APP_DESKTOP. The app-wide answers (categories, age rating, price, countries) are shared by both.
- The attach sheet's inside ends above its tab bar (Alex, 20 Nov: in Transfer the Transfer button stayed under the bar and could not be pressed): .as-body's room at the foot is the bar, its gap and the phone's home line (safe area + 108px; it was 96px with no safe area). End of styles.css; the Location tab keeps its edge-to-edge map.

The custom status shows in the profile, the contact card and the contacts list (in place of last seen). Do Not Disturb: no sound or banner inside the app (Chat.jsx notifyRef) and roar-push (deployed v18) sends no sound, Web Push silent, APNs interruption-level passive. CSS .st-* and .av-on.away / .dnd at the end of styles.css.
- Waiting (Alex, 20 Nov): Birthdays, version B of the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/8bxhZwue9vLmnH7cgnoXhk (a slim bar at the top of Messages, "Today is X's birthday" with Wish) needs a birthday in Edit Profile (day and month, year optional, seen by My Contacts or Nobody). - Send's long press and slide (Alex, 20 Nov, from Telegram's menu on Send): holding Send opens a menu above it (Chat.jsx sendMenu, .menu.send-menu, portalled): Send Without Sound (file.quiet: roar-push sends it passive with no sound to everyone, the in-app chime stays quiet; a crossed bell on the time row), Schedule Message (what the long press did before) and Send Locked Message. A slide up on Send before the menu opens picks how important it is (Chat.jsx lvl / slide, .send-lvl: Normal, Important past 56px, Urgent past 120px, a tick at each step; let go to send): file.level 1 / 2, ❗ / ‼️ on the time row and before the notification's words, Apple's time-sensitive level (needs the Time Sensitive Notifications capability in the iPhone app, not yet in ios.yml), Urgent with the Roar sound. Neither breaks through a muted chat or Do Not Disturb.
- Locked Message (Alex, 20 Nov: "opens when it should, at a time or at a place"): src/capsule.jsx CapsuleWindow (the words, At a Time with a date and time, or At a Place chosen on the map, LocationPicker with sendLabel / title, and a radius 100 m to 5 km) and CapsuleCard (locked: the time left counting down, or the place with I'm Here, which sends the phone's position; open: the words with a small pop). The message itself is '🔒 Locked message' with file.capsule { at } or { place }; the words are in roar_capsules (supabase/capsules.sql, run 20 Nov), reached only through roar_capsule_put (the sender, once) and roar_capsule_open (a member, when the time has come or the position is inside the circle; the sender always). The place is checked from what the phone says. A locked message cannot be edited. No notification when it opens yet.
- The iPhone's own sound (Alex, 20 Nov): prefs.js SOUNDS has 'iphone' (inside RoarMe a short stand-in chime; scripts/push-sounds.mjs makes no file for it); roar-push sends it to Apple as sound 'default'. supabase/push-sound-iphone.sql (run 20 Nov) lets roar_push_subs.sound hold it. It shows wherever SOUNDS is offered (Settings > Notifications and Sounds, a chat's Tone).
- The sender in iPhone notifications, the server's half (20 Nov): roar-push (v19) adds mutable-content and sender { id, name, avatar, group } to every APNs message; the avatar only when the photo is shown to everybody. The app's Notification Service Extension that turns it into the round picture is built by ios.yml.

Privacy Policy updated (also birthdays and Locked Message). Open question for Alex: also in the chat's head?
- The sender's picture in iPhone notifications (Alex, 20 Nov): mobile/ios-ext (NotificationService.swift + Info.plist), a Notification Service Extension that ios.yml adds as its own target (bundle id com.themadlions.roarme.NotificationService) and embeds; it reads roar-push's sender, fetches the picture and returns the notification updated from an INSendMessageIntent (Communication Notifications). The app's entitlements now also hold time-sensitive and communication, and Info.plist NSUserActivityTypes INSendMessageIntent. Proved only by the simulator build until the next TestFlight.

Invisible stays offline (roar_people sends 'offline').
- The iPhone app targets iOS 15 and later (Apple's ITMS-90068 notice of 20 Nov, binding from April 2027): ios.yml step "iOS 15 and later" sets IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET 15.0 in the fresh Xcode project. The iOS 27 SDK (ITMS-90725) comes by itself once GitHub's Macs carry Xcode 27: ios.yml step Newest Xcode picks the newest released Xcode_*.app on the runner (on 23 Nov the newest was 26.6; no 27 yet). A pull request touching ios.yml runs its check in the same concurrency group and cancels a running TestFlight build: start TestFlight after such a PR has finished.
- The photo before it is sent, full screen, as Telegram's (Alex, 21 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/HzvtsMf5inTA3G2i3tYQzE version 2, "βάλ' το"): src/photoSend.jsx PhotoSend (lazy), opened by extras.jsx AttachSheet whenever the picked files are photos or videos from the Gallery (not the File tab), and inside the iPhone app by a tap on a photo of the library (openLib; the ring on a tile still picks it). Top: close (back to the sheet), "1 of N" with a slide between photos, the ring that keeps or leaves out the photo shown. Foot: the caption, a glass capsule Crop · Draw · Adjust · Quality (HD: sent whole, compress false) and Send with the count. Crop: frame with corner and edge handles (grid while dragging), Free / 1:1 / 4:5 / 16:9 / 9:16 on a lens, a ruler −45° to 45° to straighten, turn, mirror, reset. Draw: Pen, Arrow, Marker, Neon, Eraser drawn as pens, eight colours and any colour, a size wedge at the left, Undo, Clear All, Text typed on the photo (no window.prompt). Adjust: Enhance, Brightness, Contrast, Saturation, Warmth, Vignette as round dials with a ring for the value, and a ruler. ✕ throws a tool's changes away, ✓ bakes them into a new JPEG on the device (onReplace). CSS .phx-* at the end of styles.css (always dark; accent fills from --halo-mine-top / --halo-mine with !important; the foot steps up by --kbh in the iPhone app). The old photoEdit.jsx is still there for a picked photo shown in the sheet's grid, which this replaces for photos and videos. Not tried in a built app or on a phone yet (no packages could be installed here).
- The attach sheet's tab bar fits a phone whole (Alex, 21 Nov: "smaller gaps and icons, so all fit, no scroll"): under 760px (end of styles.css) the seven tabs take even shares with no gap and no sideways scroll, a 20px sign and a 9.5px word. Measured at 390px: seven inside 362px.
- Message Corners back (Alex, 21 Nov, angry: "where did the squaring of the bubble's corners go?"; it had gone with the minimal Appearance of 19 Nov): Appearance > Chats, under Compact Messages, the slider from Square to Round (prefs.bubbleR 1 to 25, applyLook's --bubble-r / data-bubble-r, the CSS never left) and Theme's Own while it is set; prefs.js getPrefs no longer forces bubbleR to 0. Before taking any setting away in a tidy-up, list it to Alex by name.
- Every menu as Telegram's context menu (Alex, 21 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/8VWpUa714Mu7MaT8ocLj42, "Όλα"): last block of themes-glass.css over .menu, .menu-list, .pf-menu and .sub-menu: 30px corners, 8px top and bottom, 46px rows with 17px plain words and 22px signs 22px from the edge, no lines between rows (an hr is a 6px soft band), light grey glass rgba(238,238,244,.84) with blur 28px and saturate by Day, rgba(44,44,48,.86) by Night, red rows (.danger) #e5483f / #ff6961. Replaces the small, solid white menus of 15 Nov. Frost keeps its own.
- The photo editor's pro tools (Alex, 21 Nov, "Όλα / Πέρνα τα όλα στο build"): src/photoSend.jsx with the pixel work in src/photoFx.js (process: one pass over the pixels for exposure, brightness, contrast, highlights, shadows, warmth, tint, split toning, saturation, HSL per colour, fade, vignette, grain, curves as 256-step tables, then sharpen; the same values draw the preview at 1.25x on screen, one frame at a time, and the full photo on ✓; canvas filters are not used, since iPhones lack ctx.filter). The tool bar is Crop · Draw · Adjust · Looks · Quality. Looks: Original, Film, Cinematic (teal and orange), Noir, Mono, Vivid, Warm, Cool, Fade, each with a small picture of the photo and a Strength ruler. Adjust: Light & Colour (13 dials: Enhance, Exposure, Brightness, Contrast, Highlights, Shadows, Saturation, Warmth, Tint, Fade, Grain, Sharpen, Vignette), Curves (Light, Red, Green, Blue; tap adds a point, drag moves it, drag a middle point off the top or bottom removes it), HSL (eight colours, Hue / Saturation / Luminance). Crop adds 4:3, 1.85 and 2.39, and for the wide two a Black bars button (the frame on a 16:9 picture with bars). Draw has Draw (Pen, Arrow, Marker, Neon, Blur, Pixelate, Eraser), Shapes (line, arrow, box, circle, dragged) and Text (Sans, Serif, Rounded, Mono; filled, outlined or plain; sized by the wedge; dragged to move, tapped to write; colour, face, style and size change the chosen text). Ink now lies on its own layer, so the eraser takes away ink, never the photo. Once a photo is changed the top has Revert (back to the photo as it came) and an eye held to see the original (a long press on the photo does the same, and in Adjust and Looks shows the photo before the tool). A spinner shows while ✓ bakes. CSS at the end of the .phx block in styles.css. Not tried in a built app or on a phone before the build.
- Four more (Alex, 21 Nov, "ΟΛΑ ΕΚΤΟΣ από κωδικούς", picked from a list; the two passcodes / Locked Chats stay parked):
  - The other person's battery beside their name in a chat's head (Chat.jsx .head-batt, batteryText from battery.js), only when they share it and it was written in the last hour; green while charging.
  - A notification when a Locked Message opens: supabase/capsule-notify.sql (run 21 Nov: roar_capsules.notified_at, pg_cron 'roar-capsules' every minute -> roar_fire_capsules -> roar-push {capsule}; roar_capsule_open knocks roar-push {capsule, by} the first time a place opens it) and roar-push v20 capsule(): at its time the chat's other members are told ("🔓 A locked message is open now", in a group with the sender's name), not muted, not blocking the sender, not while RoarMe is in front of them, quiet on Do Not Disturb; reached at its place, the sender is told who opened it.
  - The player on the lock screen and AirPods (player.jsx lockShow / lockState / lockPos: Media Session with the song, its chat, play, pause, previous, next, 15 s back and on, drag to a point); the iPhone app keeps playing locked (ios.yml UIBackgroundModes audio). A sleep timer (moon, at the left of the row under the buttons: 15 min, 30 min, 1 hour, End of Song, Turn Off; player.sleep, state.sleep is a time or 'end').
  - Who sent the song and when, under its name in the big player (Sent by X · 18 Nov; loadQueue reads user_id and created_at and the names from roar_people). Notes at a moment of the song (bubble, at the right of that row): a field with the moment's time; Send posts a reply to the song in its chat, body '🎵 1:23 · words' with file.songNote { t }, so the chat reads it too; the big player shows the song's notes as small dots on the waveform and in a Notes list (loadNotes, a tap goes to that moment). CSS .rpl-tool / .rpl-pop / .rpl-notebox / .rpl-notes / .rpl-mark at the end of styles.css.
  Lock screen, background play and the new rows not yet tried on a phone.
- The photo editor on videos, with Trim (Alex, 21 Nov: "the editor on videos too, with every setting the photos have", then "with trim, start"). src/photoSend.jsx: a video plays in the stage (tap to play or pause, a round play while paused, looping inside the trim); Crop, Draw, Adjust and Looks work on a still of the frame on screen (grabFrame, 1600 px, with the edits so far) exactly as on a photo, and ✓ keeps the tool as a step of the video's recipe (recipes per photo index: { steps, trim }; steps { t: 'crop' | 'fx' | 'draw' }, replayed in order on every frame, as a photo bakes them one by one). With steps the video plays through them live on a canvas over it (900 px). Trim (Trim component) over the caption: nine frames, a yellow handle at each end, the part's length, a white line where the video is; a tap on the strip goes there. Revert clears the recipe; the eye shows the video as it came. Send readies each edited video inside the tap (videoCompress.js prepareVideo) and src/videoEdit.js exportEdited plays it once, unseen, from the trim's start to its end, drawing each frame through photoKit.js applySteps into a canvas recorded with MediaRecorder with the clip's own sound (as long as the part lasts; a card "Preparing video N of M" with a bar and Cancel); standard at most 1280 px at about 2 Mbit/s (the chat's own shrinking then leaves it alone), HD 1920 px at 8 Mbit/s. Colours run on the GPU (src/videoGL.js: photoFx.js process as a WebGL shader; measured against the CPU on a colour ramp: mean difference 0 to 0.5 of 255, at most 3; sharpen is applied before the colours there, after them on photos). Videos over 3 minutes (VIDEO_EDIT_MAX) and devices without MediaRecorder send as they are, with the player's controls. The shared pieces (turned, makeFx, paint, FONTS, applySteps) moved from photoSend.jsx to src/photoKit.js; videoCompress.js now exports pickType, once and even. The export was proved in Chromium (a 3 s clip, trimmed 0.5 to 2 s, rotated, cropped, Cinematic, a box, a blur stroke and a word: 1.52 s, 120 x 256, mp4); the editor's screens for video were not seen in a built app or on a phone.
- Every menu tighter and even (Alex, 21 Nov, two screenshots: "smaller gaps, aligned the same even with an arrow, a touch smaller letters, the side menu does not fit, the same everywhere"): last block of themes-glass.css over .menu, .menu-list, .pf-menu and .sub-menu: rows 40px, 15.5px words, 20px signs 18px from the edge and 14px before the word, the same in rows with a side arrow (.has-sub > button), the Seen row (.menu-seen) and the side menus; the band between groups 4px; corners 22px; min width 210px; a side menu (.sub-fly) takes the width SubFly gives it (the old 250px minimum pushed it off the screen). The reactions bar (.rx-pick) wears the menus' glass, corners and shadow, Day and Night. Measured on a page with the app's stylesheets: every row 40px high, every sign at the same x, every word at the same x.
- RoarTransfer inside RoarMe knows a Pro account (Alex, 21 Nov: "it should recognise my account and know I have Pro; ask the others to sign up and even push Pro"): the RoarMe account is the RoarTransfer account, so r2upload.js transferPro() asks roartransfer-pro {action: 'me'} once a visit (admins count as Pro, as on the server). transfer.jsx then shows PRO beside the mark, Pro's four choices two by two (PRO_PLANS: 1 day 50 GB, 3 days 40, 7 days 30, 30 days 20, as roartransfer-r2 PRO_GB, which checks it again) and "No download limit". Without Pro a card under the choices lists what Pro gives (50 GB, 30 days, no download limit, a page to receive files) with See RoarTransfer Pro (roartransfer.com/pricing/); it is not shown inside the iPhone app or the Mac App Store app (Apple 3.1.1: no buying outside the store from inside them). Nobody inside RoarMe is without an account, so there is no sign-up call there. CSS .rt-pro / .rt-upsell / .rt-plans.four at the end of styles.css.
- Edit on a photo or video message changes its picture too (Alex, 21 Nov, "Ναι", from Telegram's Edit): while editing such a message (Chat.jsx mediaMsg: a photo, or a video not sent as a file, not encrypted, not by RoarTransfer) the round button left of the writing box (SWAP_I) opens a small menu: Edit Photo / Edit Video (the message's own file fetched and opened in PhotoSend, all its tools, Trim for a video) and Replace Photo or Video (the gallery, then PhotoSend). PhotoSend's Send keeps the result as editFile; the reference over the box reads New photo / New video; ✓ puts it up like any photo (2048 px at .85 unless HD, a 640 px thumb or a video's still) and sets it on the same message through supabase/edit-media.sql roar_replace_media (run 21 Nov: the sender only, not deleted, not encrypted, a photo or video, the new file in that chat's own folder; it sets file, kind and edited_at), then saves the caption as before. The message keeps its place and shows "edited". Not yet tried in a built app.
- An edited video in the chat (Alex, 21 Nov, build 106: "no picture at first, black when tapped, the X too high to press, swipe down or right does not get me out"): videoEdit.js exportEdited makes a 640 px still of the first edited frame and hangs it on the File as .poster; Chat.jsx sendFiles and the Edit save use it as the message's thumb before trying videoPoster (which can fail on a recorded clip). A video with no thumb loads with #t=0.1 so the phone shows its first frame, not an empty box. The full-screen video (extras.jsx Cinema) has its X under the clock's safe area and closes with a slide down or to the right (a touch near the foot is left to the video's own line), the video following the finger.
- RoarMe's own camera in the iPhone app (Alex, 21 Nov, from a camera with VIDEO · PHOTO · DUAL: "when you open the camera anywhere in the app, these features", then "all together now"): mobile/ios-app/RoarCamera.swift, a native full-screen screen (plugin RoarCamera: available, open { only }) put in the app by ios.yml step "RoarMe's own camera" with RoarViewController.swift (a CAPBridgeViewController that registers the plugin; Main.storyboard's class is changed to it). The preview in a card with rounded corners; X, flip and flash at the top; the shutter (white for a photo, a red dot for video turning into a red square while recording, a camera sign in Dual) with the newest library photo at its left (a tap answers 'gallery'); VIDEO · PHOTO · DUAL under the card, the chosen one in the middle, a slide on the preview changes them. PHOTO: flash off / on / auto (the front camera's flash is the screen lighting up white and bright). VIDEO: the torch, the time in a red chip, at most 3 minutes, with the microphone. DUAL (AVCaptureMultiCamSession, iPhone XS and later; not offered elsewhere): back and front at once, the front in a rounded corner, one photo made of both. Pinch to zoom, a tap to focus. It answers with the file in the app's temporary folder; src/camera.js openCamera reads it into a File (undefined on the web or a build without it, then the system's camera input is used). Used by the attach sheet's Camera tile (in the library grid and on the web tiles) and File > Scan Document (photo only); the file goes on to the editor like any picked photo or video. Proved only by the simulator build until a TestFlight.
- No grey box over a touched thing (Alex, 21 Nov, a file message showed a dark rectangle when tapped: "it selects something it does not need"): the iPhone's own tap highlight (WebKit's -webkit-tap-highlight-color) is transparent on everything, and buttons inside a message take no text selection or callout (styles.css end). RoarMe's own press effects answer a touch instead.
- A video with no still of its own (sent before the still came with edited videos) shows a quiet frame half a second in under the play sign, a muted <video> with no controls and no touches (extras.jsx VideoTile, .vid-still), instead of the phone's player; so a long press opens the message's menu as on any message (Alex, 21 Nov: "the video does not give the long-press menu"), and a tap plays it as before.
- A video's still is made on the iPhone too (Alex, 21 Nov: "still no thumbnail"): lib.js videoPoster puts the clip in the page unseen (2 px, opacity 0), plays it muted for an instant so the iPhone decodes it (it loads nothing of a video that is outside the page and never played), seeks half a second in and takes the frame once one is drawn (requestVideoFrameCallback, else 120 ms), with a fallback to the frame it is on when a seek never answers; 15 s at most.
- Menus smaller again (Alex, 21 Nov, a screenshot of the message menu: "even smaller, less height, a touch smaller letters"): the last menu block of themes-glass.css now gives rows 34px, 14.5px words, 18px signs 16px from the edge and 12px before the word, 4px above and below, the band between groups 3px, corners 20px; every row alike as before.
- The Away moon drawn right (Alex, 21 Nov: "they are all perfect, make the moon perfect"): .av-on.away and .st-dot.st-away take one crisp SVG crescent as their mask (a disc the size of the other dots with a round bite out of its top left), in place of the radial-gradient bite that came out uneven; styles.css end.
- Videos in a chat no taller than 4:5, as photos (Alex, 21 Nov): .bubble .vid-box is a size container and its still, frame or player is at most 125cqw tall (1.25 times its own width), cropped to the middle with object-fit cover; shorter videos keep their shape (styles.css end).
- RoarMe's own small video player (Alex, 21 Nov, the phone's inline player with ±10, PiP, sound and our two round buttons: "take all the icons off, leave only play / pause and the bar at the foot, and the right double tap"): extras.jsx MiniPlayer, used by VideoTile and the full-screen Cinema. No phone controls and no VideoTools buttons (wide, lights off) on videos any more: a tap plays or pauses (the play sign in a dark round in the middle while paused, and for a moment after a tap), a thin white line with a knob at the foot moves through it with the time gone at its left (in full screen the time left too; in a message the line stops before the message's time chip), a double tap opens it full screen and a double tap there closes it, a long press reaches the message's menu, the slide down or right still closes the full screen. CSS .mini-vid / .mini-pp / .mini-foot / .mini-bar / .cinema-vid at the end of styles.css; the 4:5 limit applies to the player's video too.
- The steps of a slide up on Send (Urgent, Important, Normal) stand whole on screen (Alex, 21 Nov: Urgent was cut at the right edge): .send-lvl lines their right edges up with Send's (--sl-r from Chat.jsx, at least 10px from the edge) instead of centring them on it.
- RoarTransfer's PRO stands beside the mark on its line, not under it (Alex, 21 Nov): .rt .rt-top is a centred row (styles.css end).
- No user ID in a profile any more (Alex, 21 Nov: "take this out, is it needed anywhere?"): the user id row at the foot of a profile and its switch Settings > General > Advanced > Show User IDs in Profile are gone (prefs.peerIds is no longer read); on a phone the Advanced box is not shown (it only held Keyboard Shortcuts, for a computer).
- Dual made to start (Alex, 21 Nov, build 109: "Dual does not work", a black screen): an AVCaptureMultiCamSession runs only on formats both cameras can hold at once, so RoarCamera.swift setupDual now gives each camera the largest multi-camera format at most 1920 wide (multiFormat / useFormat), drops to 1280 when the session's hardwareCost passes 1, and goes back to Photo (dualFailed) if it still cannot, if the session does not run, or on a runtime error (AVCaptureSessionRuntimeError).
- Videos as Telegram's (Alex, 21 Nov, two Telegram screenshots; this replaces the same day's tap-to-play MiniPlayer inside a message). In the chat (extras.jsx VideoTile with onOpen from Chat.jsx): a video plays by itself while in sight, muted and round again, no taller than 4:5, with only a dark chip at its top left of the time left and a muted sign (.vid-chip); a tap opens the full-screen viewer (openPhoto, whose list now holds the chat's photos and videos), whole and with its sound; a long press opens the message's menu; with Auto-Download off a still waits and a tap opens the viewer. Full screen (MediaViewer, isVid): no phone controls; a tap on the video shows or hides the bars; a big glass play / pause in the middle (.vw-pp), the line with the time gone and the whole length in a glass capsule over the foot (.vw-seek), and at the foot between Share and the bin a glass pill with Picture in Picture and the speed (1x, 1.5x, 2x, 0.5x; .vw-vtools). MiniPlayer stays for the Cinema view of RoarTransfer videos and links.
- Gallery and File as one tab, Camera in File's place (Alex, 21 Nov: "make Gallery and File one tab, choose at the top whether it goes as a file or not, and put the camera where the other icon was"): the attach sheet's tabs run Gallery · Camera · Transfer · Location · Poll · Audio · Contact. Gallery has Photos | Files at its top (.as-seg, a glass capsule with a sliding lens): Photos is the old Gallery (the phone's library in the app, the tiles on the web; sent lighter through the editor), Files the old File tab (Select from Gallery, Select from Files, Scan Document, recently sent files; sent exactly as they are).
