The Camera tab is not a page: a tap opens RoarMe's camera (camera()) at once. The internal tab 'file' stays, reached from the capsule; the bar lights Gallery for it.
- The pro camera (Alex, 21 Nov, three screenshots of a pro camera app: "these I want in the camera"; mobile/ios-app/RoarCamera.swift with its pieces in RoarCameraPro.swift, both added by ios.yml). In PHOTO: at the top X, the lens's ƒ and what comes out (12 / 48 MP, JPEG / HEIF / RAW / ProRAW); a row of flash, shutter speed, EV and ISO pills (the camera's own reading, yellow once set by hand) and flip. The picture shows the whole 3:4 photo (resizeAspect) with corner marks and the thirds; a tap focuses and meters there, a hold locks both (F/E, the mark yellow); lens buttons .5 / 1 / 2-5x from the back camera's virtual device (its switch-over zoom factors); pinch to zoom. Under it a ruler dial (RulerDial) for what is chosen: EV (−2 to +2), shutter and ISO (lists clamped to the format; custom exposure), MF (lens position) or WB (2500 to 8500 K as locked gains). Foot: the newest photo, a capsule with the mode (photo, video, dual, round again; a slide on the picture does it too), the shutter (held, with Rapid Fire on, a photo every third of a second) and the self-timer (off, 3 s, 10 s); the panel button; under them HDR (quality / speed prioritisation), 12M / 48M (only where the camera makes about 48 MP, iOS 16) and SI (back to auto exposure) · WB · MF (a second tap goes back to auto). The panel, two pages: Rapid Fire, F/E Lock, Grids, Tiltmeter (LevelView from CoreMotion), Semi-Auto, Histogram (every eighth frame, AVCaptureVideoDataOutput), Aspect Ratio 4:3 / 16:9 / 1:1 (bars on the picture, the photo cropped), Format, 2nd Shutter (a round shutter on the picture's right); Orientation (lock upright, else the photo follows how the phone is held), Mirroring (front), Anamorphic 1.33x (preview and photo stretched), RAW (only where the iPhone has it; a DNG, sent as a file), Black & White (preview by a saturation blend, photo by CIColorControls). Answers: { path, type, name, raw } or { paths } for rapid fire; src/camera.js openCamera now gives { files, raw }. VIDEO and DUAL as before. Proved only by the simulator build until a TestFlight.
- The pro camera in RoarMe's own look (Alex, 21 Nov, build 116: "you made it identical in the UI too; not exactly the same, by the theme we have", and the dial's numbers ran into each other): src/camera.js hands the camera the accent pair and its ink (as #hex, read from --halo-mine / --halo-mine-top / --halo-ink), Day or Night and Frost; RoarCameraPro.swift CamTheme turns them into the ground (the app's own light, dark or Frost stone), the words' colour and glass (glassify: a blur with a fine rim; glassLit / inner: the chosen thing a pill of the accent with its ink). Set apart from the reference: shutter, EV and ISO are three parts of one glass capsule; the picture is a card with 28px corners inset 10px; the dial a glass capsule with the value itself large in the accent over a short accent needle, the marks fading to the edges, a number drawn only where it has room (never on another, never under the value); the focus mark four rounded corners with a dot; lenses as accent pills; the foot without a capsule (mode and timer as round glass either side of a shutter ringed in the accent); one glass bar at the foot for HDR · 12M | AUTO · WB · MF (SI is called AUTO); the panel in glass with accent tiles. Proved only by the simulator build until a TestFlight.
- The pro camera, second pass (Alex, 21 Nov, build 116: "the grid open from the start; manual exposure does not work; Dual / selfie turned; take the 2nd shutter out completely; F/E Lock dead; the megapixels cannot be changed"). The cause of the manual part: the back camera was the virtual triple / dual camera, which takes no custom exposure, no lens position and no locked white balance, and offers no 48 MP. Now every lens is its own camera (RoarCamera.swift findLenses: ultra wide .5, wide 1, a 2 on the wide when the telephoto is 3.5x or more or missing, the telephoto with its number worked out from the two fields of view); a lens on another camera rebuilds the session, keeping what was set by hand; in PHOTO useBigFormat picks the 4:3 420f format with the largest photo, so 12M / 48M works (a tap where there is none says so). The grid is off at the start; 2nd Shutter is gone; F/E Lock's sign is the plain lock (lock.viewfinder drew nothing). Turning: on iOS 17 an AVCaptureDevice.RotationCoordinator gives the photo's angle (horizon level), and in Dual both pictures and both photos are set upright once the session runs; the gravity fallback had the two sideways angles swapped.
- The selfie on its side (Alex, 21 Nov): RoarCamera.swift uprightPreview gives the picture on screen the rotation coordinator's preview angle (iOS 17, for the camera in use, front or back, followed by KVO), applied when the layer is made, once the session runs and 0.4 s later; captureAngle() (the coordinator's horizon-level angle, upright when Orientation is locked) is used for photos and now also for a video as it starts.
- The selfie closed the app (Alex, 21 Nov). Guarded in RoarCamera.swift photoSettings: a photo's maxPhotoDimensions only from sizes within the output's maxPhotoDimensions, the quality prioritisation never above the output's maximum, and the front camera keeps its own format (useBigFormat only on the back lenses). appstore.yml mode crashes (scripts/appstore-crashes.mjs) prints the newest crash reports testers shared from TestFlight with their logs (build, device, system, comment; never the tester's name or email).
- The attach sheet's bar (Alex, 21 Nov: "a plus at the end with Contact and Poll, the camera first"): extras.jsx TABS run Camera · Gallery · Transfer · Location · Audio, and a + (AS_I.more) at the end opens a small menu over the bar's right end (.menu.asx-more, end of styles.css) with Contact and Poll; the + lights and takes the chosen one's name while Contact or Poll is open. The sheet still opens on Gallery.
- No black at the picture's sides (Alex, 21 Nov): in PHOTO the card is sized to the photo's own 3:4 (as wide as fits, as tall as that allows), centred, so the resizeAspect picture fills it. useBigFormat also takes video-range 420v formats (preferring full range), since the 48 MP format may be only that; 12M stayed grey on build 120.
- The library in kinds (Alex, 21 Nov: "take the camera out of here, more organised to find your files, with categories, photos, videos, favourites"): extras.jsx LibraryGrid has no Camera cell (the bar's Camera tab opens it); over the grid a sticky row of chips (.lbc-row / .lbc-chip, end of styles.css): All, Photos, Videos (getMedias types), then the phone's smart albums we know by name in English or Greek (SMART: Favourites, Screenshots, Selfies, Screen Recordings, Live Photos, Portrait, Panoramas), each only if it holds something, then the person's own albums A to Z (getAlbums; getMedias albumIdentifier). The chips carry the phone's own album names. Not yet seen on a phone.
- Photos sharp in a chat (Alex, 21 Nov: "the quality is bad, it almost pixelates"): the 640 px thumb was soft on an iPhone's 3x screen. Chat.jsx Pic shows the thumb first and, once it has loaded (so only for photos in sight), swaps in the whole photo (at most 2048 px, kept on the device by picLink like the thumb; not for files over 8 MB); a photo already kept whole shows whole at once. New thumbs are 1080 px at .8 (sendFiles and the Edit save; File-tab thumbs stay 320).
- Opening from a notification shows the new message (Alex, 21 Nov: "the phone locked, a notification, I go in and the message is not there, it takes long, I must go out and in"): the iPhone freezes the app and its live link in the background. Chat.jsx Room now fetches its newest messages (sameMsgs, so nothing already on screen is drawn again), reactions and reads when the page becomes visible or focused, or on the window event roarme-wake, at most once in 1.5 s; the main Chat reloads the chat list when it comes back, and a tapped notification opens its chat, reloads the list and sends roarme-wake.
- "typing…" in the chat list (Alex, 21 Nov: "here, show if someone is typing"): src/typing.js, Realtime broadcast on a channel per chat (typing-<room id>, known only to its members; nothing stored). The writing box (Chat.jsx Room onChange) sends { u, on: true } at most every 2.5 s, and { on: false } once the box is empty or the chat is left; the list (watchTyping for its first 40 chats, onTyping -> typingIn) shows "typing" with three hopping dots in the accent in place of the row's preview (in a group "Name is typing"), until 5 s pass with no word. It shows even with Show Last Message off. CSS .rty at the end of styles.css.
- The unread counts come back (Alex, 21 Nov: "a message came and nothing shows; show how many he sent"): the main Chat's live channel (roar-<me>) dropped in the background or on a bad network and never came back, so the list missed new messages. Its subscribe callback now makes it again 3 s after CHANNEL_ERROR / TIMED_OUT / CLOSED (liveTry, a new channel name each time) and reloads the list once joined again; coming back to the front with a channel not 'joined' remakes it too; and loadRooms runs every 20 s while RoarMe is in front.
- "typing…" in a chat's head too (Alex, 21 Nov: "everywhere, but discreetly"): Chat.jsx Room listens to typing.js onTyping and, while the other person writes, puts a small "typing" (in a group "Name is typing") with three tiny dots in the accent under the name, in place of the head's own small line (.head-typing, end of styles.css).
- A notification wears the name and photo I saved for the sender (Alex, 21 Nov: Darta's came with no picture, while Vaggelis's had one; Darta has no profile photo, the one Alex sees is his own in Contacts, as is the name "Darta Man"): roar-push (deployed v21) reads each target's roar_contacts row for the sender (nickname, photo_url) and uses it for the title, a group's "Name:" and the sender's name and picture on an iPhone; otherwise the sender's own name and photo as before.
- The library's kinds made to fit (Alex, 21 Nov, build 128: "it spills over and the buttons are cut"): the row of chips widened the attach sheet's inside, which then scrolled sideways (Photos | Files, the grid and the chips all shifted and cut). The sheet's inside in the library (.as-lib .as-body) never scrolls sideways, the .lbc-row keeps to its width (only it slides) and the chips have fixed whole sizes over the sheet's button rules (end of styles.css).
- The chat's + opens the camera first (Alex, 21 Nov: "when you press + in the chat, the camera comes out first"): Chat.jsx marks the sheet as opened by the + (attachCam) and AttachSheet cameraFirst opens RoarMe's camera at once inside the iPhone app, the sheet waiting unseen behind it (.as-back.asx-hide); the camera's library picture brings the sheet on Gallery, its X brings the whole sheet, a photo or video goes on to the editor as any picked one. On the web, and for files dropped or pasted, the sheet opens as before.
- The attach sheet's bar stays at the camera's foot (Alex, 21 Nov: "when + opens the camera, I don't want the menu below to go"): AttachSheet camera() hands openCamera tabs (camTabs: camera, gallery, transfer, location, audio, more), RoarCamera.swift draws them as a glass capsule at the very foot (buildTabs, SF Symbols, Camera lit), the camera's card shrinks to make room, and a tap on another tab closes the camera and answers { tab } (gallery as before), which the sheet opens (goTab; More opens the Contact / Poll menu).
- Edit on one's own photo or video now shows in the message's menu (Alex, 21 Nov: "what happened with edit / replace of the photo?"): Chat.jsx canEdit allowed only text messages, so the Edit Photo / Replace Photo button (SWAP_I, built the same morning) could never be reached; canEdit now also takes mediaMsg(m).
- Mac App Store build, certificates (21 Nov, run 4 stopped: "You already have a current Mac App Distribution certificate"): scripts/mac-signing.mjs reads the whole certificate list (the filtered one came back empty) and picks its kind by type or name, then revokes the oldest one by one until Apple takes a new one. Only Mac App / Mac Installer Distribution certificates, which only this workflow makes. A Mac build now goes with every iPhone TestFlight build (Alex, 21 Nov: "all the changes on macOS too").
- 21 Nov, two from the Mac app: (1) on a computer (no touch, 761px and up) the attach sheet's bar has Contact and Poll as tabs of their own and no More (extras.jsx wideBar), the sheet 680px wide (.as-sheet.as-wide); a phone keeps the + / More. (2) The profile page goes by itself when another chat or tab is chosen (Chat.jsx infoAt: a copy of the page, .pf-leave, slides out of the pane in .3s while the new place shows; the real one closes at once).
- Edit on someone else's photo or video (Alex, 21 Nov, "yes" to the proposal): the message's menu shows Edit on another person's photo or video; it fetches the file into PhotoSend (Chat.jsx copyEdit, every tool, Trim for a video), and Send posts the result as my own new message, a reply to theirs (sendFiles hooks.replyTo), marked file.editedCopy, which shows a small dark "Edited copy" chip at the picture's top left (.ecopy-wrap / .ecopy, end of styles.css). Their message is never touched. The Mac app keeps only Invite Friends for contacts (no reading the Mac's Contacts; Alex chose B).
- Edit Photo / Replace's small menu stands just over its button, as tall as its two rows (Alex, 21 Nov: it stretched from the top of the screen down to the box, since .menu's own top: 52px stayed with the inline bottom); Chat.jsx gives it top / right / height auto and styles.css (end) pins it.
- Pinch to zoom the photo library (Alex, 21 Nov, a Telegram video "Zoom in the Media Picker"): in the attach sheet's Gallery inside the iPhone app two fingers change how many photos stand across, through LIB_COLS (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 13; kept in localStorage roarme-lib-cols), a step each time the fingers open or close by a quarter, the grid swelling a little with the pinch between steps; the photo under the fingers stays under them (extras.jsx LibraryGrid anchor, useLayoutEffect); thumbnails 480 px at 2 across, 300 to 5, 150 from 6, and about 30 rows are loaded; from 6 across the rings and times step aside (a picked one gets an accent outline) and a month chip (.lbz-month, from the photos' creationDate) floats under the kinds while scrolling. CSS .lbz-* at the end of styles.css. Not yet tried on a phone.
- The sign-in page (Alex, 21 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/RVnhe1u4wqkVEuMyFkNLyu: "B, with the logo, Night, this blue, nothing else"): always one look, whatever the app wears and by Day too: his picture of blue light streaks (src/assets/auth-bg.jpg) under a soft dark veil, the card a clear sheet of glass with a fine bright rim and white words, the mark and ROARME on top, slim rounded fields with their sign inside at the right (Auth.jsx MAIL_I; PwField's eye shows or hides the password, also on Set a new password), Sign in in #1bb4fb, Create an account and Forgot password as glass pills. The labels are read only by screen readers; the placeholders say what goes in. Last block of styles.css on .auth:not(.ob) (the first-time steps keep their own look). The 12 Nov orange page in themes-glass.css is overruled, not deleted.
- The Mac app's frame slimmer (Alex, 21 Nov: "20 to 30% smaller, more at the top, mind the red, yellow, green buttons"): desktop/main.js BAR 22 on a Mac (30 before) and trafficLightPosition { x: 18, y: 9 }, so the three buttons sit in the middle of a 30px strip above the panels; styles.css end: html[data-desktop='darwin'] --bar 22px and, over 760px, .app padding 8px 12px 12px with a 12px gap (16 / 14 before). The .dmg app takes main.js's part only with its next release; the Mac App Store build carries it at once.
- RoarMe lives at https://roarme.app/ (21 Nov: Alex put CNAME @ and www -> alexisdigipro-hub.github.io, DNS only, in Cloudflare; pages-domain.yml set the custom domain and HTTPS). lib.js SITE_URL (so appUrl, invite and call links, legal links), phone.jsx INVITE_TEXT, desktop/main.js HOME (the old github.io address still counts as in-app: OLD_HOME), mobile/appstore/listing.json privacyPolicyUrl and the Edge Functions roar-official (ICON) and roar-email (APP, template) say roarme.app. The old https://alexisdigipro-hub.github.io/roarme/ sends people on to roarme.app by itself (GitHub Pages). vite's base is './', so the same build serves at the root. Supabase Auth: Site URL https://roarme.app and the redirect list (Alex, by hand).
- Messages on a phone 85 / 15 (Alex, 21 Nov: "85-15, in every message, ours and the others'"): under 760px one's own messages leave 15% of the screen empty at the left, the others' 15% at the right, cards and pictures at most 85vw (styles.css, the 18 Nov block; 90 / 10 before).
- Contacts From Your Phone without "That is enough for today" (Alex, 21 Nov): phone.jsx friendsFromContacts keeps the server's answer on the phone (localStorage roarme-c-phone: the hashes found, with the person, and the first 20 letters of every hash asked; per account, cleared on signing out). The whole address book is asked at most once in 24 hours (FOUND_TTL); in between only contacts added since are asked; if the server says no (the 15,000 a day limit, no network) the last answer stands and no error shows. Someone who joins RoarMe may still show with Invite for up to a day. The server's limit is unchanged.
- The sign-in page has the line "Born to connect. Built to roar." under the mark and ROARME (Alex, 21 Nov; Auth.jsx .auth-tag, 14px, white at .78, end of styles.css).
- How RoarMe starts and what Reset Appearance goes back to, 21 Nov (Alex, four screenshots of his Appearance: "these are the factory settings, on the phone, the app and the desktop"): as 18 Nov's (Roar, Light, Your Colour #1bb4fb, wallpaper Sky, contacts Plain, Spacing Standard, System font, App Text Size 100%, Smooth Animations on, Power Saving off, Compact off, Name Colour automatic) with Message Text Size 12.5 px (DEFAULTS.textSize 0), Message Corners 6 px (bubbleR 6; Frost's branch keeps 0) and Wallpaper Strength 31% (wallStrength 31). The desktop apps: the .dmg and Windows apps load the live site, the Mac App Store build carries the site of its build; desktop 1.5.2 carries the shell's changes of 21 Nov (slimmer Mac frame, roarme.app).
- The email's Confirm opens the iPhone app (Alex, 21 Nov): supabase/email-templates/confirm-signup.html links to {{ .SiteURL }}/?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=signup (Alex pastes it into Supabase > Authentication > Emails > Confirm signup; Site URL is https://roarme.app); public/.well-known/apple-app-site-association hands roarme.app links with a token_hash to U56B8UZ9SQ.com.themadlions.roarme; ios.yml adds applinks:roarme.app to the entitlements and turns Associated Domains on for the App ID; @capacitor/app in mobile/. src/emailLink.js watchEmailLinks (App.jsx): the page's own address and the app's appUrlOpen / getLaunchUrl go to verifyOtp, which signs the account in; a used or expired link shows a line on the sign-in card. Without the app (or on a computer) the same link opens the website and signs in there. Not yet tried on a phone.
- Each chat in one folder (Settings > Chat Folders, prefs.folderAlone), with one exception (Alex, 21 Nov: "Unread will be the only folder that shows inside All"): an unread chat shows in Unread and in All; read again, it goes back to its own folder (or Groups) and leaves All. Chat.jsx inFolder. Off, every folder shows all it fits, as before.
- The ad demo on @roaraccount ("The Mad Lion", Alex's account for filming, 21 Nov): its picture is a lion; seven made-up people (auth.users emails demo.<username>@roarme.invalid, raw_user_meta_data demo true, no password, not findable by contacts): nefeli_alx (birthday 21 Nov, battery), aris_frames (Do Not Disturb, battery), marina_sea, daphne_r, nikolas_music (Away), zoe_st, stelios_sk8, faces by Higgsfield Soul 2 in roar-avatars/<id>/demo-avatar.jpg; groups Night Shoot 🎬 (pinned call time, a place, a photo with hearts, a poll, an ended video chat, replies), Summer Squad ☀️ (a poll, two photos, a Locked Message, a live location), The Band 🎸 (two photos), and a chat with each person (a shared contact, two calls with Aris). Online people have last_seen 30 days ahead so they stay online for the filming. To remove it all: delete from auth.users where email like 'demo.%@roarme.invalid' (their rooms' messages stay; delete the rooms where created_by is @roaraccount and kind group, and the direct ones whose direct_key holds a demo id), and the files under roar-avatars and roar-files with those ids. Removed on 26 Nov (Alex: "delete all the test profiles"): the eleven demo.*@roarme.invalid accounts, their 14 chats and files, and the 14 empty "Test NN" rooms; @roaraccount and @review stay.
- Menus as liquid glass (Alex, 21 Nov, from WhatsApp's long-press menu: "ours is so dull; all our long-press menus the same: transparency, texture, rounding, design"): last block of themes-glass.css (seven :root) over .menu, .menu-list, .pf-menu, .sub-menu and .rx-pick: a clear glass that lets the chat's colours through (white .62 to .34 by Day, grey .58 to .42 by Night, blur 22px with saturate), 34px corners, a bright rim inside and a fine dark line outside, rows 38px with 16px words and 22px signs 22px from the edge, the band between groups a hairline inset 22px. Replaces the same day's 34px rows and near-solid grey glass. Frost keeps its own. Seen on a rendered page, Day and Night, not on a phone.
- Files > Select from Gallery inside the iPhone app shows the app's own library (Alex, 21 Nov: "a video from Files came compressed"): the iPhone's own picker shrinks every video it hands to a web page before RoarMe ever sees it, so extras.jsx AttachSheet libRaw shows LibraryGrid in the Files tab (a tap picks, no editor), and Send fetches each whole file (libFile raw: no HEIC to JPEG) and sends it as a file with no shrinking (onSend(got, caption, false, true)). Select from Files (the Files app) was always untouched. On the web the system picker stays. Whether the media plugin hands a video's very bytes or a highest-quality export is not yet checked on a phone.
- The two long-press menus are one glass (Alex, 21 Nov, build 153, two screenshots: "is this and this the same now?"): the message's menu showed the chat sharp through it, since its holder .msg-menu kept will-change: opacity (themes-halo.css), which closes its children off from what lies behind, so their backdrop blur had nothing to blur; the last block of themes-glass.css lets it go. The veil behind the chat's long-press menu and the message menu's scrim are the same grey by Day (rgba(70, 76, 92, .2)), so the glass stands out alike in both. Rule: never put will-change: opacity, opacity under 1, filter or a mask on the parent of something that has a backdrop blur; and test a menu inside its real holder (.msg-menu, .ctx), not alone. Seen on rendered pages with the real holders, Day and Night, in Chromium; not on a phone.
- The long press as Telegram's (Alex, 21 Nov, two Telegram screenshots: "image 3 must become like 1 and 2"; this replaces the same day's see-through "liquid glass" wash): behind a message's menu and a chat's long-press menu the whole screen goes soft (the scrim .msg-veil and .ctx: blur 26px under a grey veil), the pressed message stays sharp (Chat.jsx lays a copy of its row, .msg-lift, over the veil at its own place, under the menu, taking no touches), and every menu and the reactions bar are a light, nearly solid glass (rgba(236, 237, 241, .9) by Day, rgba(46, 46, 51, .9) by Night) that nothing reads through. Shapes, rows and corners as before. Last block of themes-glass.css. Seen on rendered pages, not on a phone.
- Where a message's menu stands on a phone, as Telegram's (Alex, 21 Nov, four Telegram screenshots: "look where it puts the menu according to the message"): Chat.jsx's menu-fitting effect now also makes the pressed message's copy (.msg-lift) and, on a touch screen, puts the reactions right over the message and the menu right under it, on the message's own side (right for one's own, left for theirs; the reactions bar's margin-bottom holds the message's height); when the three would run off the screen they move up or down together, the copy with them. A message too tall for that keeps its place and the menu stands where the finger was. A computer's right click keeps the pointer's place. A tap on the gap beside the message closes the menu. Not yet tried in a built app or on a phone.
- The blur is the background's, not the menu's (Alex, 21 Nov: "the background blurs, not the menu"): the veil behind a long-press menu (.scrim.msg-veil, .ctx) blurs the whole screen at 44px; the message's menu, its reactions bar and the chat's long-press menu have no backdrop blur of their own, only a light glass at 88% (Day rgba(240, 241, 245), Night rgba(48, 48, 53)), so the soft colours behind come through as in Telegram. Last block of themes-glass.css.
- The menus' glass with its shine (Alex, 21 Nov, build 153, Telegram's menu beside ours: "the same blur in the menu and the same glass, it has a shine; all the menus we have like it"): every menu and the reactions bar (.menu, .menu-list, .pf-menu, .sub-menu, .rx-pick) is one glossy glass: its own blur 30px with the colours kept, a 72% wash (Day rgba(242, 242, 246), Night rgba(44, 44, 49)) under a faint sheen from the top left, a bright rim thicker along the top, light pooling inside the edges, a soft shadow. Replaces the flat 88% glass and "no blur on the menu" of an hour earlier; the veil behind a long-press menu still blurs the whole screen at 44px, and the placement (reactions over the message, menu under it) was seen right on his phone. Last block of themes-glass.css (ten :root). Frost keeps its own.
- The long press opens and leaves as Telegram's (Alex, 22 Nov, from his screen recording of Telegram, read frame by frame: "yes, go on and send TestFlight"; prefix tgm-, last block of themes-glass.css). Opening, about .3s: the veil's blur comes on over .16s; the menu grows out of the corner that touches the message (.19s, one small settle; Chat.jsx sets data-side 'r' / 'l' on .msg-menu); the reactions bar starts .08s later, uncovered from the message's side (clip-path), its emoji popping in one after another from that side; the message's copy glides to its place (.msg-lift moves by transform, .2s). Leaving, .15s: Chat.jsx's effect cleanups keep copies for 180 ms (.tgm-out: the menu's copy draws back into its corner and fades, the bar and a veil fade, the message glides home; the chat's long-press view is copied whole and fades, its menu drawing back). The real ones are already gone, so no click waits on it. This replaces LIQUID POP's wobble on these two views. Off with Power Saving, Motion off and reduced motion (calm()). Checked as computed animations on rendered pages; not watched on a phone.
- The menu no longer rides over the pressed message's foot (Alex, 22 Nov, build 155): the message's size was read from the real bubble while it was sunk under the finger (scaled), so it read small; Chat.jsx now measures the copy (.msg-lift), which stands still at the same place, and the copy's bubble takes no animation or scale.
- Messages soften under a chat's head (Alex, 22 Nov, from Telegram's chat head: "in the chats I want this blur and fade, not cut as ours"): Chat.jsx Room measures everything that stands above the messages (the head, Now playing, the pinned message; a ResizeObserver and a MutationObserver) into --under on .room-view; the last block of themes-glass.css lets .scroll start that much higher (negative margin, same padding), fades its top away with a mask, and .top-haze (three plain strips of 1.5 / 3 / 6px blur, each shorter than the one under it) blurs what passes; the head, .nowbar and .pinbar float over it (z-index 3). Two things learned: styles.css switches every backdrop blur inside .side / .main off with !important (a new one there needs !important), and a mask on a layer that has a backdrop blur switches the blur off in Chromium, so the growing blur is made of steps, not a mask. Seen on a rendered chat in Chromium, not on a phone.
- The chat list softens under its head too (Alex, 22 Nov, from Telegram's Chats page: "like this on the page where the chats are"): Chat.jsx measures where .rooms-pager starts inside .side (the head, the search, the folders, Now playing, the birthday bar) into --under on .side; themes-glass.css starts the pager at the panel's top (negative margin; every .rooms gets that padding), masks its top and lays the same .top-haze over it; .side-head, .search-wrap, .nowbar, .bday-pill and the folders float over it (z-index 3). In both places the softening ends exactly where the head's stack ends (--under), so the first row or message at rest is clear. Seen on rendered pages in Chromium, not on a phone; the pull to refresh and the folder swipe were not tried with it.
- Copy link and Share in Invite people are the same pill (Alex, 22 Nov: "why do the buttons have different rounding"): WebKit draws a backdrop blur on a 999px pill with smaller corners, so the accent button's blur showed as a rounded box. Accent-filled buttons (.btn-main, .send.ready and their family) have no backdrop blur in Glass, and the invite's two buttons take 22px corners (half of 44px). Rule: a pill that carries a backdrop blur takes half its height as corners, never 999px.
- One theme again, Roar (Alex, 22 Nov: "take Frost out; for a start we keep only Roar, so take both out"): Settings > Appearance > Colours has no Theme row (Roar | Frost), and prefs.js getPrefs sets frost false for everyone, so whoever wore Frost is back on Roar with the colours and wallpaper they had there. themes-frost.css and the frost branches in the code stay, matching nothing while frost is false; Frost's pieces in Roar (the Frost wallpaper, the Stone and Umber accents, the Frost chat theme) stay.
- A person's link is roarme.app/username (Alex, 22 Nov: "plain username for everyone"): invite.jsx inviteLink gives `${appUrl()}${username}` (Invite people, the contact card's chain and Share Contact). GitHub Pages has no page there, so public/404.html turns a one-segment path that is a valid username into /?u=username (then takeInvite as before; anything else goes to /). The old ?u= links keep working. In the iPhone app public/.well-known/apple-app-site-association now also hands roarme.app/<one segment without a dot> to the app (not /transfer, nothing deeper, no files); emailLink.js passes every link the app is handed to invite.jsx takeInviteUrl first, which keeps the name and sends roarme-invite, which Chat.jsx answers by opening that person's chat (also when the app is already open). The app part needs the next TestFlight build and Apple re-reading the association file (it caches it for a while); not yet tried on a phone.
- Smooth Animations is always on (Alex, 22 Nov: "let it be smooth, give no choice, take the switch out"): Settings > Appearance > App > Effects has no Smooth Animations switch, and prefs.js getPrefs sets motion true for everyone (so html[data-still] is never set by the app). Power Saving Mode and the system's Reduce Motion still calm the animations, as before.
- Five more colours in Appearance > Colours (Alex, 22 Nov: "add #3AA7E8, our basic colour, and four more we don't have"): prefs.js HALO_ACCENTS starts with h-roar Roar Blue #3AA7E8 (shown right after the orange) and ends with h-mint Mint #14B89A, h-violet Violet #7B4DFF, h-magenta Magenta #D6338A, h-ocean Ocean #0F6E7A, each with its light form for the dark, its two-colour fall and white ink. The starting colour (DEFAULTS, #1bb4fb) is unchanged until Alex says otherwise.
- The address book (Alex, 22 Nov: "contacts as a real database for each user, in the cloud, to take out or join with another app; a full contacts suite", sample https://claude.ai/artifact/DFB7sDjj3c7TyQhTTWmHnv, "βάλ' το", and "not full of buttons, nicely grouped and lean"). supabase/address-book.sql: roar_book (owner-only RLS with roar_aal_ok, at most 10,000 a person): names, company, job title, photo, phones / emails / websites / socials as [{label, value}], addresses [{label, street, city, postcode, country}], birthday (day, month, year), notes, groups, favourite, person (their RoarMe account). src/book.jsx: loadBook / saveEntry / deleteEntry (kept on the device in localStorage roarme-c-book-<me>), vCard out (toVcf, 3.0) and in (parseVcf, 2.1 to 4.0, folded lines, quoted-printable), findSame / mergeEntry (the same email or the last 10 digits of a phone: joined, not doubled), readPhoneContacts and toPhone (the Contacts plugin inside the iPhone app; elsewhere the .vcf goes to the share sheet or a download), BookCard (the contact card's look: round actions, then one soft box per kind of detail, Share / Save to iPhone Contacts / Favourite, Delete alone; "isn't on RoarMe yet" with Invite when not linked), BookEdit (New / Edit Contact: green add, red minus, the blue label word changes the kind; groups as chips; link to a RoarMe @username) and BookIO (Import and Export in one small window: from a .vcf, from iPhone Contacts, export all or one group). Chat.jsx Contacts: rows are the book (cRows) then RoarMe people from my chats who are not in it; a slim row of group chips under the search only when there are groups; the head's + has New Contact (BookEdit) and Find on RoarMe (the old find-people window); the head's ⋯ has the sort (the chosen one in the accent, no tick: csort) and, under a line, Import and Export. A RoarMe person's card has Add to Contacts (BookEdit linked to them). Privacy Policy (legal.jsx, both languages) says what the book keeps. CSS bk-* at the end of styles.css. Not seen in a built app or on a phone (nothing could be built here); the database table still had to be run when this was written.
- Appearance centred and compact (Alex, 22 Nov, yes to six points): slimmer tabs (36px), 10px between boxes, Day and Night a small centred capsule under the preview with no title (SettingsPages.jsx modeRow .apx-mode), the colours' title small and centred (.apx-cap "Colour", no subtitle) over a centred grid of 36px dots five to a row (25 dots: the orange, HALO_ACCENTS, Any colour), Spacing beside its word (.apx-line), the preview only as tall as its message and writing box. CSS at the end of styles.css. Seen on a rendered page at 390px.
- The starting colour is Roar Blue #3AA7E8 (Alex, 22 Nov: "#3AA7E8 is our basic colour", yes to making it the start): DEFAULTS.accent 'h-roar' (HALO_ACCENTS), accentHex '', myBubble follows the accent. A saved choice is kept; Reset Appearance goes to it.
- Build 160 opened on a white screen (Alex, 22 Nov): Chat.jsx's address-book block stood after favs but before `const [contactSort …]`, and cRows read contactSort while rendering ("Cannot access 'contactSort' before initialization"); neither the build nor no-undef sees that. The block now stands after contactSort. Rule: a new hook or memo in Chat goes after every name it reads. How to run the app here when npm cannot install (the registry is blocked): transpile src with the global TypeScript (/opt/node22/lib/node_modules/typescript, jsx react-jsx), serve it with an importmap to cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/<pkg>@<ver>/+esm (react, react-dom, @supabase/supabase-js, livekit-client), launch Chromium through the agent proxy, put a made-up session in localStorage roarme-auth-2 and answer every supabase.co request with Playwright routes; then click through the changed screens before shipping.
- The number on RoarMe's icon (Alex, 22 Nov, from Messages' icon: "when a message comes, show the number like here"): supabase/badge.sql (run 22 Nov) roar_badge_counts(ids) gives each person's unread messages in chats neither muted nor archived (the app's own unreadAll count), for the service role only; roar-push (deployed v22) sends it as aps.badge with every iPhone notification (no number when the call fails). Inside the app, mobile/ios-app/RoarBadge.swift (plugin RoarBadge.set { count }, registered in RoarViewController, added by ios.yml) is what push.js setBadge calls, so the icon follows the app's count as chats are read; the Mac app and the web keep their own badges. Settings' badge switch (prefs.badge) still governs the app's own count; the server sends the number regardless.
- The icon's number follows Settings > Notifications > App icon > Unread Count on the App Icon in the iPhone app too (Alex, 22 Nov: "fix it"): the switch shows there (inApp()), push.js keeps each iPhone's choice in roar_push_subs.badge (supabase/badge.sql, run 22 Nov) on sign-up and on every change (syncPushSound), and roar-push (deployed v23) sends badge 0 to an iPhone that has it off.
- Phone calls through the carrier (Alex, 22 Nov: "if the contact has a number, the choice to call through the carrier"): book.jsx BookCard's Call, for a contact on RoarMe that has numbers, opens a small menu (.bk-callmenu): RoarMe Call, and Phone Call · <number> for each number (tel:); a contact not on RoarMe calls tel: at once, and every number in the card is a tel: link. Chat.jsx NewCall (Calls' +) lists, under RoarMe's people, Phone Calls: every number of every contact in the address book (phoneBook = book), a tap calls it through the phone. Chat.jsx loads the RoarMe people the book links to (loadPeople for bookPeople), so a linked contact with no chat yet still shows On RoarMe. Seen in the app run in Chromium with Supabase stubbed.
- RoarMe Pro, first web test (Alex, 22 Nov, from the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/HzxD32EAhTGsWVS7QPs4dT, "Pro grouped", "only the web version, not even the Apple app"): src/pro.jsx. proOk() is true only on the website on a computer (not inApp(), no window.roarDesktop, wider than 760px, a mouse). A Pro button in the bar at the foot, before Settings (FOOT_I.pro, a briefcase), turns prefs.pro on and off (lit in the accent while on); nothing else changes in the chat. With it on: the chat list stays the chat list, Projects and Direct join the folders (Chat.jsx BUILTIN / fits), a group that is a project shows its stage as a tinted chip before its last message (ProChip), and beside the open chat a panel (ProPanel, .prw / .prj, hidden under 960px): for a group Make It a Project, then the stage (In prep, Shooting, Post, Delivered), Due, Brief with fields of its own (Client, Budget, Location, Lead to start), Files in folders (Documents, Photos, Videos, Audio, Other, read from the chat's own messages; a tap shows it in the chat) and Tasks (To do, Doing, Done; the circle moves it on), and Stop Being a Project; for a chat with one person Projects together and Shared files. supabase/pro.sql: roar_pro_projects (roar_projects was already taken by an older empty table, left alone), one row per group, read and changed by its members, live. Turning Pro off hides all of it; the rows stay. Channels inside a project are not built (asked). CSS .prj-* at the end of styles.css.
- The Pro panel's width follows its left edge (Alex, 22 Nov: "drag it wider or narrower, left and right"): pro.jsx ProPanel wraps the panel in .prj-wrap with a .prj-grip on its left edge (a bar shows on hover), 260px up to the window less 760 (at most 640), kept in localStorage roarme-pro-w, a double click back to 330.
- In a Pro project the chat keeps only the talk (Alex, 22 Nov: "when it becomes a project, no materials stay in the chat, only the conversations; all the assets go to the side"): Room takes assetsAside (Pro on and the group a project) and shownMsgs leaves out every photo, video, document and sound file (pro.jsx isAsset: kind image or file with a path, not a round video or a group call); one with a caption stays as its words alone (asideAsset). They live in the panel's Files, which follows the chat live (a channel on roar_messages for that room) and opens a file in a new tab, its caption under its name. Voice messages, round videos, places, polls and contacts stay in the chat. Pro off, or a chat that is not a project, shows everything as before.
- A project's chat keeps only text and voice (Alex, 22 Nov: "voice stays in the chat, all the rest and the maps too go to the right"): pro.jsx isAsset now also takes round videos, places, live locations, polls and contacts; call cards, scheduled calls and locked messages stay. The panel adds Places (a tap opens OpenStreetMap at the spot; a live one shows a green dot while it lasts), Polls (the real PollCard, voting works there) and Contacts (a tap opens the card through roarme-card), each only when it has something. Only a photo's or file's caption stays in the chat as words.
- The project's workspace (Alex, 22 Nov, from the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/AKd1UNfScaixsPqkoaQqSg, "yes, let's go"): in a Pro project the panel is the main thing. pro.jsx Work: the chat a column on the left (.prw.big, --pro-chat, 400px to start, the grip between them drags it, roarme-pro-chat), the workspace the rest. One row on top: the name, the stage as one button (a menu; Rename Stages opens Stages: rename, colour, add, take away; roar_pro_projects.stages, DEFAULT_STAGES Development, Pre-production, Shooting, Post-production, Delivered), the date as a button whose name changes (Shoot Date, Due Date, Delivery, Premiere or one's own; date_label) beside its date, the people. Tabs on the app's Lens: Overview (brief, fields, At a Glance, latest media), Media (big tiles, All / Photos / Videos, a big viewer of its own with arrows, .prv), Files, Location Scout (a card per place: cover, status Option / Confirmed / Rejected tapped to change, photo strip, contact, permit, notes, Edit, Map; photos uploaded to roar-files <room>/scout-*; scouts jsonb), Calls (Start Video Chat, Voice Call, the call that is on with Join, scheduled, past; group calls and scheduled calls now leave the chat too), Links (every link in the chat, which stays there), Tags (every #tag; a tap shows its messages as one thread; a tap on one goes to it in the chat), Polls / Places / Contacts when there are any (Places also takes Google Maps, Apple Maps and OpenStreetMap links, which stay in the chat), Tasks (a board of three columns). In the chat, what left is one small line per run from the same person ("Nefeli · 2 photos and videos · in Media"), a tap opens that tab (window event roarme-pro-tab). Non-project chats keep the small panel. supabase/pro.sql adds stages, date_label, scouts (run 22 Nov).
- A #tag in a message is lit and can be tapped (Alex, 22 Nov, "ναι"): Chat.jsx withMentions (.msg-tag, in the accent; bold on one's own messages, never underlined); in a Pro project it opens the Tags tab on that tag (roarme-pro-tab {tag}), elsewhere it searches the chat for it.
- The workspace, second pass (Alex, 22 Nov): the tabs are a menu bar (.prw-bar on the app's Lens: a line sign over the word, spread across, the count as a small badge; TAB_I); a gear at the end of the top row opens the project's settings (which of Location Scout, Calls & Meetings, Links, Tags, Tasks show, kept per chat on the device, roarme-pro-hide-<room>; Rename Stages; Stop Being a Project in red). A document in Files opens inside RoarMe (DocView): a PDF in RoarMe's own PDF viewer, a Word .docx as its words (read from the file on the device: pro.jsx unzipEntry with DecompressionStream, docxParas), plain text kinds as text, anything else with Download. Location Scout has the TML Hub location fields (name, address, type Interior / Exterior / Both, contact, phone, notes, mapsUrl, lat, lon, tags, photos) plus status and permit, so a scout can later be sent to the hub's library as it is (not built: the hub is another Supabase project). Map opens Google Maps (search by the address, or the place's lat,lng); Places have Add to Location Scout beside every map link and place (In Location Scout once added). Calls is Calls & Meetings: Schedule Meeting (the chat's own schedule window, roarme-roomact 'schedule'); a scheduled meeting stays in the chat as its card (tap to join) and is listed in the tab.
- The Overview, calmer (Alex, 22 Nov: "only client and budget"): a new project starts with Client and Budget (FIELD_START); Location and Lead are taken out of existing projects once; the facts are small tiles (.prw-facts) with Add Field as a dashed tile. Ordinos (Alex, 22 Nov: "tap there, the ordino app pops up, you make it and it stays here"): Overview has an Ordinos box; New Ordino opens Roardino (themadlions.com/roardino/?embed=1, its embed mode, which now also trusts https://roarme.app) in a window (pro.jsx OrdinoWindow): on roardino:ready RoarMe posts the account's short-lived access token (roardino:session, again on TOKEN_REFRESHED; never the refresh token), and Roardino's Send posts roardino:send with the published ordino's link and details, kept in roar_pro_projects.ordinos and shown as cards (label, title, date, call time, place, its accent) that open the call sheet. This is Alex's own ask, over the 8 Nov "nothing of Roardino in RoarMe" (that was about the chat).
- A scheduled call's card in the chat, compact and readable (Alex, 22 Nov): events.jsx EventCard is .evx: its own calm card in any bubble (white by Day, #2a2a2e by Night, even on one's own blue message), the date tile, the kind small over the title, the day and time, the note, a foot with the time left (orange when near, green live), Join (from 10 minutes before) and the calendar, and a hairline showing how near it is.
- Location Scout gathered, with Cards and List (Alex, 22 Nov): a choice at the top (roarme-scout-mode); a card is a short 2:1 cover with the status and a photo count, the name and address beside two round buttons (Google Maps, Edit), one line of small chips (type, contact, tags); List is one slim row per place (thumbnail, name, address and type, contact, status, the two buttons). New Location sits at the top right.
- The workspace fresher (Alex, 22 Nov: "glass, buttons, motion"; the end of styles.css): a soft glass ground lit from the corners in the accent; boxes, cards, rows, calls and tag messages as glass with a bright rim (their backdrop blur needs !important inside .main); the choices (All / Photos / Videos, Cards / List: .prw-seg) roomy, 42px with 20px each side; each tab comes in with a short rise (.prw-pane keyed by the tab), cards and tiles lift under the pointer and press in, the chosen tab's sign pops, menus drop open, the viewer and documents zoom in. Off with Power Saving (data-lite), Smooth Animations off (data-still) and Reduce Motion.
- The project's facts are Artist / Client, Title, Category (suggestions in a list: Music Video, Commercial, Feature Film, Short Film, Documentary, TV Series, Branded Content, Photoshoot, Live Event) and Budget (Alex, 22 Nov), two to a row; existing projects are brought to them once (Client renamed, Location and Lead gone, anything else added kept after).
- Bins in the workspace (Alex, 22 Nov: "a small bin, it asks twice"): pro.jsx Trash on photos and videos (on the picture's corner, shown on hover), files, links, places and map links, polls, contacts, calls and meetings, and Location Scout cards and rows. Two confirms each; one's own message is deleted for everyone (it leaves the chat too), an admin deletes anyone's (roar_admin_delete_message), someone else's is Delete for Me (roar_msg_hides; the chat hears roarme-hide and hides it at once); the workspace leaves out what I hid. A fact has its own small bin (one confirm when it holds something).
- Artist / Client and Title name the group (Alex, 22 Nov): writing either sets roar_rooms.title to "Artist – Title" (or the one there), for a group admin (others get "Only an admin of the group can change its name"); the list reloads. An empty fact named Field, or with no name and no value, is taken out once.
- The small lines in a project's chat ("You · 1 photo or video · in Media") are off by default (Alex, 22 Nov: "I don't want them shown, but a setting to turn them on"): the project's gear > Chat > Show What Went to the Side (prefs.proNotes, false); a photo's or file's caption still stays in the chat as words.
- Stats, the workspace's last tab (Alex, 22 Nov: "the statistics of the whole chat, made nicely"): pro.jsx Stats counts the newest 5,000 messages on the device: numbers on top (messages, photos and videos, files, voice messages with minutes, links, size shared, days active since), Who Writes Most (bars), Last 30 Days, By Hour (the busiest hour and weekday), The Project (tasks done, locations confirmed, ordinos, members), Top Tags. One hue (the accent), thin bars, a hover names each bar. It can be hidden in the gear. At a Glance is gone from the Overview.
- The Overview with a cover (Alex, 22 Nov: "much more beautiful, with menus and animation"): pro.jsx Hero, the newest photo behind a dark fall (a slow drift), or the accent's glow; small glass chips for the category, the stage and the countdown to the date ("Shoot Date in 6 days"); Artist – Title large; the brief written on it. Then Details (the facts, two or four to a row), Ordinos with a date tile, Latest Media as a mosaic (the first one large) with See All.
- The group's name is shown once (Alex, 22 Nov: "the title is in the chat and on the other page, no need twice"): the workspace's top row has no name (it starts with the stage); the cover shows Artist – Title only when they are written.
- The ordino as a slim bar under the cover (Alex, 22 Nov: "there is usually one, the big box would stay empty; throw it in the group or send it to another"): with none, one wide Make the Ordino (opens Roardino); with one, its date tile, label, title, day, call time and place, then View (its page inside RoarMe, OrdinoView, with Send to… and Open), Send to This Chat, Send to… (SendPick: the chats, groups first, ticked, Send to N), Edit in Roardino and the bin (two confirms; the call sheet stays in Roardino). Sending posts a text message "📋 <label>: <title>", the day, call and place, and its link; in the chat it is an ordinary message, so Forward works on it too.
- The folders are the same with Pro on or off (Alex, 22 Nov: "what was made once stays the same; the only thing is pressing Pro and the groups change"): no Projects or Direct folders; one's own folders, Unread, Groups, Archived as always. Pro changes only what a project group shows (its stage chip in the list, the workspace beside the chat).
- An ordino in the chat is a card (Alex, 22 Nov: "a View so all the kids see it straight away, and the link with a Copy beside it"): pro.jsx ordinoOf reads file.ordino (sent with it since this day) or an older "📋 Label: Title / when / link" text; OrdinoCard (on the scheduled call's calm card, .event-b .ord-b) shows the label, title, day, call and place, View the Ordino (OrdinoView inside RoarMe, for anyone in the chat) and the link with Copy (Copied for a moment). The text body stays for the list preview and notifications; Forward copies it with its card.
- An ordino's name carries its artist everywhere (Alex, 22 Nov: "Ιουλία Καλλιμάνη – INTRO VIDEO"): Roardino's roardino:send already gives artist, now kept; pro.jsx ordName gives "Artist – Title" on the bar, the viewer's head, the chat card and the sent message; an ordino saved before without one takes the project's Artist / Client.
- Music, a tab of the workspace (Alex, 22 Nov: "MP3 or WAV dropped into the chat; a tab, or the Overview"): songs and sound files (player.jsx isAudioFile kinds) leave Files for Music: each the chat's own MusicTile (plays in the one player, the big player and the lock screen as in a chat), who sent it and when, a bin; the Overview shows Latest Music (three, See All). It can be hidden in the gear. A song dropped in a project's chat goes there (the line, when on, says "in Music").
- A song is a slim bar in the workspace (Alex, 22 Nov: "like the ordino, a small box, the song with its waveform, play; it keeps playing across chats and with the phone locked; send it as the other chats do"): pro.jsx SongBar on the Overview (the newest three, then All N songs) and in Music: a round play in the accent's pair (a slow beat while it plays), the name, kind and size and who sent it, its waveform (player.jsx shapeOf, or the real one once the big player read it) filling as it plays, a tap on it seeks, the time while playing; Send to This Chat, Send to… (SendPick "Send the Song": a copy of the file into each chosen chat's folder, as Forward does) and the bin. It plays in the app's one player, so it goes on when the chat changes, opens in the big player, and shows on the lock screen where the browser allows.
- 22 Nov, four more in Pro: (1) the workspace's tabs wear the foot menu's look (the --lg capsule, --lg-ink signs and words, the --lg-chip lens, the chosen one in --halo, counts as small accent badges). (2) A chat with one person shows that person's profile page at the right (Profile inline: ui.jsx Modal inline draws the page in place, no portal; Back hidden), with the chat column beside it. (3) ⇄ swaps the sides (roarme-pro-swap: .prw.big.swap turns the row over and the grip goes to the other edge); on the workspace it stands before the faces, on a profile at its top left. (4) The cover has its own pictures in turn (arrows and dots): one's own cover (uploaded through CropModal wide=3, 1600 px, roar-avatars/<me>/cover-<room>-*.jpg, a public link in roar_pro_projects.cover), the group's photo, then the project's newest photos; the chosen one is kept (cover_pick); remove takes one's own cover off. supabase/pro.sql adds cover and cover_pick (run 22 Nov).
- On the Overview the songs stand above the ordino (Alex, 22 Nov).
- Anything dropped goes straight into the chat (Alex, 22 Nov: "anything I drop in the chat, file, text, PDF, video, any file: upload it, not a window, not playing it"): Room dropIn sends the dropped files at once through sendFiles (photos and videos lighter as from the Gallery, everything else as a file), with a "Sending …" notice, in place of opening the attach sheet; dropped text goes into the writing box. In Pro the workspace takes drops too (a veil "Drop to send to the chat", window event roarme-drop to the open chat). A file dropped anywhere else is never opened by the browser (window dragover / drop guard in Chat).
- A song's bar has no Send to This Chat (it is in the chat already; Alex, 22 Nov), only Send to… and the bin.
- Admin, the workspace's last tab for those let in (Alex, 22 Nov: "under Stats an Admin: budget, entering expenses, who worked and fees, the statistics more gathered below; admin will be specific members"): supabase/pro.sql roar_pro_admin (run 22 Nov), read and changed only by the group's admins and the members in its access list (RLS; a trigger lets only the group's admins change access). pro.jsx Admin: Who Sees Admin (the group's admins tick members), Budget (typed, EUR / USD / GBP), Expenses, Fees (with what is unpaid), Left or Over budget, a bar; Expenses (what, amount, category, date, paid by; totals by category) and Who Worked (name, role, fee, Paid / Unpaid) each with a one-line form and bins; then the chat's numbers, smaller (Stats compact). Others see the Stats tab as before.
- The gear's last switch names the last tab: Admin for those who see Admin (hides it), Stats for the others (Alex, 22 Nov).
- Stats fully gathered and minimal (Alex, 22 Nov): one card, the numbers as one quiet line, the four parts side by side (two on a narrower screen) with hairlines between, thin bars and short charts; the same in Admin.
- Music lives in Files and hashtags are gone (Alex, 22 Nov: "put music in Files, apart: Documents, a line, Music, anything else apart; take the hashtags out completely"): the Files tab has sections Documents, Music (the song bars) and Other, each with a count and a line between; there is no Music tab and no Tags tab, no lit or tappable #tag in the chat, no Top Tags in Stats. The Overview keeps its song bars (See All opens Files). Location Scout's own tags (the hub's field) stay.
- Places live in Links (Alex, 22 Nov: "places are links, put them in Links, under a line"): the Links tab has sections Links (every link that is not a map) and Places (places, live locations, Google / Apple Maps links, each with Add to Location Scout); no Places tab.
- Pro shows nothing beside a chat with one person (Alex, 22 Nov: "as the normal chat; only in groups"): no profile page, no small panel; groups only.
- Pro on the computer apps too (Alex, 22 Nov: "make it for the Apple desktop app"): proOk no longer leaves out window.roarDesktop (the .dmg / Windows apps and the Mac App Store app); not the iPhone app. Roardino trusts app://roarme too (themadlions-site roardino/index.html ROARME_ORIGINS), so New Ordino works inside the Mac App Store build. openFile in the computer apps opens the file's link straight in the browser. Privacy Policy (22 November 2026, both languages) says what a project keeps and who sees its admin side.
- Pro on the phone, version B (Alex, 22 Nov: "put it out everywhere, the second with the tiles"): proOk is true everywhere (website, computer apps, iPhone app). Under 960px (useNarrow) the chat and the workspace are two pages: the chat as it is with a Chat | Project switch (.prw-sw2, Project in gold) floating under its head; Project is a page over the chat (.prw-phone, a short slide in) with the switch on top, the stage, date and gear, then a home (Work phone: tab 'home' = the cover, songs, ordino, details, latest media) and tiles (.prw-tile2: Media, Files, Location Scout, Calls & Meetings, Links, Tasks, Admin or Stats, each with a one-line summary) that open their page with a back (.prw-back). A group that is not a project shows Make It a Project there. Fields in it step up by --kbh in the iPhone app. Roardino trusts capacitor://localhost too.
- PROJECTS (since 22 Nov evening PROject, and late that night PROjects, in the folder's gold, everywhere: the folder, Settings and its page title, the notices, the hub's button), the gold folder, in place of the Pro button (Alex, 22 Nov): no Pro in the bar at the foot; a fixed folder PROJECTS (PRO bold, JECTS plain, all capitals, a gold fall, .tab-gold) that cannot be deleted or renamed, last until moved (Reorder Tabs; its menu has only Mute All and Reorder). A group becomes a project from its long-press menu (Make It a Project), goes into PROJECTS (with Each Chat in One Folder it leaves Groups), and opens its workspace wherever it is opened (on a phone the Chat | Project switch); Stop Being a Project in the workspace's gear brings it back. Every other chat is the normal chat. prefs.pro is no longer read.
- A phone has version A after all (Alex, 22 Nov: "in the end I want version A"): the project page shows the same tab bar as a computer, sliding sideways (.prw-tabs.slide), the chosen tab scrolled into the middle; no home of tiles.
- The workspace's tabs are signs only, on the folders' white capsule with a soft grey lens for the chosen one (Alex, 22 Nov); the word is the button's title and label.
- The project's top row on a phone is one line (Alex, 22 Nov, a screenshot: the gear fell under and its menu opened off the screen, the date was hidden): the stage, the date (only the calendar sign before it, its name in the menu), the gear at the end; on a phone the stage's, date's and gear's menus are fixed inside the screen's edges (.prw-phone .prw-menu).
- Pro on a phone, sliding (Alex, 22 Nov: "swipe right and left to move about"; and the gear went out of the screen): on the project a slide left or right goes to the next or the previous tab, and right on Overview goes back to the chat (window events roarme-pro-swipe / roarme-pro-chat; Chat.jsx's slide-back leaves .prw-phone alone); on the chat a slide to the left that does not start on a message (there it is the reply) opens the project. The top row is a grid of three (stage, date, a 36px gear), the stage's word ending in dots, so the gear never leaves the screen; checked at 360px.
- Pro on a phone set straight (Alex, 22 Nov: "most pages spill over and slide a little to the left; the buttons, the banner"): the project page clips anything wider than the screen (overflow-x clip, min-width 0), and the tab bar no longer scrolls its parents (goTab scrolls only the bar, and only if it overflows); the eight tab signs share the bar evenly; Calls has three even buttons with the sign over a short word (Video Chat, Voice Call, Schedule); Links rows keep the link, Add to Location Scout as a round + and the bin (no name · date pill); the cover is shorter with its tools at the top and no stage chip (the stage is in the row above); task columns have no tall empty height; Who Sees Admin shows only when the group has other members. End of styles.css. Checked at 390px in the app run in Chromium: no element past the screen's edges on any tab.
- Import from Telegram (Alex, 22 Nov: "can all the chats and files come out of Telegram and into RoarMe? for all users"): the profile's More > Import from Telegram, on a computer only (a fine pointer, wider than 760px; in a group, an admin). src/tgImport.jsx (lazy) reads the folder Telegram Desktop exports (result.json in "Machine-readable JSON" and its photos, videos, voice messages, files; a whole-account export lists its chats to pick one), asks who each Telegram person is here (a member of this chat, or "Not here", which keeps the Telegram name), and brings the messages in 100 at a time with their own dates, replies kept, files into this chat's folder in roar-files (at most 50 MB each; larger or missing ones leave a line). Each Telegram message always gets the same id here (SHA-256 of room, chat, message), so a stopped import run again skips what is here. supabase/telegram-import.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_import_messages(r, rows) (a member of a direct chat or a group's admin, 200 a call, 150,000 a day in roar_import_log, only plain file keys, only paths of this chat, not during Encrypted Chat) sets roar.importing, and while it is set roar_message_stamp keeps the date and roar_touch_room, roar_push_hook and roar_autoreply_send do nothing. Every such row carries file.imported { src, by, as? }: the chat shows "imported" before the time, and a message with `as` is drawn as that person's (name over it, never as mine: Chat.jsx ghost). Imported rows are left out of the live handlers; the chat reads them when it is read again (roarme-wake). Seen in the app run in Chromium with a made-up export; not yet with a real Telegram export.
- A project's chat on a phone has a gold round button in its head, between the name's pill and the picture (Alex, 22 Nov: "the bar, the project, then the picture"): Chat.jsx .head-proj (FOOT_I.pro, shown under 960px when the chat is a project) sends roarme-pro-open, which ProPanel answers by showing the project's page. The floating Chat | Project switch over the chat is gone; the project's page keeps its switch at the top to go back.
- The name and Project are one capsule on a phone (Alex, 22 Nov, replacing the round gold button of an hour earlier: "join the title bar and Project; not the word Chat, the title and Project"): in a project's chat the name's pill ends in a Project segment (.room-head.has-proj, .head-proj, gold word); the project's page has the same capsule at its top (.prw-join: the group's name goes back to the chat, Project lit). No Chat | Project switch anywhere.
- A project's head on a phone shows only the artist (Alex, 22 Nov: "we know what is what, show only the artist's name; let the bar shrink"): Room takes proArtist (the project's Artist / Client field) and under 960px the name's pill shows it in place of the group's title (.ht-art / .ht-full; the title when the field is empty), as wide as its words, at the right beside Project and the picture. In the chat the name still opens the profile; on the project's page the same capsule's name goes back to the chat and Project is the lit half.
- The project's date on a phone (Alex, 22 Nov: "the date does not show and cannot be picked"): an empty date field has no size on an iPhone, so Work (phone) shows the date as words ("Set Shoot Date" while empty) in .prw-datewrap with the date input laid unseen over them; the date-name menu's "Your own name" field is a full-width box there.
- A project's head on a phone is the Chat | Project switch itself (Alex, 22 Nov, from its screenshot: "exactly this, same height and letters, only the first is wider with the project's name, the picture at the right"; replaces the joined capsule and the artist pill of the same day): Chat.jsx .prw-sw2.head-sw between Back and the picture (the artist, or the group's title when there is none, chosen: a tap opens the profile; Project opens the project's page); the name's pill (.head-card) is hidden there. The project's page has the same switch (.to-proj) with Project chosen and the name going back to the chat. The chosen half slides in from the other side (prw-lens-l / -r).
- On the project's page on a phone the switch reads Chat | Project (the cover names the project; in the chat's head it reads the artist | Project), and the gear stands in the top bar at the right, 44px, where the chat has the group's picture (Alex, 22 Nov); the row under it holds only the stage and the date. .prw-phone .prw-gear-wrap is laid absolutely on the page (.prw-work and .prw-top are static there).
- The project's settings are a page (Alex, 22 Nov: "the gear opens a page, not a menu, with all of it; the details' fields set from there, only the written ones on the Overview; navigation inside, back and with a swipe"): pro.jsx ProSettings covers the workspace (on a phone the whole project page, .prs2): Project Settings with Details (the fields: name, value, move up / down, delete, Add Field; Category keeps its suggestions), Stages (rename, colour, add, remove, Reset, kept as they change) and Date (its name: Shoot Date, Due Date, Delivery, Premiere or one's own; the date itself, Clear the Date) as pages of their own; Tabs (which tabs show) and Chat (Show What Went to the Side) as switches; Stop Being a Project in red. Back (or X on the first page), Escape and a slide to the right go one step back; each page slides in from its side. The Overview's Details shows only written fields, read only, with Edit (opens Settings on Details); with none written, an Add Details button. The workspace's top row lets the faces give way so the gear stays on screen on a narrower computer screen.
- The project's stage and date-name menus on a phone (Alex, 22 Nov, TestFlight: "I tap elsewhere and it stays stuck, the page under it gets selected"): inside the project's page their veil sat under the tabs and the list, so taps reached the page. Hang takes `portal` (on a phone): the veil (a light grey wash) and the menu go to document.body (.prw-portal), any tap beside the menu closes it; "Your own name" is kept when the field lets go too, not only on Enter.
- Archived in the folder bar is a sign, not a word (Alex, 22 Nov, D of the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/XLEmcfMjYRk6CTsbU7JXwN: a drawer): Chat.jsx ARCH_I in .tab-arch in place of the name; its long-press menu, Reorder and the rest still call it Archived.
- Archived is held at the folder bar's right end (Alex, 22 Nov: "always at the right, it cannot be moved, stuck on the menu; with many folders the names go behind it"): fRank puts it last whatever folderOrder says, the tab drag skips it and keeps it last, and in the bar it is position: sticky at the right with its own solid ground and a short fade on its left (styles.css end), the chip of the lens on it while chosen.
- The project settings page's boxes never squeeze when the keyboard comes up (Alex, 22 Nov, TestFlight: tapping a field folded Details into one row): .prs2-body's children have flex-shrink 0 (a flex column item with overflow hidden has no minimum height, so the box shrank when the page made room for the keyboard). Rule: in a scrolling flex column, boxes with overflow hidden get flex-shrink 0.
- Project Info, one page for everything about the project (Alex, 22 Nov: "all the project's information in one tab, filled in there and shown outside"; replaces the separate Details and Date pages of the same day): Settings > Project Info has Details (the fields), Brief, Stage (pick the current one; Edit Stages opens the stages page) and the date (its name and the date). ProSettings keeps a stack of pages, so Back from Stages returns to Project Info. The Overview has no Details box any more: the cover shows the artist, the title, the category, the stage, the date and the brief.
- What was set right on a phone, on a computer too (Alex, 22 Nov: "all of it applies to the desktop version, except the placing"): the project's date as words with the picker over them (.prw-datewrap everywhere, the date name kept beside it on one line), Calls' three even buttons, Links rows with only the link, a round + for Location Scout and the bin, no stage chip on the cover, and the chat's head showing the artist alone for a project (Room proArtist). The phone's Chat | Project switch, the gear in the top bar and the menus on the page stay phone-only (placing).
- The project's switch in a phone's chat head fills the row like any chat's name pill (Alex, 22 Nov: "fill left and right as on the other pages; the project, not the artist; the same heights as the other heads"): .head-sw is 44px with 16px / 600 letters, 8px from Back and the picture, and reads the project's Title field (else the group's name) | Project; the project page's Chat | Project switch has the same height and letters, filling the row up to the gear.
- A project has one date, the shoot day (Alex, 22 Nov: "only the shoot day, the most important for everything; take the choices out everywhere"): the top row's date pill reads Shoot Day and the date (no calendar sign, no menu of names; the year only when it is not this year), Project Info has a Shoot Day box (the date, Clear the Date) and no "the date is called" choices, and the cover's countdown says "Shoot Day in N days". DATE_NAMES and the name menu are gone; roar_pro_projects.date_label stays in the table, unread.
- What's New (Alex, 22 Nov: "after an update a small window once with what changed, in bullets, minimal, closed it does not come back; and a history in Settings"): src/whatsnew.jsx NEWS (newest first: id, date, items). WhatsNewHost (mounted in Chat beside ReportHost) shows the newest entry once per device, 1.2 s after the app opens (localStorage roarme-news-seen); Got It or X closes it. Settings has a What's New door above Sign Out (page 'news', WhatsNewPage: every entry as a box of bullets). Rule: every change people will notice adds a line to NEWS (today's entry, or a new entry with a new id on a new day), in plain English, before the TestFlight build.
- Pro's phone layout is for phones only (Alex, 22 Nov, from the Mac app: "on the desktop neither button is needed; the name's bar fills it with the group's full name as before"): every Pro breakpoint is 760px now (pro.jsx NARROW and the @media blocks in styles.css, 960 before), so a computer's narrow window keeps the workspace beside the chat, and over 760px the chat's head never shows the switch (.head-sw hidden) and shows the group's own name (Artist – Title), not the Title field.
- The workspace's faces are never cut (Alex, 22 Nov, Mac: half a face beside ⇄): on a computer .prw-top is a size container (prwtop) and under 540px the faces step out whole; they never shrink.
- The Overview gives a taste of everything (Alex, 22 Nov: "latest media much smaller, like thumbnails; under them documents, Location Scout, links and tasks as lists, small, each with All"): Latest Media as small square thumbnails (.prw-mini-grid, up to 12), then .prw-peeks, two boxes a row on a computer (one on a phone): Documents (the newest 3, open in DocView), Location Scout (3, with their status), Links (3) and Tasks (the open ones, a tap moves one on), each with its count and See All to its tab; a box shows only when it has something and its tab is not hidden.
- The project page's top row on a phone is the chat head's row (Alex, 22 Nov: "the gear one height, the bar another; check the whole navigation, the same logic and look"): Back (44, to the chat) at 10px, the Chat | Project switch from 62px, the gear 44 at the right, all at the head's top and height (measured equal to the chat's head in Chromium); the stage and date pills 40px.
- What's New grouped and with more air (Alex, 22 Nov: "more spread out, the theme's letters; group what was for the phone and what for the desktop"): NEWS entries have sections (Everywhere, On a phone, On a computer; a small accent label over each, a hairline between), the day's date a 18px title, bullets 15.5px with room, all in the app's own face (var(--font) when one is picked). The window waits until the app is in sight with no other window or the lock over it, and tries again.
- An empty Archived reads "Nothing here in Archived." (Alex, 22 Nov).
- Small questions asked of Alex on 12 to 14 Nov and never answered (kept from the first long chat, which he deleted on 22 Nov; ask again only when the thing comes up, do not act on any of them unasked): whether the name in a chat's head is sharp on his iPhone (the blur fix of 12 Nov was never confirmed; the fallback was to put the pill's blur on a layer behind the words); whether "Messages" in the foot menu should read "Chats"; 14 empty rooms named "Test NN" in roar_rooms, to delete only on his clear word; a GIPHY key for gifs, to ask for with request_credential and never in the chat.
- The Workspace (the project's pane beside the chat; Alex asked for a name for it, 22 Nov): on a computer a tap on a project chat's name pill shows or hides it (Chat.jsx proShut, localStorage roarme-pro-shut, window event roarme-pro-toggle; the pill's title says so). The chat fills the pane while it is hidden.
- The banner, calmer (Alex, 22 Nov): pro.jsx Hero shows only the project's name (Artist – Title, else the group's name) on one's own cover photo or the theme's colour; no arrows, dots or picture rounds (group photo and chat photos are no longer used as covers; cover_pick unread), no category chip, no stage chip, no brief on it (the brief lives in Project Info). The upload and remove buttons are 26px. The shoot day's countdown is a small line under the banner (.prw-when). A Location Scout status chip in the Overview's small list (.prw-mrow .prw-st) stays in its row (it had ridden up onto the banner as "Option").
- Location Scout's sign is a folded map, not a camera (Alex, 22 Nov: "the camera does not say location"): pro.jsx TAB_I.scout, used by its tab, its settings row and the empty cover / list thumbnail of a place with no photos.
- The banner's title stands in the middle, both ways (Alex, 22 Nov): .prw-hero and .prw-hero-in centre it, with room each side for the small cover buttons in the corner.
- The Workspace's tabs on a computer show a short word under each sign, as a menu (Alex, 22 Nov): pro.jsx .prw-bl (TAB_SHORT: Locations, Calls; the others their own names), 54px tall; when the Workspace is narrower than 520px the words step out (container prwtabs). A phone keeps signs only.
- A song's waveform is real and fine (Alex, 22 Nov: "what is this waveform? make it more detailed"): player.jsx finePeaks / useWave(file, n): a WAV is read straight from its samples (16 / 24 / 32-bit, float; up to 160 MB on a computer, 60 MB on a phone, no decoding), other kinds are decoded up to 40 / 20 MB; a mix of RMS and peak per bar; kept in memory and in localStorage roarme-wave-<path>|<n>, so once per song. The Workspace's song bars draw 110 thin bars (1px gaps); the big player's 56 come from the same reader (loadWave), so a long WAV is no longer a made-up shape. A file over the cap keeps the calm made-up shape.
- The Workspace's tabs in Alex's order (22 Nov): Overview, Media, Files, Location Scout, Tasks, Links, Calls & Meetings, (Polls, Contacts when there are any), Admin / Stats; the settings' Tabs list follows it (OPTIONAL). No counts anywhere on the tabs, nor on the Overview's small boxes ("leave them clean").
- The Workspace's tabs on a computer are words only, a touch bold, no signs (Alex, 22 Nov: "only letters, minimal but bold; the icons for the phone"): pro.jsx .prw-bl (TAB_SHORT), each button as wide as its word, the bar scrolls sideways if it must; a phone keeps the signs.
- Tasks as the TML Hub's (Alex, 22 Nov: "doing and done not as columns, inside each task, in list mode"): pro.jsx Board is one list (.tk-list / .tk-row): a circle marks it done; under the words tinted pills for Status (TASK_ST: To do, In progress, Blocked, Done; a select over the pill), Due date (a date input over the pill, red when overdue), Priority (Normal, High, Urgent; urgent and high rise to the top with a red or orange line at the left), who it is for (a menu of the group's members, t.who) and Subtasks (t.subs [{id, t, d}], ticked and added under the task); a tap on the words edits them; Completed folds away at the foot. Typing @ in a new task finds the group's members (arrows, Enter) and puts the chosen one on it. Old tasks ({t, s}) read as they are; 'doing' is In progress. The Overview's Tasks box shows each open task's status and due date, a tap marks it done. CSS .tk-* at the end of styles.css.
- A project linked to a TML Hub (Alex, 22 Nov: "a key from the hub, put in RoarMe, every location goes straight into the hub"; step 1 of the hub sold one day as an empty monthly product): Project Settings > Connections > TML Hub (pro.jsx ProSettings page 'hub'): a group admin pastes the hub's key (tmlhub_…, made in the hub at Settings > Integrations > Connected apps) and presses Link to the Hub; then Linked to <workspace>, how many locations are there, Send Now, Unlink. supabase/hub-link.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_hub_links (the key, readable only by the service role), roar_hub_state(r) for members. Edge Function roar-hub (deployed 22 Nov): connect (checks the key with the hub's hello), disconnect, sync (every scout as location.put to the hub's hub-bridge with photos as 10-minute signed links; one taken out of Location Scout is location.delete). kit.save calls it 1.2 s after any save that touches scouts (hubSoon, only when linked). New scout photos also get a 1600 px copy (photo.small) for the hub, whose pictures are at most 2 MB. Next agreed: tasks and budget, then both ways.
- Files and Media centred (Alex, 22 Nov): Media's All / Photos / Videos stands in the middle; Files has the same kind of menu in the middle, All then only the kinds there are (PDF, Documents, Music, Other; pro.jsx ff), a new kind joins it by itself, and a chosen kind shows only its files. What's New is wider (760px) with smaller, quieter words (14px at 72% of the ink, a small date, 5px dots).
- Production Hub (Alex, 22 Nov, replacing the per-group key of the same afternoon: "the key goes in RoarMe's Settings, call it Production Hub; every group in PROJECTS is a project of the hub; a project made in the hub makes a chat here, one made here makes one there"). Settings > Production Hub (src/hub.jsx HubPage, a door above What's New): paste the hub's key (tmlhub_…, the hub's Settings > Integrations > Connected apps), Link; then Linked to <workspace>, N projects tied, Sync Now, Unlink. supabase/hub-link.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_hub_accounts (the key per account, service role only), roar_hub_links (room -> hub_project, owner, base hashes), roar_hub_me(), roar_hub_state(r).
