Edge Function roar-hub v2 (deployed 22 Nov): connect / disconnect, linkall (my project groups tied to the hub project of the same title, en dash and hyphen alike, or a new one with the next TML code; a group made for every hub project created since I connected), link { room } (Make It a Project), sync { room } both ways: title, stage (Shooting <-> Production, the rest by name), shoot day (due <-> startDate), Category, tasks (same ids both sides; status, due, priority, subtasks, the first person by name) and locations (a scout is a location of the hub project with a library entry carrying its photos); three-way by base hashes, on the first sync the hub's values win where it has them, after that what changed wins and RoarMe's when both did. hub.jsx: hubStart (on start, at most every 10 min, linkall), hubPull (opening a project's workspace and every minute while open), hubSoon (1.2 s after a save that touches tasks, scouts, status, due or fields), hubLink. Project Settings > Production Hub shows "A project of <hub>" with Sync Now, or Add to <hub>. The hub's side: hub-bridge v2 in the hub's project (projects.list, project.get, project.create, project.put). Not built yet: Merge two projects in the hub (agreed), the members of a hub project brought into the group, photos coming from the hub into Location Scout.
- Admin's People and folds (Alex, 22 Nov: "statistics with an arrow that folds; how many times each user came in, how much each one worked in it"): supabase/project-visits.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_room_visits (room, person, day: opens, seconds in sight, last), written only by roar_visit(r, opened, add_secs) (a member for themselves; ProPanel sends an open when a project's workspace mounts and 30 s at a time while the page is in sight), read only by roar_visit_stats(r) for the group's admins and Admin's access list. pro.jsx Admin: Fold (an uppercase head with an arrow that turns, kept open or shut in localStorage roarme-pra-fold-<id>) round People (each member: last in, a bar of time in, visits, time in, messages, this week; counted since 22 Nov) and The Chat in Numbers. The Settings door that opens the messages people send to RoarMe reads Customer Service ("Messages and feedback to RoarMe · you answer as RoarMe"). The Privacy Policy says so (both languages).
- What's New as B (Alex, 22 Nov, from the sample https://claude.ai/artifact/AU8jUvGrMNKYDnFRb4ijGD): whatsnew.jsx draws each day as a quiet timeline (.wn-tl: a fine line, a small accent dot per change, the words before ": " in bold when they are short, the rest at .62), the date as "22 November" with the year at the right, and All / Phone / Computer on a small capsule (.wn-seg; items keep their section, Everywhere is All only); the window shows the capsule only when the day has more than one kind. The song's waveform in the Workspace (Overview and Files) is 1 of the same sample: pro.jsx FineWave, a canvas at the screen's sharpness, 1px bars every 2px mirrored round the middle, the played part in the accent's pair and the rest the ink at .32, from useWave(file, 240). The big player keeps its own.
- Settings > PROJECTS (Alex, 22 Nov: "in Settings the tab is the gold Projects, and inside it the hub you join, and the subscription, on a phone and a computer"): the Production Hub door became a gold PROJECTS door (SettingsPages.jsx .set-proj: the briefcase in gold, PRO bold and JECTS in the folder's gold, "Production Hub" under it), page 'projects' (old links to 'hub' land there too), titled Projects: hub.jsx ProjectsPage with a Production Hub section (HubPage). The plan / subscription joins this page once its terms are agreed with Alex.
- Create PROject in the hub (Alex, 22 Nov: "in the hub, on the projects we already have and are active, a button Create PROject that makes the group in RoarMe"): the hub's project Overview (ov-acts) shows Create PROject on an active project (not Delivered) while the workspace has a used Connected-apps key (hub_has_app()); pressing it writes data.roarme { request } in the project; Creating PROject… while it waits; In RoarMe once tied. roar-hub (v3, deployed with verify_jwt off: it checks the sign-in or the Vault secret itself) answers pg_cron's 'roar-hub-pull' every minute (supabase/hub-link.sql, end; only while someone is linked): for every linked account, a group (the key's holder its admin) for each hub project that asked for one or was made there since linking, tied and synced at once (makeGroup / wanted). The Settings door reads PROject (PRO bold, ject plain). Members of the hub project are not brought into the group yet.
- Production Hub live both ways (Alex, 22 Nov: "if I delete something in the hub it goes in RoarMe and the other way round"): roar-hub v4's minute cron also syncs every tied project whose hub updatedAt differs from the last sync (base.hu), so a change in the hub reaches RoarMe within a minute even with RoarMe closed; a change in RoarMe reaches the hub about 1.2 s after its save (hubSoon). Tasks and locations taken away on one side go on the other (three-way merge). A whole project deleted in the hub does not delete the group: its link gets "This project is not in the hub any more." (ask Alex before changing that).
- A project deleted in the hub deletes its group too (Alex, 22 Nov, "B"): roar-hub v5's minute cron, for a tied project missing from projects.list, asks project.get for that very id and only on the hub's 404 deletes the roar_rooms row (kind group) and its link. Settings > PROject opens with Plan: "PROject is free for now" and a Coming soon pill (Alex: "free, we see the subscription later"); nothing is locked. Deleting a group in RoarMe does not delete the hub project.
- A group the Production Hub makes shows as a project at once (Alex, 22 Nov: "Create PROject made a plain group, not in the PROject folder"): the row in roar_pro_projects was there, but the app read projects only at start and by Realtime, which a phone in the background misses. Chat.jsx loadRooms now reads the projects again whenever a chat new to the list appears (seenRooms), and pro.jsx useProjects reads them again when RoarMe comes back to the front.
- From the hub, shown in RoarMe (Alex, 22 Nov: "the project's photo from the hub; the locations' photos do not show; where is the song? they must talk"): hub-bridge v5 project.get returns cover (coverThumb), music (each track with a link good for a day: a pCloud song as a signed address of the hub's pcloud function, made with the same PCLOUD_FULL_TOKEN key; a stored one from the audio bucket) and libPhotos (the library's photos for a location with none of its own). roar-hub v6 keeps roar_pro_projects.hub_cover and hub_music (supabase/hub-link.sql end, run 22 Nov; a song's link is renewed when under six hours are left, at every sync) and gives a place from the hub its photos as { url } (its public thumbnails) unless RoarMe has photos of its own. lib.js picLink / fileLink answer a file's url when it has no path; player.jsx uses a file's own peaks; pro.jsx Hero uses hub_cover when there is no cover of one's own, and the hub's songs stand first in Music and on the Overview ("From the Hub", no Send to or bin).
- Unlink in place of delete, and Archived Projects (Alex, 22 Nov: "deleted in the hub, not deleted in RoarMe, and back: an unlink; an Unlink button on both; Delivered on both, and here a folder of Archived Projects", then "B"). roar-hub v7: a project deleted in the hub (its 404) or unlinked there (data.roarme.unlinked, hub-bridge v6) only unties the group (untie: roar_hub_links keeps its row with hub_project null, so linkall never ties it back by name; Add to the hub ties it again); action unlink (Project Settings > Production Hub > Unlink, admins or who tied it; also Stop Being a Project, through useProjects remove) marks the hub's project unlinked; a group deleted here leaves its hub project marked unlinked; wanted() never makes a group for an unlinked project. roar_hub_state answers only for a tied group (supabase/hub-link.sql end, run 22 Nov). The hub's Overview has Unlink beside In RoarMe. Delivered already follows both ways through the stage; pro.jsx isDelivered (the stage named Delivered, or 'done'), and Chat.jsx shows PROject's delivered projects under a folding "Archived Projects" row at the foot of the list (.proj-arch, localStorage roarme-proj-arch).
- PROjects has an on / off switch, off to start (Alex, 22 Nov: "normally it will not be open for users; whoever wants turns it on and the folder shows"; RoarMe only): Settings > PROjects opens with the switch (hub.jsx ProjectsPage; Plan and Production Hub show only while on). roar_profiles.pro_on (supabase/hub-link.sql end, run 22 Nov; on once for whoever had a Production Hub or ran a project group, which was Alex alone), kept on the device in localStorage roarme-pro-on (proSwitchNow / proSwitchLoad / proSwitchSet, window event roarme-pro-switch). Chat.jsx proOn = proOk() && the switch: off, no PROjects folder, no Make It a Project, project groups open as plain chats (no Workspace, no switch in the head), hubStart does not run; nothing is deleted.
- A project group's picture is the hub's cover (Alex, 22 Nov: "the group's picture in PROjects did not take the project's picture from the hub"): roar-hub v8 sets roar_rooms.avatar_url to the hub's cover when the group has no picture, or still has the hub's previous cover; a picture given to the group by hand stays.
- The banner's tools are one settings button (Alex, 22 Nov: "not three buttons, one settings with a small menu, plus reposition, crop"): pro.jsx Hero, a gear opening .menu.prw-hero-menu with Change Photo, Reposition (drag the picture, Cancel / Done; roar_pro_projects.cover_pos { x, y } as background-position %, supabase/hub-link.sql end, run 22 Nov), Crop (the picture there is now, one's own or the hub's, fetched into CropModal) and Remove (cover 'none' when the hub has a cover, so the hub's does not come back; Use the Hub's Picture brings it back). A group's picture made from the hub's cover is copied into roar-avatars (roar_room_avatar_own allows nothing else) by roar-hub v9. The location's status chip is absolute only inside a card's cover (.prw-cover > .prw-st).
- Development and Delivered are not picked as stages (Alex, 22 Nov: "take Development and Delivered out of here", then "a Mark as Delivered button"): pro.jsx pickStages leaves them out of Project Info's Stage list and the top row's stage menu (they stay in the data and Edit Stages, so the hub's statuses still map); under the stages a Mark as Delivered row (Project Info and the stage menu) sets the Delivered stage, which moves the project to Archived Projects, and Back to Active returns it to the last pickable stage.
- Appearance > Colour on a computer (Alex, 22 Nov: "spread them on the desktop, and many more, just as basic"): the dots spread up to 620px wide and 30 more plain colours (SettingsPages.jsx MORE_COLOURS, set as Your Colour with that hex) show only with a fine pointer over 760px (.acc-more); a phone keeps its own grid.
- How RoarMe starts and what Reset Appearance goes back to, 22 Nov (Alex, a screenshot of his Chats tab: "these settings as I send them, at first start and on reset"): Message Text Size 13.5 px (DEFAULTS.textSize 1), Message Corners 13 px (bubbleR 13), Compact off; the rest as 21 Nov's. A saved choice is kept.
- 22 Nov, late: (1) Light mode words measured on every screen with a contrast check in Chromium (phone and computer) and fixed in the last block of styles.css: --muted #61616d by Day, coloured words (senders' names, stage chips, the chosen foot tab, ordino label) take a deeper form of their colour, faded hints keep full opacity; what remains under 3:1 is white on accent buttons and words on photos. (2) Project Settings shows Project Info's details, brief, stage, Mark as Delivered and shoot day on its first page; there is no Project Info page. (3) The banner's menu is portalled to the page (the banner clipped it) and Reposition's Done works (the drag no longer captures taps on the tools).
- Rule (Alex, 22 Nov: "remember, wherever centring is needed, it is in the centre"): rows of choices, chips, captions and short lines that stand alone on a page are centred; a list keeps its left edge.
- A PROjects group is three chats (Alex, 22 Nov: "three chat tabs only, PRE PRODUCTION - PRODUCTION - POST PRODUCTION, each seen by different people of the group, set by the chat's admins; only pre production sees the Workspace; the others only the chat and what we send there"; admins see all; a new member starts in PRODUCTION; older messages are PRE PRODUCTION; straight into the app). supabase/project-channels.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_messages.channel ('pre' | 'prod' | 'post', null = pre), roar_members.channels (null = prod) and cur_channel; roar_sees(u, r, ch) (true for a group that is not a project; admins all); a restrictive select and insert policy on roar_messages; a before-insert trigger that puts a message with no channel into its sender's open tab (cur_channel), so none of the 16 places that send needs to know; roar_set_channels (admins); the Workspace's row (roar_pro_projects) only for admins and PRE PRODUCTION (restrictive policy), so the others get a plain chat; roar_my_rooms (redefined, now also project and channels), roar_push_targets and roar_badge_counts count and tell only what one may see. Chat.jsx Room: CHANS, a minimal centred row of three words under the head (.ch-tabs: no frame, the chosen one in the words' colour with a small accent dot; only when one has more than one tab), filters the messages by tab and writes cur_channel when a tab is chosen (localStorage roarme-chan-<room>). Profile.jsx: beside each member who is not an admin, Pre · Prod · Post chips for the admins (.pf-chans). The PROjects folder also knows a project by roar_my_rooms.project.
- Project Settings, gathered (Alex, 22 Nov: "take Budget, Add Field and the brief out, Edit Stages out, Mark as Delivered higher, gathered, one page"): Mark as Delivered first; Details are Artist / Client, Title and Category in one line of three cells (MAIN_FIELDS; stacked on a narrow screen); the Stage as three chips; the shoot day with a small Clear; Tabs as chips (struck through when hidden); More holds Production Hub, Show What Went to the Side and Stop Being a Project. New projects start with no Budget field.
- The project's three chats look as version C (Alex, 22 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/FREKbL9q4EFFH4kGUP81Zn): words only, the chosen one in the words' colour with a soft glow of its stage colour behind and a short line of it under (Pre #d48806, Production #e5483f, Post #7b4dff; CHANS in Chat.jsx), unread of the other tabs as a small number (counted from the loaded messages since last_read_at). End of styles.css.
- The project's three chats are version A after all (Alex, 22 Nov: "in the end this one is better"): a glass capsule (Lens with GLASS TRAVEL, .ch-seg) with a dot of each chat's stage colour, the chosen one on the travelling lens, unread of the others as a small number. Replaces version C of an hour earlier.
- Who Sees Which Chat is in Project Settings too (Alex, 22 Nov, desktop: "I still have not found the members list"; on a computer a tap on a project's name shows or hides the Workspace, so the profile is not reached there): pro.jsx MembersChats, every member with Pre · Prod · Post (admins shown with all three, not changeable), "Sees the Workspace" / "Chat only"; only the group's admins change them (roar_set_channels).
- Project Settings > Who Sees Which Chat ends with Add People (Alex, 22 Nov: "and how do I add people"), which opens the group's own page (window event roarme-profile) with its Members and Add people; a new member starts in PRODUCTION until an admin changes it.
- Project chats on a computer (Alex, 22 Nov: "if the bar opening the window on the right is such a problem, put it back and a round button"): the name's pill opens the profile again; a round gold briefcase (.head-ws, Room wsOpen) beside the picture shows or hides the Workspace (roarme-pro-toggle, lit gold while open). Add People in Project Settings closes the settings first.
- Add people in a group (Profile.jsx) suggests the people I have direct chats with, from the first letter (the last talked to first; with nothing typed, the latest eight), the server's search joining from the second letter; the field has autocomplete off and no "username" in its placeholder, since Safari offered the saved login there (Alex, 22 Nov).
- Hub Member (Alex, 22 Nov): whoever linked a Production Hub (roar_hub_accounts) sees on a RoarMe person's card (ContactCard, and BookCard for a linked entry) hub.jsx HubMemberBox: a Hub Member switch and, once on, the category Pre / Production / Post (several allowed). supabase/hub-members.sql (run 22 Nov): roar_hub_members (owner, member, channels; RLS owner only, inserting needs the owner's own hub), a new member gets pro_on true; joining a project group (roar_members insert with no channels) or a group made a project (roar_pro_projects insert) puts them in the category's chats of the hub of one of the group's admins (roar_hub_channels_for). Members belong to one owner's hub; another company cannot see or set them.
- The project's banner is off to start (Alex, 22 Nov): Project Settings > More > Banner (prefs.proBanner, false in DEFAULTS); off, the Overview starts with the shoot day's line. The Workspace button in a project chat's head is round glass with a panel sign (Safari's sidebar), no colour (Alex, 22 Nov); its open state is the class ws-open, never .on (which paints the accent).
- In a project chat on a computer the Workspace button stands at the left of the name's pill and the group's picture at its right (Alex, 22 Nov; CSS order on .room-head.has-proj, end of styles.css).
- The shoot day's countdown (in N days, today, tomorrow, N days ago; pro.jsx dueIn) stands right of the date in the Workspace's top row, not under the banner, and the date is 15px (Alex, 22 Nov).
- Send from the Workspace (Alex, 22 Nov: "the classic share arrow on all of them"): pro.jsx shareBtn on media tiles (on the picture, beside the bin), files, song bars, places and map links, links, contacts, Location Scout cards and rows, and the ordino bar (its Send to This Chat / Send to… became one Send to…). ShareMenu (portalled, fixed beside the arrow, .prw-share-menu with --sl / --st / --sb): the project's chats the sender may write to (all three for an admin, else room.channels; This Chat when the group has no channels), then Another Chat or Person… (SendPick). sendAny: a message copied (its file copied into another chat's folder, imported dropped), a link as text, a scout as text with its address and map link and a location pin when it has lat / lon, the ordino as before; a pick of one of the three chats inserts with that channel.
- The Overview's Location Scout list: ScoutPhoto is a plain span when it has no onClick (a button inside the row's button broke the layout and threw the status chip out of the box, Alex, 22 Nov).
- Hub photos large (Alex, 22 Nov: "the photos are very small"): hub-bridge (v7) project.get adds photoLinks { photoId: pCloud link } valid to the end of the next 30-day window (so it changes once a month, not every sync); roar-hub (v10) gives each hub photo url = that link and thumb = the hub's 320px thumbnail; ScoutPhoto shows the thumb, the viewer the url, and the viewer fills the stage whatever the picture's size.
- Hub Member is also on a person's profile page, under the username (Profile.jsx), and its categories stand on one line.

- From the Workspace a hub song is sent by fetching its day-long link and uploading the sound into the chosen chat's folder; Another Chat or Person… opens SendPick through a portal (inside the phone's project page it opened under it, Alex, 22 Nov).
- The hub's published ordinos (Roardino in the hub, roardino_ordinos with project_id) come with hub-bridge v8 project.get as ordinos (the crew's link go.themadlions.com/?o/<token>, label, title, artist, date, call, place, accent) and roar-hub v11 keeps them in roar_pro_projects.hub_ordinos (supabase/hub-link.sql end); the Workspace's ordino bar shows them first, marked From the Hub, with View and Send to… only. Add Ordino Link pastes any ordino's link into the project's own list (Alex, 22 Nov).
- The Workspace's background (Project Settings > More > Background; Alex, 22 Nov): Upload / Change (CropModal 4:5, roar-avatars/<me>/wsbg-<room>-*.jpg), Crop again, Remove, and Opacity (5 to 100, 40 to start); roar_pro_projects.ws_bg / ws_bg_op, so everyone who sees the Workspace sees it; .prw-work.has-bg::before draws it under the glass.

The Apple association file leaves /app and /app/* on the web. The iPhone and Mac App Store builds carry the app at their own root, untouched. Notifications are unchanged (Apple shows no picture for a group without a photo). lib.js initialsOf reads names in RoarMe's bold / italic letters and with under / strike lines as plain letters (NFKD, first letter or digit of each word), so a group named in bold no longer shows a broken "?" box. CSS at the end of styles.css.
- A transfer going up (Alex, 24 Nov: 580 KB took 46 s, the ring stood at 0%, the buttons sat to the right): transfer.jsx creep moves the ring toward 95% while the phone reports nothing (the iPhone app gives upload progress only now and then), never more than 60 points past the real share; Stop and Keep Chatting are .rt-up-acts, an even centred pair on one line (the transfers list's .rt-acts rule had pushed them right). r2upload.js put has a stall watchdog: a piece silent for 6 s (or size / 100 KB/s for a big one) is aborted and tried again at once; each file's complete call carries ?kb=&ms=&retries= in its address, so the function's edge log shows how long it took (query function_edge_logs for roartransfer-r2).
- If a pull request's build check never starts (24 Nov: GitHub dropped the opened event of #785), push the next real change to the branch; never an empty commit, never close and reopen the PR, never merge without the green build.
- A transfer's pieces are read into memory before they go up (Alex, 24 Nov: IMG_8041.png failed six times as "The connection dropped" in about 90 s, nothing reached R2): r2upload.js reads each 16 MB piece with arrayBuffer() and sends the bytes; a file that cannot be read stops at once with "Couldn't read the file. Pick it again." When a file gives up, its abort call carries ?fail=read|net|stall|httpNNN|other&kb=&ms=&retries= (query function_edge_logs for roartransfer-r2).
- A group with no photo wears a Glass Orb, in place of the aurora of 23 Nov (Alex, 24 Nov, his screenshot of the Glass Orb sample: "these fit better"): ui.jsx Avatar .av-aura: a radial fall from --g1 (light, top left) through --g2 to --g3 (deep), a frosted sheen (.aura-a) and a top light (.aura-b), a fine bright rim, the people sign (AURA_I) in white straight on it at 44% of the size (.aura-ic, no chip); AURA holds eight trios picked by the name. No drift any more. End of styles.css.
- Archived is a word again in the folder bar (Alex, 24 Nov: "take it out, as it was before, that just said Archived"): no binders sign (ARCH_I gone), no hairline, not held over the bar's right end (showTab leaves no room for it), the lens on it when chosen; it still stands last (fRank). The 22 and 23 Nov notes on its sign and sticky place are history. Last block of styles.css.

The height moves (not a transform), so the bar, the caption and Send stay at the foot.
