The head: X at the left; on Media with the library, the album's name with an arrow (Recents ⌄, .asx-albs menu: Recents, Photos, Videos, the smart albums, one's own; LibraryGrid takes cat / onCat / onAlbums and then shows no chips). The + no longer opens the camera first (cameraFirst is ignored). The bar is one frosted capsule (blur 24px, white .74 / grey .74 by Night, 36px corners). End of styles.css. Seen in the app run in Chromium at 390px, Day and Night; the drag with a finger and the live camera square not yet on a phone.
- A group with no photo wears the users' own round (Alex, 24 Nov, from App Review's AR: "keep the design of the users' button, only put the icon you put in the groups"; replaces the Glass Orb of the same day): ui.jsx Avatar with group and no src is .av.av-init.av-gico with the name's INIT_PAIRS colours and the grain (themes-glass.css .av-init), and AURA_I in white at 48% of the size (.av-gi) in place of the letters. AURA / auraOf and the orb CSS are gone. End of styles.css.
- Settings > Chat Folders on a phone (Alex, 24 Nov: "the folders do not drag up and down"): the row has touch-action pan-y, so the iPhone took a vertical slide as the page's scroll and cancelled the drag. SettingsPages.jsx Folders: with a finger a folder moves only from its grip (.fold-grip, touch-action none, a 34 x 44 place to press), and a non-passive touchmove holds the page still while it drags; a vertical slide that starts elsewhere on the row is left to the page; the slide to the left for Delete is unchanged; a mouse drags from anywhere as before.
- An empty folder of one's own (Alex, 24 Nov, C of https://claude.ai/artifact/GAKLEmbkT2TYJx7eEF8nFP, "just more minimal"): Chat.jsx shows .fold-empty in place of the old line: our own glass folder drawing (FOLD_EMPTY, 64px, no motion), "No chats in this folder yet." in the muted colour, and two small pills side by side, Add Chats in the accent's pair (editFolder(id, [], true): Edit Folder opens with its Included chats picker already open, kit.pickNow) and Edit Folder in glass. Archived, Unread and the rest keep their plain line. End of styles.css.
- What's New's All / Phone / Computer by Night (Alex, 24 Nov): the chosen one was white words on the light card; now a 14% white wash with white words (styles.css end, with the same html:root:root:root:root weight as the Day rule).
- The empty screens' buttons as version B (Alex, 24 Nov, https://claude.ai/artifact/KX46mUwE4VRcwnCiwZeKns: "more minimal, no colour, smaller, all the same size"): Pick a chat (New chat, New group, Invite; "Invite people" shortened to fit) and the empty folder (Add Chats, Edit Folder with a pen, FE_PEN) are 104 x 30 pills with no fill, a hairline of the words' colour at 12%, a 6% wash on hover and a press. Last block of styles.css.
- The computer's two panels without the white line (Alex, 24 Nov, B of https://claude.ai/artifact/QNDzifXKy2mfJGYqYhv4Pz: "glassy shine, not a white line"): over 760px .app > .side and .main have a transparent border, light pooled inside their edges (inset shadows) and a soft sheen from the top left (background-image), by Day and Night. Last block of themes-glass.css.
- Pick a chat's brand (Alex, 24 Nov): no mark over ROARME there, "by THEMADLIONS" 13px (10px elsewhere) and 6px closer under it (.pick-mark, last block of styles.css). The sign-in page, lock and Settings' foot keep their mark.
- Empty screens, version B of https://claude.ai/artifact/PQWXfWDRF6CxeHFanGGQNG (Alex, 24 Nov, after he turned down the hairline pills and the folder drawing): an empty folder of one's own shows only "This folder is empty." and one glass pill, Add Chats (no drawing, no Edit Folder; FOLD_EMPTY and FE_PEN are gone); Pick a chat shows only New chat (New group and Invite stay in the head's +). The pill: 34px, clear glass with light inside its edges and a soft shadow, no colour, Day and Night. Last block of styles.css.
- The computer's welcome screen (no chat open) is only ROARME and "by THEMADLIONS" (Alex, 24 Nov: "take them out completely"): no line, no buttons (.pick has only .pick-mark). New chat, New group and Invite are in the list head's +.
- A folder's Included chats window (Alex, 24 Nov: "the search very thin; check its colours light and dark, the window as all the others"): SettingsPages.jsx FolderEdit's picker uses the app's search pill (label.search with I.search, "Search chats", 44px) as the other windows do; the empty tick circles have a lighter ring by Night. The window itself was already the shared Modal glass.
- A profile no longer slides sideways on a phone (Alex, 24 Nov: "it spills over"): .pf-dock::before reached 16px past the dock while the page's padding is 14px, so the page was 2px wider than the screen and the iPhone let it drift; it now reaches 14px. In a project group the Pre · Prod · Post chips go on their own line under the member's name on a phone (.pf-row:has(> .pf-chans) wraps), so names are no longer squeezed to nothing. End of styles.css.
- RoarMail inside RoarMe, step 1 (Alex, 24 Nov, from https://claude.ai/artifact/Yb79GqDnoCkSXzMy7AVyGP: "yes, this; start with the hidden switch"). src/mail.jsx: Settings has a gold ROARMAIL door (MailWord, "Only you see this") shown only to inThemeLab(me); its page MailPage is one switch kept on the device (localStorage roarme-mail-on, window event roarme-mail-switch). On, Chat.jsx (mailOn) puts a fixed folder 'mail' (ROAR gold, MAIL thin, a mail sign) after PROjects in the folder bar; no chat fits it, it is not offered in Add to Folder or Settings > Chat Folders, and MailView takes the list's place: RoarMail's accounts as round pictures (All, then each account in its colour, read from roarmail_sync, the setup the RoarMail computer app keeps in the Roar Account; never mail or passwords), Inbox ▾ and the filters (search, unread, starred, attachments, conversations, promotions) as RoarMe pills and round buttons, and a quiet line until mail can be read on the device. Next: the mail engine (a native part in the iPhone app, RoarMail's engine in the Mac app), then the list, a mail page, folders and the reply window as in the sample. CSS .rmx-* at the end of styles.css. Seen in the app run in Chromium (phone, Day and Night).
- ROARMAIL kept on the phone (Alex, 24 Nov: "the mail should be on the phone, at least the latest, it is very slow"): cache.js keepKv / readKv / keptKv / dropKv (IndexedDB roarme-data, a memory copy, cleared on sign-out); mail.jsx keeps the setup, which accounts are in RoarMe and the counts in localStorage (mail-setup, mail-on, mail-counts, mail-fcounts), each list's newest 60 (ml:<key>) and every mail opened (mm:<account>|<path>|<uid>, the newest 150, list mail-kept). Kept things show at once and the cloud's answer replaces them; after a list loads its newest 8 mails are fetched ahead one by one with seen false (fetchAhead), so they open at once; a kept mail opened unread is marked read on the server quietly. Proved in the app run in Chromium: opening a fetched-ahead mail made only the flag call.
- ROARMAIL's pages never stand sideways (Alex, 24 Nov: "most pages spill out, make them steady"): the iPhone cancels a touch once it scrolls the page, and useSlide never heard of it, so a page was left shifted; now onTouchCancel (and the mail frame's touchcancel) puts it back, every touch starts from zero, and .rmx is touch-action pan-y with no sideways overflow (end of styles.css). The unified Unread row of the same evening is gone (Alex meant the app's own Unread).
- The bar over the mails is one slim capsule of liquid glass (Alex, 24 Nov, B of https://claude.ai/artifact/BWSMApRMEgeGjPpgRXjeut): mail.jsx .rmx-head holds Back, the folder's name and the four filters (bare signs, the chosen one on the --lg-chip in --halo). End of styles.css.
- An open mail runs edge to edge on a phone (Alex, 24 Nov: "the mail's body across the whole screen, no gaps left and right"): .rmx-rd has no side room or corners, the frame's body no padding for an HTML mail (14px 16px for plain text), and while it is open .side:has(.rmx-rd) drops its 12px and the pager's 4px, giving the head (12px), the folder bar (.tabs 17px) and the foot (20px) their own places back. End of styles.css.
- Unread on every folder in ROARMAIL (Alex, 24 Nov: "the unread numbers on the folders"): mail.jsx fcounts, asked from roarmail-cloud counts 30 folders at a time when a folders page opens; an account's every folder shows its own, All Inboxes' rows the sum of the accounts' folders of that kind.
- Slides in ROARMAIL (Alex, 24 Nov: "swipe right and left in all the mail"): mail.jsx useSlide on the folders, the mails and an open mail: right one step back (mail > mails > folders > accounts), left one step on (back into what was just left), and in an open mail left is the next mail; the page follows the finger and springs back if let go early (80px or a flick). The mail's sealed frame hands its touches over (fit). On the accounts page the list's own slide still turns the folders; elsewhere Chat.jsx's pager leaves .rmx alone.
- A mail's row in ROARMAIL keeps its own three lines, left-aligned, whatever Show Last Message says (Alex, 24 Nov: "everything mixed up"; .rooms.no-prev had made it a grid): the last block of styles.css on .rmx .room.
- ROARMAIL stands in Settings > Chat Folders as PROJECTS does (its gold word and the mail sign), so it can be moved like the others (Alex, 24 Nov: "why does it not show here").
- RoarMail inside RoarMe is for a phone only (Alex, 24 Nov: "only for the phone; on the desktop there is the app itself"): mail.jsx mailHere() (push.js onPhone) gates the Settings door, its page and the folder; a computer, the web on a computer and the Mac apps never show it.
- RoarMail inside RoarMe, step 2 (Alex, 24 Nov, from https://claude.ai/artifact/SodwiHjKHvXDTjZmKjTTAU: "Μ' αρέσει, ξεκίνα", and "pass the mail through the cloud too"): the ROARMAIL folder is three steps, as RoarMail on the Mac: the accounts (All Inboxes, then each account with its picture and address and the Inbox's unread count), an account's folders (Inbox, Drafts, Sent, Junk, Trash, Archive, then its own under Folders; for All Inboxes, All Inboxes / Drafts / Sent… across the accounts), the folder's mails (filters: search, unread, starred, with files; Older Mail; in All Inboxes a bar of the account's colour), and a mail opens over them (from, to, the mail in a sealed frame: no scripts, nothing from outside until Show Images; files that download or go to the share sheet; Mark Unread, Star, Archive, Delete). src/mail.jsx, CSS .rmx-* at the end of styles.css. Accounts, pictures and folders come from roarmail_sync; the mail through RoarMe's Edge Function roarmail-cloud (source in the roarmail repository: supabase/functions/roarmail-cloud, supabase/roarmail-cloud.sql, run 24 Nov): RoarMail 0.39.0's Settings > Roar Account > My mail in RoarMe > Send to RoarMe hands an account's password (or Microsoft refresh token) to it, it checks it over IMAP and keeps it in Supabase Vault (roarmail_cloud: owner reads, never the secret); actions state, connect, disconnect, counts, list (30 a page), get, file, flag, move, delete; nothing of the mail is stored. Privacy Policy says so (both languages). Still to come: reply and new mail (SMTP; Outlook's 587 may be closed to Edge Functions), and a phone's slide back.
- ROARMAIL, round two (Alex, 24 Nov: "build all these and put them on TestFlight"). src/mail.jsx: a mail slid from the screen's right edge (EDGE 72px, or a row already open) brings out Read / Unread, Star, Move, Block and Trash as capsules of colour with their word under them (MailRow, .rmx-sw / .rmx-swacts); past a third they stay out until a tap, a touch on another row puts them away first, and the touch never reaches the page's own slide (stopPropagation). A long press (460 ms) opens MailMenu (Reply, Reply All, Forward, Mark as Read / Unread, Star, Move To, Archive, All From This Sender, Block Sender, Delete), the reader's ⋯ too. New Mail: a round accent button sticky at the list's foot on every step but an open mail; Compose (a Modal: From among the accounts in RoarMe, To, Cc Bcc, Subject, the text, files up to 18 MB, the account's signature from RoarMail's identities); Reply / Reply All / Forward from the reader's bar and the menus (a forward carries the original's files). Filters version A of https://claude.ai/artifact/Nmb2XBaCTYcECN2sYubCsL: the name on its line, under it one glass bar as tall as the folder bar with Search, Unread, Starred, Attachments, Conversations, Day (the phone's date picker), Oldest first, Same sender; Unread, Starred, Day, Same sender and words are searched on the server (find), Attachments and Conversations on the loaded mails. Trash and Junk have Empty Trash / Empty Junk. A sender in the address book (or RoarMail's contacts) shows with that name and photo (useWho). In the background (useMailUnread from Chat, warmMail: at start, every 3 minutes and back in front) the counts, every Inbox's newest 30 and the unread are fetched and kept, and the newest mails fetched ahead. The app's Unread counts chats plus mails (uCount + mailUn) and shows the unread mails under the chats (MailUnread, version A); a tap opens it in ROARMAIL. Settings > ROARMAIL: the switch, the accounts and whether each is in RoarMe, Reading (Mark as Read When Opened, Load Images, Preview Lines, Oldest First), Actions (Ask Before Deleting / Blocking), Writing (Add My Signature), On This Phone (Kept Mail, Clear), kept in localStorage roarme-mail-prefs. roarmail-cloud v5 (source in the roarmail repository): send (SMTP on 465, since Supabase closes 25 and 587; a copy appended to Sent except on Gmail / Outlook, the answered mail flagged), empty, block (the address and the name into roarmail_sync's contacts / block_rules with device 'roarme', so RoarMail on the computers takes it in; the mail to Junk), list with find { unseen, flagged, from, on, text }. Outlook accounts may not send from the cloud (no 465 there): the error says so. Seen in the app run in Chromium at 390px; not yet on a phone.
- ROARMAIL, corrected to Alex's choices (24 Nov, angry: "I told you B, twice"; his "1. Β 2. ..." answered the filters sample, not the bar's): the filters are version B of https://claude.ai/artifact/Nmb2XBaCTYcECN2sYubCsL, one glass capsule as wide and as tall as the folder bar, back and the folder's name at the left, the eight filters at the right, sliding sideways (end of styles.css). Unread is version B: the unread mails stand among the unread chats by time (Chat.jsx, folder 'unread': shown rooms by last_message_at and mail.jsx useUnreadMails rows by date, each mail an UnreadMail: MailRow in a .rmx.rmx-mu1 with its slide and long press; what needs the folder opens it in ROARMAIL), and the head holds All · Chats · Mail in ROARME's place, as Calls' All | Missed (uKind, .u-kind), so no line of its own. Read the answer to each question against the sample it answers before building.
- What's New sections can be lab: true (whatsnew.jsx newsLab, set by Chat from inThemeLab): only who sees what is being built sees them, and an entry with nothing for someone else is skipped for them (newest()). RoarMail's changes go there, under RoarMail (Alex, 24 Nov: "nothing goes into What's New").
- RoarMail is not in RoarMe on a computer (mailHere is onPhone; Alex, 24 Nov). He offered, as a choice, a ROARMAIL place on the computer that only says to download the RoarMail app; not built, asked.
- The hub's Calendar in a PROject's Overview, above the Team (Alex, 25 Nov: "exactly the TML Hub's calendar, its calendars and functions, talking to it, the phone's form on both"; he chose: the whole hub calendar, each person as their own hub account sees it, the same layout in RoarMe's glass). src/hubCal.jsx HubCalendar (only for a group tied to a hub, hubState): the hub's phone layout (type menu All types + feeds, ⊘ Not available, + Add event; the month card with round arrows, letters, up to three dots, today in the accent pair, the picked day ringed, Greek holidays red; the picked day's events as tinted cards; the same event window: Title or Who is not available, Type, Project, Date / All day or From / To, Start / End, Location with the Maps button, Notes, delete). src/hubCalLib.js is a copy of the hub's src/lib/dates.js and the parsing half of src/lib/ical.js (keep in step). roar-hub v13 cal.get / cal.put / cal.delete { room } -> hub-bridge v11 cal.* with the caller's RoarMe email: the hub's types and calendarAccess, project access, own days off (an admin's anyone's), the hub's calendar feeds (their .ics texts fetched by hub-bridge when asked, at most every 30 minutes from RoarMe, kept in IndexedDB hcal:<room>:f); no hub account: that project's events only, read-only. Writes go to the hub's events table as its Calendar writes them (createdBy, personName). Google Calendar: a google-type event changed here reaches Google only when the hub syncs. Seen in the app run in Chromium (phone and computer) with a stubbed hub; not on a phone.
- Make the Ordino's + opens a small menu, Make the Ordino or Add Ordino Link (Alex, 25 Nov: "not a line of its own"); pro.jsx ordMenu, portalled .menu.prw-ordmenu.
- The call buttons on a PROject's Overview (Alex, 25 Nov: "under the big menu, about its width, centred, thinner"): pro.jsx CallStrip, three even pills (Video Chat in the accent's pair, Voice Call, Schedule) in .prw-callwrap right under the Workspace's tabs, as wide as the tab bar, 32px tall (30 on a phone; the bar is about 52); the same actions as the Calls tab's buttons, which stay. Not for view-only members. End of styles.css.
- No Calls tab in the Workspace (Alex, 25 Nov, on a phone and a computer: its three buttons are the Overview's CallStrip): pro.jsx TABS and OPTIONAL lost 'calls', the Calls component and its lists are gone; a group call's card (file.groupcall) now stays in the chat like a scheduled meeting (isAsset no longer takes it). Rule from Alex the same day: what he asks for holds on a phone and a computer alike unless he names one.
- One Workspace background for all projects (Alex, 25 Nov: "the one on Γιώργος Σαμπάνης on every project, now and to come; a setting for all, and change one if you want"): supabase/ws-bg-all.sql (run 25 Nov) roar_profiles.ws_bg_all / ws_bg_all_op; pro.jsx useWsAll / wsAllSet / wsAllPatch (kept on the device in localStorage roarme-wsbg-all). A project shows its own ws_bg, else the one for all ('none' = no background here). Setting the one for all also writes it into every project I administer that has none or had the previous one, so their members see it; Make It a Project starts with it. Project Settings > More > Background has two parts, This Project (Own Picture, Use the One for All, None Here) and All Projects (Upload / Change, Use This Project's, Crop, Remove, Opacity). Alex's three projects were given the Σαμπάνης picture on 25 Nov. Groups the Production Hub makes get it only on Alex's own screen until the one for all is set again.
- The iPhone app is upright only (Alex, 25 Nov): ios.yml sets UISupportedInterfaceOrientations to Portrait (an iPad keeps its own list); public/manifest.webmanifest says portrait for installed web apps. RoarMe's camera still turns photos by how the phone is held.
- ROARMAIL's account rows always show the address under the name (Alex, 25 Nov: some did, some did not): .rooms.no-prev hid a row's second line unless it had a count; .rmx-accrow's .room-bottom / .room-prev are forced back (end of styles.css).
- The look list of 24 Nov, points 4 to 8 (Alex: "the app alike everywhere, settings too"; he asked for a sample of 1 to 3 only, https://claude.ai/artifact/Lpqqz2UBidN1s3cBD9DkjM, still waiting for his pick): Call devices are set-row rows with the choice in small words and an arrow, an unseen select over the row opens the system's list (SettingsPages.jsx CallDevices, .set-devpick); Media's head as every page's (back, the title in the middle, tabs and search under it, no line; .lib-top grid); What's New's All / Phone / Computer centred; by Night .danger rows read #ff6961 and a Settings .seg's chosen lens is white glass with white words; the brand at the foot of Settings clears the foot bar on a phone; Media's empty line centred. End of styles.css.
- ROARMAIL's account rows: the unread count stands in the middle of the row's height just left of the arrow, the same place on every account (Alex, 25 Nov: "one up, one down"); end of styles.css.
- Inside ROARMAIL the bar at the foot is Contacts · New Mail · Settings (Alex, 25 Nov; he chose: no Calls there, Contacts = everyone together). Chat.jsx inMail (mailOn, tab chats, folder mail, MailView mounted) swaps Contacts / Calls / Messages for Contacts and New Mail (FOOT_I.newmail); they send roarme-mail-cmd {what: 'contacts' | 'new'}, MailView answers and tells its step back with roarme-mail-step (Contacts lit on its page; the foot lens follows inMail / mailStep). mail.jsx MailContacts: everyone with an email in the address book, RoarMail's contacts and the senders of the mail kept on the phone (no-reply and notification addresses left out), a search, a tap opens Compose with init.to. The round pencil (rmx-new) is gone (newBtn null). Leaving the folder brings the usual bar back.
- ROARMAIL's bar over the mails is one line (Alex, 25 Nov): a round back (.rmx-hback) and the seven filters in the capsule, no folder name or address (the Crumb is gone from the list; a sender or day filter shows its words as a hint under it), as wide as the folder bar (17 to 373 at 390px); the Unread filter is gone (unread mail shows anyway).
- No list search over ROARMAIL (Alex, 25 Nov): a pull down in the mail folder does not open .search-wrap (Chat.jsx: open only when folder is not 'mail'; entering the folder closes and clears it). Other folders keep it.
- Every head inside ROARMAIL is one capsule (Alex, 25 Nov: "make the menus alike"): mail.jsx Crumb is .rmx-crumb.rmx-cap, back at the left, a small picture and the name on one line in the middle, an end slot at the right (Contacts' count); as wide and as tall as the folder bar (17 to 373, 44px at 390px) on an account's folders, an open mail and Contacts; the address under the name is gone.
- Settings > ROARMAIL made to work, and more (Alex, 25 Nov: "nothing works, None and 1 line are the same; add more; make the spam"). Preview Lines lost to a stronger row rule; now forced (.rmx.rmx-lN). mail.jsx PREF_START adds pics (Sender Pictures, .rmx-nopic), quote (the mail under a reply), defAcc (Send New Mail From, an unseen select over the row), countUnread (mail in the app's Unread and its count), every (Check Every 1 / 3 / 10 min: useMailUnread's interval and warmMail's throttle), autoJunk, keep (Keep Up To 50 / 150 / 500, keepMax), allHide (an account's All switch: out of All Inboxes, its total and the app's Unread). Junk: loadSetup reads RoarMail's block list (contacts.blocked, block_rules name / domain) into setup.block; isBlocked(from, setup) tests address, domain and folded name as the computer does; with Blocked Senders to Junk on, MailList moves such mail out of an Inbox to the account's Junk by itself and warmMail leaves it out; Settings lists Blocked Senders with Unblock; the long press has Move to Junk (Not Junk in a Junk folder: back to the Inbox and the address unblocked) and Block @domain (never a mailbox provider, PERSONAL as RoarMail's shield.ts). roarmail-cloud v6 (roarmail repository, deployed 25 Nov) adds blockDomain and unblock, editing roarmail_sync so the computers take it in.
- ROARMAIL never pans sideways on an iPhone (Alex, 25 Nov, a screenshot of the list shifted left): the .rooms-pager, .rooms-track and .rooms that hold it take overflow-x hidden, overscroll-behavior-x none and touch-action pan-y while they hold .rmx (styles.css end), .rmx-sw rows clip their buttons, and MailView puts any sideways scroll of those boxes back to 0 on every scroll event (WebKit lets a finger pan an overflow-auto box by a few pixels).
- Block Sender in ROARMAIL takes every mail of that sender out of the list at once (Alex, 25 Nov: "why does Block Sender not work": only the mail pressed went to Junk, the others stayed until RoarMail's list was read again): mail.jsx noteBlock(kind, value, on) writes the block or unblock into the kept setup and sends roarme-mail-setup, which MailView takes, so isBlocked and the Junk sweep see it now; Block also moves the sender's other mails in the list to Junk. Known gap: RoarMail on the computer merges blocked contacts with max() and block_rules insert-only (mail.ts importSetup / syncImport), so an Unblock made in RoarMe is put back when the computer next syncs; fixing that needs a RoarMail desktop release that honours unblocks (asked Alex).
- Writing a mail in ROARMAIL as Apple's Mail (Alex, 25 Nov, its screenshot: "clean, nice, a full page to write what you want"): mail.jsx Compose is .rmx-c2 inside the shared Modal, whose own head steps aside (.modal:has(> .modal-body > .rmx-c2)); X at the left and a round Send at the right (the accent's pair once there is someone to send to, a spinner while sending), the title large (New Message / Reply / Forward), hairline rows To: (with a round + that lists the people with an address, the address book first, then RoarMail's contacts, filtered by what is typed) · Cc/Bcc, From: (one line until tapped, then Cc, Bcc and From with an unseen select) · Subject: (with the clip), then the textarea fills the page. On a phone the window fills the screen under the clock, solid white / #1c1c1e; with the keyboard up in the iPhone app it ends at the keyboard's top (measured with a stand-in keyboard). End of styles.css.
- 25 Nov, three from the iPhone (Alex): (1) a slide right goes back in ROARMAIL again: mail.jsx useSlide's finish() runs on touchend and on touchcancel (the iPhone cancels a touch it hands to its own scrolling; a slide already past 70px or quick still counts), and the open mail's frame has touch-action pan-y with no sideways overflow of its own. (2) The folder bar scrolls sideways: ui.jsx useLiquid set touch-action pan-y on the bar when all its choices fitted, measured once before every folder was there, and never asked again; now a MutationObserver, the children in the ResizeObserver, every touchstart and pointerdown ask again. (3) ALL stays blue while anything is unread whichever folder is chosen: on All the lens is blue (23 Nov), elsewhere ALL is its own blue pill in the accent's pair (.wheel-box.u-has:not(.u-all) > .tabs > button[data-tab='all'], end of styles.css). This replaces 23 Nov's "only on ALL blue".
- Picking several mails in ROARMAIL (Alex, 25 Nov: "select multiple and delete or move in bulk, as on the desktop"): the long-press menu starts with Select (MailMenu, not in the app's Unread); then every tap picks or lets go (MailList sel, a round tick before each row, .rmx-check / .rmx-checked; no slide or long press meanwhile), the head reads N Selected with X and All / None (.rmx-selhead), and a glass bar over the foot (.rmx-selbar, portalled) does Read / Unread, Star, Move (one account at a time, MovePick for all), Junk and Delete for every picked mail, four at a time (bulk, moveTo with _bulk). The bar at the foot inside ROARMAIL is now Contacts · Mail · New Mail · Settings: Mail (FOOT_I.mail, roarme-mail-cmd home) goes back to the accounts and is lit there. With Preview Lines on None the unread dot rides beside the time (.rmx-udot) and the empty row is gone. End of styles.css.
- Edit Mailboxes on ROARMAIL's home (Alex, 25 Nov, Apple Mail's Mailboxes > Edit: "on the home page this must be possible, a round button with dots"): the home has the head capsule too (Mailboxes, a round ⋯ at the right that turns into the accent's ✓ while editing). mail.jsx HomeEdit lists All Inboxes, every account and SMART (All Sent, All Drafts, All Trash, All Junk, All Archive: that folder of every account; Flagged, Unread, Attachments, Today: All Inboxes opened with that filter, MailList step.preset) with a round tick (shown on the home or not) and a grip that drags the row up or down; kept in mail prefs home { order, hide } (the smart ones hidden until ticked). A smart row on the home is a tinted round sign, its name and a small line. End of styles.css.
- ROARMAIL as a place of its own (Alex, 25 Nov: "don't add extra lines, put things in the ones there are; the folder bar goes, or is replaced; tapping ROARMAIL slides as if into another environment", then "take Contacts out of the bar, we put it elsewhere; put RoarMe in the bar and it goes back"): while the folder is open the folder bar is not drawn (Chat.jsx wheel-box waits for !(mailOn && folder === 'mail')), so the mail's own capsule (Mailboxes on the home, back and the filters in a list) stands in its place; .side.mail-env, and .side.mail-in / mail-out for 420 ms slide the page in from the right and the chats back from the left (mail-env-in / -out, off with Power Saving and reduced motion). The bar at the foot inside it is RoarMe · Mail · New Mail · Settings: RoarMe (FOOT_I.messages) sets the folder left before (mailBack) and slides back; Contacts is out of the bar for now (MailContacts and roarme-mail-cmd 'contacts' stay in the code). End of styles.css.
- Inside ROARMAIL the head's own buttons are the mail's (Alex, 25 Nov, from Apple Mail): the ⋯ at the left is Edit Mailboxes (roarme-mail-cmd 'edit'; from any step it goes home and edits; it shows Done / ✓ while editing, told back by roarme-mail-edit), the + at the right is New Mail with Apple's square-and-pen sign (MAIL_NEW_I). The bar at the foot is RoarMe · Mail · Settings (New Mail left it). The home has no capsule of its own any more (no extra line), and Edit Mailboxes has no hint line under it.
- Inside ROARMAIL the head's logo reads ROARMAIL in the ROARME wordmark's letters (Alex, 25 Nov): ui.jsx Wordmark takes b (the bold half, 'ME' by default); Chat.jsx passes 'MAIL' while the mail folder is open.
- A reload keeps one's place (Alex, 25 Nov: "swipe to refresh on any page stays on that page"): src/stay.js keeps tab, folder, the open chat, the Settings page and ROARMAIL's step (folders or list; an open mail comes back as its list) in sessionStorage roarme-stay and gives them back only when the page was reloaded (navigation type reload: our pull to refresh, Safari's own, a reload for a new version); a fresh start still opens on the chats. Replaces the Contacts-only roarme-tab of 15 Nov.
- ROARMAIL is a button of the bar at the foot, Mail right of Messages with the unread mail count, not a folder in the folder bar (Alex, 25 Nov, screenshot: "take ROARMAIL off the top and put it at the foot right of Messages"): Chat.jsx .foot-mail sets tab chats and folder 'mail' (still the internal folder MailView hangs on); allFolders no longer holds 'mail', so it is gone from the folder bar, Settings > Chat Folders and the folder swipe (which does nothing while in mail); Messages takes one out of mail back to the folder left. Inside the mail the foot stays RoarMe · Mail · Settings.
- The bar at the foot never changes (Alex, 25 Nov: "the same bar everywhere, in the mail too"): Contacts · Calls · Messages · Mail · Settings, Mail only while ROARMAIL is turned on in Settings; inside the mail Mail is lit and a tap on it goes back to the accounts (roarme-mail-cmd home). The mail's own foot (RoarMe · Mail · Settings, foot-roarme / foot-mailhome) is gone.
- RoarTransfer's way round (Alex, 25 Nov, a 277 KB photo from the iPhone's Safari stuck at 100%; the logs since 24 Nov show pieces failing as net or hanging, never reaching complete, while the computer uploads fine): r2upload.js sends a piece straight to R2 as before, but once a piece fails (net, stall, an HTTP error, or a 2xx whose ETag cannot be read) the rest of that file goes through the Edge Function roartransfer-put (supabase/functions/roartransfer-put, deployed 25 Nov, verify_jwt off: it checks the account itself; POST ?key=&uploadId=&part= with the bytes, only one's own free|week|pro/<id>/ or a guest's free/guest/<uuid>/ key, at most 16 MB a piece; answers { etag }), which passes it on to R2. After the last byte is out a piece waits 15 s for R2's answer, 90 s for the function's. complete and abort notes carry &via=1 when it was used. The R2 key cannot read the bucket's CORS rules (AccessDenied), so whether roarme.app is missing there is unchecked; Alex could look in Cloudflare > R2 > roartransfer > Settings > CORS policy. The test function r2-cors-check was left as a 410 stub (no tool deletes a function).
- ROARMAIL has no head capsule of its own any more (Alex, 25 Nov, screenshot of All Inboxes' bar: "this bar goes; the arrow goes left and the dots right of it"): mail.jsx Crumb renders nothing and only registers its back (useMailBack: a stack, the newest answers; roarme-mail-canback tells the head); MailList registers its back too (a conversation first) and its filter capsule lost its own arrow. Chat.jsx's head inside the mail: .mail-hl = a round back (mailBackNow, shown while a step can go back) then the ⋯; ROARMAIL in the middle, New Mail at the right. The account's name, the mail's subject and Contacts' count over the page went with the capsule.
- Inside ROARMAIL the head's right button is the app's own + again (Alex, 25 Nov: "let the + stay as it was; it makes a new message"): the same round I.plus, sending roarme-mail-cmd 'new' (Compose); Apple's square-and-pen sign (MAIL_NEW_I) is gone.
- The Mail sign in the bar at the foot is one fine outline (an envelope and its V), drawn like Contacts and Messages (Alex, 25 Nov: "the mail icon closer to the others"); ui.jsx FOOT_I.mail. The old one, two filled shapes, came out thick once the bar strokes its signs.

- Unread is chats only again (Alex, 25 Nov: "the mails should not go in this unread, only the messages"): no mails among the unread chats, no All · Chats · Mail in the head (ROARME there), the round Unread button counts chats only (uCount); the unread mail count stays on Mail in the bar at the foot (mailUn). Chat.jsx lost uKind, uMail, UnreadMail and mailWho; mail.jsx still exports useUnreadMails / UnreadMail, unused. The head's ⋯ already picks several chats in any folder, Unread too; its bar at the foot reads Delete · Read · Move · Archive (Folder renamed Move) and goes when ✓ closes it.
- 25 Nov, RoarMail round (Alex): (1) the swipe across the bar at the foot follows the bar, Contacts · Calls · Messages · Mail · Settings (Chat.jsx goStop: stop 'mail' while mailOn, reached as tab chats + folder mail; from mail a slide right lands on Messages' last folder). (2) One account in RoarMail opens straight on its folders, with no back to a home of one (mail.jsx single). (3) The head's ⋯ does the page's own thing (mail.jsx useMailDots / Dots, a stack, the newest answers; roarme-mail-cmd 'edit' calls mailDotsNow): the home edits the mailboxes, an account's folders page moves its folders up or down by their grips (HomeEdit with no ticks; prefs forder { [account]: keys }, Inbox and the rest kept above Folders), a folder's mails turn Select on and off, an open mail shows its menu (no Select there); ✓ shows while any of these is on (tellEdit). (4) A mail's long-press menu stands in the middle of the screen (.menu.rmx-lp inset 0, margin auto, 300px).
- ROARMAIL's search is a round button in the head, left of the + (Alex, 25 Nov: "beside the plus, on every page of the mail"): Chat.jsx .head-mailsearch sends roarme-mail-cmd 'search'; on a list MailList opens or closes its own search field (roarme-mail-search; an open mail closes first), elsewhere MailView opens the Inbox of the account in sight (All Inboxes from the home) with the search open (preset 'search'). Search and Conversations left the filter bar (FILTERS: Starred, With attachments, Day, Oldest first, Same sender); Conversations is Settings > ROARMAIL > Reading > Conversations (prefs conv, off to start).
- 25 Nov, more from Alex: (1) a mail wider than the screen (fixed-width tables) is drawn smaller to fit whole, as Apple's Mail does (mail.jsx MailRead fit: body zoom = frame width / scroll width, at least .3), not cut at the sides. (2) The filter bar over a folder's mails is sticky at the top of the list (.rmx-ml > .rmx-head, top 0; --under is not set inside the mail). (3) Settings > ROARMAIL > Reading > Show: Both, Sender or Subject (prefs show; .rmx-show-from / .rmx-show-subject on the list). (4) Pull down to refresh everywhere (Chat.jsx gPull: a document touch handler on a phone, for any scroller at its top outside the chat list, Contacts, a chat, a window or a sheet, a field; the round arrow .ptr.ptr-fixed under the head; it reloads and stay.js brings the page back); in ROARMAIL the list's own pull reloads at once instead of opening the list search.
- RoarMail on a computer (Alex, 25 Nov, asked the day before: "under PROjects as on the phone; on the desktop a page that tells people to download the real RoarMail"): Settings shows the gold ROARMAIL door to inThemeLab on every device ("Get the RoarMail app" under it off a phone); off a phone its page is mail.jsx MailPromo: the gold icon, "Every mailbox. One private inbox.", four points, Download for Mac (Apple Silicon, Intel) and Windows from the roarmail repository's latest release (the computer in use first, in the accent), See RoarMail (themadlions.com/lab/roarmail/). Inside the Mac App Store build only See RoarMail. CSS .rmp-* at the end of styles.css.
- An HTML mail keeps 16px of room at its sides and 12px above (Alex, 25 Nov, a screenshot: the words touched the screen's left edge); plain text keeps 14px 16px 18px. mail.jsx MailRead's frame CSS; the zoom-to-fit counts the padding.
- Sliding a row sideways made solid (Alex, 25 Nov, a screenshot of a mail whose buttons lay over its words: "not solid, everywhere, chat too"): a mail row slides a plain box round it (mail.jsx .rmx-slide carries --sx), never the row's button, whose press effects hold a transform; the row is solid (--bg) while out, and the buttons keep 60px each, right-aligned, so they come into sight one by one from the edge (Trash first) and their words are never cut. A slid chat drops its .pressing / .held at once (Chat.jsx pager) and keeps scale 1; its buttons keep 62px each (.row-acts .ra-in width auto), from the screen's edge. End of styles.css.
- Add Account in ROARMAIL (Alex, 25 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/RJeD4N28wVHkkhxZPJmLt3, "Το 1 ναι"; the motion sample https://claude.ai/artifact/UQBcJuL8AbMehm7ER6xNW8 he turned down): mail.jsx AddAccount, opened by the Add Account row at the foot of Mailboxes (AddAccountRow; on a single account's folders page too) and from the empty ROARMAIL ("No mail accounts yet"). Import from RoarMail first (only when RoarMail on a computer has accounts): pick them, RoarMe writes a row in roarmail_cloud_asks (roarmail repository supabase/roarmail-cloud.sql, applied 25 Nov) and waits; RoarMail 0.40.0 on the computer checks every 15 s, asks Allow / Don't Allow and sends each account as Send to RoarMe does (state done / declined). Then Gmail, iCloud Mail, Outlook, Yahoo Mail, AOL Mail, Other Mail with their logos (LOGO, PROV): the app password's steps and a button to the service's own page, Email and Password, Connect -> roarmail-cloud 'add' (v7: servers from PRESETS or Thunderbird's autoconfig list, checked over IMAP, the folders listed, kept in Vault with cfg.origin 'roarme', name, colour and folders); Outlook is Sign in with Microsoft by a device code ('msStart' shows the code and Microsoft's page, 'msPoll' every few seconds). loadSetup merges these accounts (state's `own`) with RoarMail's; Settings > ROARMAIL > Accounts has a bin for them (disconnect). Sending from iCloud and Outlook may not work from the cloud (their SMTP is on 587). CSS .rma-* at the end of styles.css. Seen in the app run in Chromium with the cloud stubbed; not yet with a real account.
- Swipes made to catch on the iPhone (Alex, 25 Nov, the third time: "swipe left right for navigation does not work on all pages", and "swipe down to refresh?"), checked with real touch events (CDP) on every page in Chromium at 390px: the page-wide pull (Chat.jsx gPull) decides only after the finger has gone 10 px (its first move, a pixel sideways, cancelled it) and now runs inside ROARMAIL past its home too (folders, a list, an open mail); the slide back on a page in .main no longer stops on buttons, segments or colour dots (NO holds only fields, maps, canvases, windows, menus and tabs past the first; a row that really scrolls sideways is found by sideScroll), always goes back from the left 30 px, and a slide the iPhone cancels after it is clearly on its way still goes back; both listen in the capture phase. The bar-at-the-foot swipe counts a cancelled touch too (onTouchCancel). From Mail a slide right lands on the folder left for it (mailBack), not on Archived; one account's folders page slides to Messages and Settings (window event roarme-stop); AllMedia's back on a phone lands on Settings itself. A new swipe or pull: decide after ~10 px, listen in the capture phase, handle touchcancel.
- The filter bar over the mails (Alex, 25 Nov, a screenshot: "centre them a little, not so much at the edges, and put the group conversation after all"): six signs spread evenly 8px in from the capsule's ends with no fade (Starred, With attachments, Conversations, Mail of one day, Oldest first, Same sender); Conversations is back in the bar as a switch of the same setting (prefs conv) and left Settings > ROARMAIL > Reading, so it stands in one place only.
- The head's round signs (back, ⋯, search, +) are drawn as the bar at the foot's: 24px with a 1.6 line, the filled ⋯ 22px (Alex, 25 Nov, two screenshots: "much chunkier and bigger than the menu; make them as the menu"); last block of styles.css on .side-head .round > svg, every page's head.
- ROARMAIL open to everyone on a phone (Alex, 25 Nov: "open roarmail inside roarme for everyone"): Chat.jsx mailOn = mailHere() && the switch (no inThemeLab); Settings shows the gold ROARMAIL door to everyone ("Your mail inside RoarMe" on a phone, "Get the RoarMail app" on a computer); the switch stays off until each person turns it on. Someone without RoarMail on a computer keeps their blocks in roarmail_cloud_blocks (roarmail repository supabase/roarmail-cloud.sql, applied 25 Nov; roarmail-cloud v8 writes it when there is no roarmail_sync row, mail.jsx loadSetup reads it). What's New tells everyone (On a phone); the lab RoarMail section stays lab. Privacy Policy dated 25 November 2026 (both languages): an account added in RoarMe, the check against its server, blocks kept in RoarMe, removing it in Settings > ROARMAIL.
- The chat's calendar (Alex, 25 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/PQ57XutzNJ5LWw6HLJ7bDc, "βάλ' το", the dot for days of words, both Clear choices): a tap on a day's chip in a chat (Chat.jsx .day > span.day-chip) opens src/chatCal.jsx (lazy) in the shared Modal, solid white / #1c1c1e: Calendar with Select, every month from the chat's first day (at least the last three) to now, the newest at the foot; a day with a photo or video shows its newest one's thumb (picLink of thumbOf), a day with only words keeps its plain number with a dot in the accent under it (Alex: "only the dot"), a day with nothing is faint; a tap goes to the day's first message (goTo). Select picks days; Clear History For This Day asks Clear for Me (roar_msg_hides) or Clear for Me and <name> (also deletes my own messages of those days for everyone). supabase/chat-calendar.sql (applied 25 Nov): roar_chat_days(r, tz) and roar_clear_days(r, days, everyone, tz), both security invoker so every message rule holds. CSS .ccal-* at the end of styles.css.
- ROARMAIL fast and whole (Alex, 26 Nov: "everything to do with deleting is very slow, and Empty does not even empty; delete at once, in the background; tidy it so it flies; a button to bring more or all mail; all of the desktop's settings"). Delete, Move, Junk, Archive, Block (the sender's other mails too) and Select's bulk actions take the mail off the list at once and keep it gone on the device (mail.jsx GONE, 15 minutes), then one roarmail-cloud 'bulk' call per folder does it on the server (op delete / move / flag, 400 at a time on one connection; serverBulk, away); a failure says so and reloads. Empty Trash / Junk clears the list at once and calls 'empty' again while it answers left > 0 (the function deletes 400 at a time for at most 35 s a call, since one big delete ran into its time limit and stopped half way; EMPTIED keeps the folder empty on the device and resumes after a restart). roarmail-cloud v10 (roarmail repository). At a folder's foot All Mail brings page after page, up to 1,500 (loadAll), and a search has More Results (found.total). Settings > ROARMAIL now carries RoarMail's desktop settings, starting from the computer's own (setup.settings, fromDesktop) until changed here: Mark as Read (On Open / After 3 s / Never), Conversation order, Account Colours, Unread Counts, Detect Links, Plain Text in Fixed Width, Quotes in Colours, Privacy Mode (forces images off, trackers blocked, clean links), Block Trackers (1-2 px pictures and known tracker hosts taken out, cleanHtml), Clean Links (utm_ and the like, cleanLink on a tap in the frame), Undo Send (Off / 5 / 10 / 20 s: the window goes, UndoBar counts down, Undo reopens it as it was, a failure reopens it too) and Full Slide (Trash / Archive / Read / Off: a slide past 62% of the screen does it). The old Unread-mails pieces (useUnreadMails, UnreadMail, useMailWho) are gone. setMailPref writes only the key changed.
- A tap on Mail in the bar at the foot always opens the mail's home, from anywhere, the mail itself included (Alex, 26 Nov): Chat.jsx calls mail.jsx mailHomeNext(), which sends roarme-mail-cmd home to a MailView in sight and makes the next MailView start at { s: 'accs' } instead of the kept step; a pull to refresh still comes back to the same step. With one account the home is its folders.
- Going to a message is seen at once (Alex, 26 Nov: "from the calendar I do not understand which messages; a reply's quote should take you to it with emphasis for 1-2 s, message, photo or anything"): Chat.jsx goTo keeps the flash 2.4 s; styles.css (end) lights the whole row in the accent (mf-row) and pops the bubble once with a glowing ring (mf-pop, mf-ring), for a reply's quote, search, pins, Show in Chat. From the chat's calendar goTo(id, { day: true }) brings the day's chip to the middle lit in the accent (day-hl, mf-chip) and every message of that day glows softly (day-glow) for 3 s. Colour only with reduced motion or Power Saving.
- ROARMAIL's mail body and freshness (Alex, 26 Nov: "swipe right does not catch on the mail's body; mail comes late and in odd places; deleted in Apple Mail, still here"). An open mail is no longer an iframe (the iPhone kept the frame's touches, so the page's slide never got them): mail.jsx sealMail draws it in a shadow root on the page (scripts, frames, forms, handlers, base, link and meta taken out; href only on links to the web, mail and phone; pictures and CSS url() from the web only after Show Images; position fixed made absolute; the mail's body / html rules aimed at .mb; the host contains layout and paint), so the slide works anywhere on it; a too-wide mail is zoomed to fit; Clean Links rewrites a link as it is tapped. The list asks the server again every 30 s while open and whenever RoarMe comes back to the front (load(0, quiet)); the newest page replaces what was in its time span, so mail deleted or moved on another device goes, older pages stay. roarmail-cloud v11 leaves out mail flagged \Deleted (deleted by Apple Mail but not yet expunged) and orders by arrival (internalDate), which is also the date shown.
- A mail's slide-out buttons (Read, Star, Move, Block, Trash) work again (Alex, 26 Nov, a screenshot: "none of these work"): the row's touchend took a tap on a button as a tap on the open row and slid it shut first, so the click landed on the row; mail.jsx MailRow now leaves a touch that starts or ends on .rmx-swacts to the button. Checked with real touch events (CDP): Star, Read and Move each made their call.
- In ROARMAIL a slide right on a short page goes back a step, never to Messages (Alex, 26 Nov, an account's folders: "it catches with difficulty and then takes me to Messages"): the slide started in the empty space under the page, outside .rmx, so the chat list's folder swipe took it. Chat.jsx's pager now leaves every touch alone while a ROARMAIL page past its home is shown, styles.css (end) lets .rmx-fl / .rmx-ml / .rmx-rd reach the foot of the screen, and mail.jsx useSlide goes at 50 px (or a quick 24 px), not 70. Checked with real touch events on the folders, on a row and in the empty space.
- A reload does not lock RoarMe again (Alex, 26 Nov: "when I refresh it shows Face ID, why, it is not needed"): lock.jsx useAutoLock keeps the time in sessionStorage roarme-unlocked-at while unlocked and in sight (every touch, on unlock, before unload), and at start stays open if that time is within the lock's own minutes (at least 1). A reload (pull to refresh, a new version) keeps sessionStorage; a fresh start of the app does not, so opening the app still asks. Locking clears it.
- ROARMAIL's home shows the unread its next pages show (Alex, 26 Nov, two screenshots: All Junk 4 and All Trash 16 inside, nothing on the home): the home asks the counts of every account's Inbox, Drafts, Sent, Junk, Trash and Archive (the fcounts effect runs on 'accs' too); an account's row is the sum of every folder of it with a count (accUnread), All Inboxes the sum of those kinds across the accounts not kept out of it; the Inbox's own count until the folders have answered. The Mail badge in the bar at the foot still counts Inboxes only.
- The back arrow in ROARMAIL's head after a pull to refresh (Alex, 26 Nov: "why no back arrow here"): a mail page that comes back with RoarMe registers its back in its own effect, which runs before Chat's listener is there, so the head missed it; Chat.jsx now asks mailCanBack() once its listener is on. Proved in Chromium: before the fix the arrow was gone after a reload on a folder's list, after it it stays.
- ROARMAIL's way back no longer hangs on one signal (Alex, 26 Nov, angry: the back arrow gone and a slide right on All Inboxes took him to Messages). The head's back shows whenever the mail step is not the home (mailStep, from roarme-mail-step; one account's folders count as the home), and when no page has registered a back it sends roarme-mail-cmd 'up' (a list goes to its account's folders, folders to the home). Chat.jsx's pager ignores every touch while folder is mail and the step is not the home (pagerNow.mailStep), whatever the DOM. Checked at 430 px with touch events: arrow list > folders > home, slide list > folders > home > Messages, and the arrow still shows and works with the back stack forced empty.
- The crushed mail list of build 256 (Alex, 26 Nov: "my God, you destroyed everything"): the min-height given to .rmx-fl / .rmx-ml / .rmx-rd took away a flex item's own floor (min-height auto), so in the .rooms column a long list was squeezed to the screen's height and every row crushed to 9-12 px. styles.css: those pages and every child of .rooms .rmx have flex-shrink 0. Measured in Chromium with 48 mails: 9-12 px before, 60-79 px after. Rule: never give a flex item a min-height without flex-shrink 0, and test lists with many rows, not four.
- One side gap on a phone (Alex, 26 Nov: "the same gap left and right as the menu at the foot, on every page"): under 760px the side column's padding is 20px, the bar at the foot has no margins of its own (so it stays 20px in), the head's round buttons, the search, the folder bar, the mail's boxes and filter bar start and end at 20px, and list rows (chats, contacts, mail accounts, mails) reach out so their picture starts at 20px. Measured at 430px on chats, mail home, folders, a list, Contacts, Calls and Settings: every outer edge 20px. Last block of styles.css. The open mail keeps its edge-to-edge body; pages in .main (an open chat, Settings' inner pages) were not changed.
- Which account a mail page is in (Alex, 26 Nov: "in each account, minimal, which mail it is, somewhere at the top"): mail.jsx MailCap, one small quiet centred line at the top of an account's folders (the address) and of a list (the folder in the words' colour · the address); for All Inboxes "All Inboxes · every account" or "All Trash · every account". CSS .rmx-cap at the end of styles.css.
- RoarMail Live (Alex, 26 Nov: "deleting is slow, as Spark does it"; he took Cloudflare's Workers Paid and made the API token himself, kept as CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN in the roarmail repository's secrets): an always-on mail engine on Cloudflare, roarmail repository live/ (a Worker that checks the RoarMe sign-in and hands each person to their own Container, MailBox, a Node server with imapflow that keeps every account's IMAP connection open for 15 minutes after its last use and runs each account's calls one at a time; the container sleeps after 20 minutes). It is at https://roarmail-live.alexisdigipro.workers.dev, deployed by the roarmail workflow live.yml (Run workflow; live_key only when the shared key changes). The passwords stay in Supabase Vault: the container asks the Edge Function roarmail-creds (verify_jwt off; two locks: the shared key in Vault roarmail_live_key, roarmail repository supabase/roarmail-live.sql, and the person's own sign-in, which must own the account). mail.jsx cloud() sends counts, list, get, flag, move, delete, bulk and empty to Live first (live()); a mail error of its own (502 / 404 / 400 with an error) is the answer, anything else falls back to roarmail-cloud and leaves Live alone for two minutes. Send, add, block and the rest stay on roarmail-cloud. The Worker and the creds function were checked from outside (health 200, no sign-in 401, no key 401); the container's way to a real account is first tried on Alex's phone. Mail rows (.rmx-sw) reach out only at the left: ROARMAIL's list clips sideways, and a row 5px past the clip at the right lost the finger there.
- An empty writing box is always one line (Alex, 26 Nov, a project chat on a computer: the box stood two lines tall with nothing in it): measured while the chat column was narrow (beside the Workspace, during a slide in), the placeholder wrapped and the box kept that height. Chat.jsx's sizing effect clears the height whenever the text is empty, and the placeholder never wraps on any screen (styles.css end).
- Touch ID in the Mac app (Alex, 26 Nov: "the fingerprint on the desktop, as Face ID on the phone"): a page inside Electron cannot make a passkey that asks for the finger, so desktop/main.js answers roar:touchid-can / roar:touchid with systemPreferences.canPromptTouchID / promptTouchID, preload.js gives it as roarDesktop.touchId { can, ask } (Mac only), and lock.jsx uses it first (bioAvailable, bioOn keeps bio 'mac', bioCheck): Settings > Privacy and Security > Passcode lock > Unlock with Touch ID, asked at once on the lock. The Mac App Store build carries it at once; the .dmg app with its next release (main.js / preload.js). The web on a computer keeps the passkey way. Windows not done.
- The keyboard's arrows on a computer (Alex, 26 Nov: "left and right through the folders, up and down through the chats"): Chat.jsx (after `shown`, arrowsNow) on a fine pointer over 760px, on the chats tab outside ROARMAIL: ← → set the previous / next folder of the bar (Unread and mail left out) and bring its tab into sight (showTab); ↑ ↓ open the chat above / below in the list in sight (shown, pins first) and scroll its row into view. Plain arrows only while no field has the cursor and no menu, window, lock or editor is open; Option / Alt + arrow works from anywhere, the writing box too (its own ↑ edits the last message only without Alt). Esc with nothing to cancel lets the writing box go, and a chat opened by the arrows does not take the cursor into its writing box (arrowOpenAt), so the next arrow moves on. Listed in Settings > Keyboard Shortcuts. Checked with real key presses in Chromium at 1280px.
- ROARMAIL lighter at every level (Alex, 26 Nov: "still slow and heavy"; the logs showed about 50 roarmail-creds calls at once, 4 to 5 s each, when Mail opened). RoarMail Live's container (roarmail repository live/container/server.mjs): one ask for an account's credentials shared by every call that needs it at once (credAsk), kept 12 hours (a Microsoft token 45 minutes) until the server refuses it; two connections per account, 'fg' for what the person taps and 'bg' for counts and what is read ahead (the body's bg: true, counts always), so a tap never waits behind the background; a connection made once even when many calls need it together (opening); a mailbox already open is not opened again except to list it (openBox); connections stay 40 minutes with a NOOP every 9; the container sleeps after an hour (live/src/index.js). RoarMe (mail.jsx): fetchAhead and warmMail's lists send bg: true; MailRow is memo'd (rowSame compares what the row shows; handlers through rowH), so the 30 s refresh, a selection or a slide no longer redraws every row; rows out of sight are not drawn (.rmx .rmx-sw content-visibility, end of styles.css).
- More than one account on a device (Alex, 26 Nov, from Telegram's Settings: "multiple accounts that switch"): src/accounts.js keeps each account's whole Supabase session in localStorage roarme-accounts (at most MAX_ACCOUNTS 3, kept fresh on every SIGNED_IN / TOKEN_REFRESHED; App.jsx adds the name, @username and picture). switchAccount writes the other session into roarme-auth-2 and reloads (stay.js opens the chats, or the chat a notification asked for); addAccount keeps the present one, removes roarme-auth-2 without signing out (signing out would revoke its token) and reloads to the sign-in page, which then has Back to Your Account (cancelAdd); Settings > Sign Out is signOutHere: only this account, its notifications on this device go (push.js pushForget), and the next account takes its place. Settings shows AccountsBox under one's own card (SettingsPages.jsx: the one in use with a tick, the others a tap away, Add Account while there is room; .set-accs at the end of styles.css). Notifications from every account: supabase/push-multi-account.sql (applied 26 Nov) makes roar_push_subs' key (endpoint, user_id), so one phone or browser is kept for each account on it; roar-push v24 adds `to` (the account) to every notification, and a tap on one for another account switches to it and opens the chat (Chat.jsx openForAccount on the iPhone app's tap, the service worker's message and #c=<room>&to=<id>). Each account's own caches on the device were already kept apart by id. Not built: the quick switch by holding Settings in the bar at the foot (offered, not answered).
- Holding Settings in the bar at the foot (Alex, 26 Nov, "yes, the hold too"): Chat.jsx accHold (480 ms, or a right click) opens .menu.acc-menu above the button, portalled with its own veil: the accounts on the device (the one in use with a tick), Add Account while there is room; a tap switches (switchAccount). The tap that ends the hold neither opens Settings (accMenuAt) nor closes the menu (the veil waits 600 ms). CSS at the end of styles.css. Checked with real touch events in Chromium at 390px.
- A hub project makes a PROjects group only when its Create PROject is pressed in the hub (Alex, 26 Nov: "whenever I add a new project it must not make a group by itself; I will press the button we have"): roar-hub v14 wanted() = request && !delivered (before: also any hub project made after linking, which made 75 empty groups for the 2025 projects imported into the hub that night; they were deleted, and the hub's statuses and categories the sync had written back were put right). Groups made here with Make It a Project still create their hub project as before.
- A delivered project shows only in PROjects > Archived Projects (Alex, 26 Nov: "nowhere else, only in archived"): Chat.jsx inFolder returns false for it in every other folder (All, Unread, Groups, one's own, Archived) and unreadAll leaves it out; a search on the list still finds it; Back to Active brings it back everywhere.
- A message of only a big RoarMoji or sticker with a reaction (Alex, 26 Nov, the lion at 00:25: the heart squeezed the time's chip into "00:2 / 5"): under the picture, on one line at the message's own side, the reactions then the time's chip, which never wraps (last block of styles.css, .bubble.big-emoji).
- How Alex wants fixes and builds (26 Nov): a plain fix of something broken he points at is made at once, no asking ("don't ask me, pass them"); new looks and features still follow How we work. Merging to main (the web) is fine after each fix, and TestFlight and the Mac build go out straight after every round too, unless he says to wait (Alex, 26 Nov evening: "we said you pass them all straight away unless I tell you to wait"; his earlier "I'll tell you when I want them batched" was read the wrong way round).
- ROARMAIL's Junk, Trash and the rest open at once, and Live is not given up so soon (Alex, 26 Nov: "still slow, above all Junk, Deleted"; the logs showed roarmail-cloud still doing most calls, about 1.3 s each with a fresh login): mail.jsx live() asks RoarMail Live twice (700 ms apart) and only then sends that call the old way, leaving Live alone for 20 s (it was two minutes after one miss); warmMail also reads every account's Junk, Trash, Sent, Drafts and Archive (30 each, bg) and keeps them as `<account>|<path>` and `all|<role>`, so those lists show at once. RoarMail Live's Worker (roarmail live/src/index.js) asks a container that refuses up to three times before answering 503.
- The Calls sign in the bar at the foot has the line weight of Contacts and Messages beside it (Alex, 26 Nov: "a bit bolder, like the icons next to it"): src/icons/phone-bold.png, the same handset's mask widened by one pixel of 96 (a 3x3 max filter on its alpha), worn by ui.jsx FOOT_I.calls; I.phone elsewhere keeps the fine one.
- The round Unread button is a sonar (Alex, 26 Nov, C of https://claude.ai/artifact/PUMpBg54MtDc7bUx9h49n3): Chat.jsx puts two .u-son rings and the number (or a .u-core accent dot when all is read) inside .u-pop; the rings go out from the middle one after the other every 2.2 s (u-son, end of styles.css), off with Power Saving and reduced motion.
- New mail notifies at once, as Spark's (Alex, 26 Nov: "notifications the moment a mail arrives"): RoarMail Live's container (roarmail repository live/container/server.mjs, watch) keeps an IMAP IDLE on the Inbox of each account of whoever has a roarmail_watch row with enabled on (roarmail repository supabase/roarmail-watch.sql, applied 26 Nov; the app makes the row, on, the first time ROARMAIL is used: mail.jsx ensureWatch), and the Worker's five-minute cron (live/src/index.js scheduled, wrangler crons) wakes it with the list from roarmail-creds (v2: watchers, seen, watch). A new unseen mail not from a blocked sender (the newest three at a time) goes to roar-push (v25) {mail} with x-live-key: title the sender (as named in the address book), body the subject and a line, room 'mail:<account>|<path>|<uid>', quiet on Do Not Disturb. Chat.jsx openMailNote opens that very mail (openMailFromUnread) from an iPhone tap, the service worker's message or #c=mail:…; for another account kept on the device it switches to it. Settings > ROARMAIL > Notifications: New Mail and one switch per account (roarmail_watch.enabled / muted). Privacy Policy says so.
- Add All Team (Alex, 26 Nov: "when a group is made in a project not everyone goes in; keep adding and removing as now, and a button Add All Team"): supabase/hub-team.sql (applied 26 Nov) drops the trigger roar_project_add_hub_members, so a new project group takes nobody by itself, and adds roar_add_hub_team(r) (an admin of the group; my roar_hub_members not in it yet and not blocked either way, each with their category's channels). The button shows to an admin while some of the team is out: the group's Members tab (Profile.jsx, under Add people) and Project Settings > Who Sees Which Chat (pro.jsx MembersChats, beside Add People).
- B, I, U and S in the writing box show at once with no marks to see (Alex, 26 Nov: "not the asterisks, just bold where you write"): Chat.jsx markSel / selHas turn the chosen letters into Unicode's bold / italic letters or give them the combining underline / strike, as names do (fancy.jsx markName / nameHas); Cmd+B / I / U / Shift+X and full-screen writing the same; nothing chosen does nothing. Code (`) and spoiler (||) keep their marks. Older messages with ** still render as before.
- The Workspace in a window of its own (Alex, 26 Nov: "an extra window that snaps to the chat, but can stand apart if needed"; computer apps only): a round button beside ⇄ on the Workspace's top row (pro.jsx POPOUT_I, shown when window.roarDesktop.ws exists) asks desktop/main.js roar:ws-open; a second BrowserWindow loads RoarMe at #ws=<group>, src/wsPop.js marks it html.ws-pop (the side, the chat column and the grip hidden, the Workspace fills it; styles.css end) and Chat.jsx opens that group with the Workspace always shown, no sounds, no notifications, no What's New. It opens snapped to the main window's right edge (or laid over its right side when the screen has no room) and follows its moves and size; dragged more than 32 px away (decided on 'moved') it stands apart, brought back within 32 px it snaps again; it hides and comes back with the main window. Its Back into the chat window button (DOCK_I) or closing it puts the Workspace back in the main window, which meanwhile leaves it out of its pane (poppedWs from roar:ws; the head's Workspace button then brings the other window forward). One at a time. The .dmg / Windows apps get it with their next desktop release (main.js / preload.js), the Mac App Store build with its next build.
- The spoiler shimmers (Alex, 26 Nov, B of https://claude.ai/artifact/XyQ4LKnUCM9aAj4ioj8WGk): .fmt-spoiler not yet shown is a soft block (--sp-base, lighter on one's own messages, by Night a white wash) whose own gradient carries a light across it every 2.4 s (sp-shim, background-position, so a spoiler over two lines shimmers on both); a tap opens it from left to right (sp-open, clip-path, .5 s). Off with Power Saving and reduced motion. Last block of styles.css.
- Each account keeps its own chat-list things on the device (Alex, 26 Nov: "why do the folders of my other account show here"): roarme-folders and the rest were plain localStorage keys shared by every account, so a switch carried them over and folderSync's first sync merged them into the other account's cloud row (roar_folder_sync of @lion and @roaraccount got @iamalexkon's THEMADLIONS / BLVRT / Various). src/acctLocal.js bindLocal(uid), called in App.jsx just before Chat draws. Second pass the same night (Alex: "nothing from one account goes to another, nothing"): when the device's owner (localStorage roarme-local-owner) is another account, every roarme-… localStorage key (folders, pins, drafts, prefs and appearance, the mail's setup and counts, the kept chat list and people, Workspace and Tasks state…) is moved aside under roarme-acct-<owner>:<key> and the new account's are moved back; only the device's own keys stay shared (DEVICE: roarme-auth-2, roarme-accounts, the owner mark, roarme-lock, roarme-news-seen). The kept chats and mail in IndexedDB are a database per account (cache.js kvOwner: roarme-data-<id>); the old shared roarme-data is deleted once. Encrypted Chat's keys were already per account (e2e.js device:<id>). A new localStorage key needs nothing: anything named roarme-… follows its account. The two polluted cloud rows were emptied on 26 Nov with a fresh updated_at, so the devices take the empty set down.
- The Mail badge in the bar at the foot counts only the mail accounts this RoarMe account has in RoarMe (Alex, 26 Nov: "4 mail and not even an account"): mail.jsx sumShown keeps only the counts of accounts in mail-on with status ok, so counts kept from another account or a removed one never show.
- The folders share the bar evenly (Alex, 26 Nov: "the folders at the start are not centred", a screenshot of ALL · GROUPS · ARCHIVED where ALL was wide and ARCHIVED narrow): every tab in .rooms-area .tabs, Archived too, is flex 1 1 0 with min-width max-content, so while they fit each takes an equal part with its word in the middle; many folders still scroll. Last block of styles.css. Measured at 430px: three tabs of 125px each.
- The Terms of Service and Privacy Policy links on the sign-in / sign-up card are #6cc8ff, bold, no underline (Alex, 26 Nov: "they are black"; they took --accent, dark on the dark glass). Last block of styles.css, .auth:not(.ob) .auth-check-t a / .auth-legal a.
- Reset All Settings at the foot of Settings > General (Alex, 26 Nov: "a reset somewhere in Settings that takes you to the first settings in everything"; the old one sat in the middle of General and he never found it, it is gone): SettingsPages.jsx ResetAll puts roarme-prefs back to DEFAULTS, keeping RESET_KEEP (lang, folderOrder, folderNames, hiddenFolders, folderExtra, folderTags, pro) and every stored key not in DEFAULTS (the one-time migration flags), clears roarme-walls, runs mail.jsx resetMailPrefs (ROARMAIL's settings, keeping home and forder) and syncPushSound. Asks first (window.confirm). Per account, on this device. Checked in the app run in Chromium.
- The sign-in page's background is chosen in Settings > Admin > Wallpapers > Sign-in Background (Alex, 26 Nov: "in admin, change the sign in / sign up background whenever I want"): admin.jsx AuthBgBox (a phone and a computer preview, Choose / Change Picture, Use the Original) uploads the photo whole (shrinkImage 2560 px, no crop: it is laid with cover, so phones and computers both take it) to roar-walls/auth/<time>.jpg and sets supabase/app-look.sql roar_app_look ('auth_bg', path; anyone reads, roar_is_admin() writes; applied 26 Nov). src/authBg.js useAuthBg: Auth.jsx shows the copy kept on the device at once (localStorage roarme-auth-bg, a device key in acctLocal.js DEVICE) and asks again each time the page opens; .auth.auth-own::before takes --auth-img under the same veil (end of styles.css). For everyone, no new build needed after this one.
- ROARMAIL only for the admin again (Alex, 26 Nov: "only me, the admin"; it was open to everyone on a phone from 25 Nov): Chat.jsx mailOn = mailHere() && the switch && inThemeLab(me); Settings shows the ROARMAIL door and page only to inThemeLab. What's New's mail lines are lab sections (RoarMail) again. The two other accounts that had opened it (@roaraccount, @vaggtsipras, no mail accounts) had their roarmail_watch rows set enabled false. To open it to everyone later: drop the inThemeLab checks in those three places.
- Tasks only for the admin (Alex, 26 Nov: "take out tasks too", right after ROARMAIL went admin-only): Chat.jsx tasksOn = prefs.tasksTab && inThemeLab(me) drives the foot button, the swipe stops, the phone head's Tasks and the footLens; the once-on for whoever had tasks waits for inThemeLab; Settings > General / Features shows the Tasks box and its name only to inThemeLab. A saved tasksTab stays for everyone else, unused. Remind Me on a message still works.
- A gold TASKS door in Settings, under PROJECTS and ROARMAIL, only for the admin (Alex, 26 Nov: "only I see it, and make a gold door for these"): SettingsPages.jsx TasksWord, page 'tasks' with the Tasks switch (it left Features). Remind Me in a message's menu shows only to inThemeLab(me) too (Chat.jsx Room).
- No TestFlight or Mac builds for now (Alex, 26 Nov, late: "from now on, whatever changes we make, don't send them to TestFlight, I'll see them on the web"): changes go to main (the web, roarme.app/app/) only; ios.yml testflight and mac-appstore.yml are run again only when he asks. This replaces the same evening's "every round straight to TestFlight".
- The first screen (Alex, 26 Nov, sample https://claude.ai/artifact/PAGZSPeozxrB9a1guyH2gU): Auth.jsx shows the background photo with ROARME (Wordmark, no mark, no tagline) over two small pills side by side, Sign in (blue) and Sign up (glass), 112 x 38 (.ax-intro). A tap slides the panel up from the foot (.ax-sheet, shown 'in' | 'up'; an invite opens it on 'up', a used email link on 'in'); the round chevron over it or a slide down (not while a field has the focus) puts it away. In the panel everything is centred and symmetric: title, the words in the fields (50px of room each side), the mobile number, the links in a column. The tagline "Born to connect. Built to roar." is gone. Sign up has Mobile number, optional: the country as +code (an unseen select over it, .ax-cc) and the number; it is kept as user_metadata.phone_pending { cc, num } and the first-time phone step (Onboarding) starts with it filled in, where the SMS code (Twilio) confirms it. The email is still confirmed as before (Alex asked whether the SMS could replace it; told why not: the account's email must be the person's own). In the iPhone app the sheet's foot steps up by --kbh. CSS at the end of styles.css (.auth.ax). Seen in the app run in Chromium at 390 and 1280 px.
- The first screen's ROARME is 40px (10% smaller) and stands 45% closer to the buttons (Alex, 26 Nov): .ax-wm line-height 1, 13px under it; the letters end about 20px over the pills (about 39 before).
- The first screen's two buttons are 129 x 44 with 15.5px words (15% bigger, Alex, 26 Nov), grown downward (.ax-intro 48px from the foot instead of 54) so ROARME and the gap over the buttons stay where they were.
- The sign-in panel (Alex, 26 Nov): no sign over ROARME, BY THEMADLIONS half as far under it with BY in capitals and as thin as THE (margin-top -7px, text-transform uppercase, weight 250), Create an account and Forgot password side by side (a single link stays one pill, Back to Your Account a row of its own), and the round chevron that puts the panel away stands under the card (.ax-close order 2). Only inside .auth.ax .ax-sheet; the lock and Settings keep their mark.
- Sign up, shorter (Alex, 26 Nov: "no chatter"): the title reads Create your RoarAccount (21px, higher), OPTIONAL in small capitals over the mobile number, the note under it on one line ("So friends can find you, confirmed by SMS."), the boxes "I agree to the Terms and the Privacy Policy. No abuse tolerated." (the no-tolerance words App Review asks for, made short) and "Send me RoarMe news by email.". The Terms and the Privacy Policy no longer leave the page: a tap opens them over it (Auth.jsx legal, .ax-legal with legal.jsx LegalDoc, an X back), so nobody is stranded in the browser inside the iPhone app.
- On the first screen, while adding an account, Back to Your Account is one line as wide as Sign in and Sign up together (268 x 44, 10px under them; Alex, 27 Nov).
